## Cannabis Regulations {#cannabis-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2018-144
:::

[CANNABIS ACT](/eng/acts/C-24.5)

[CONTROLLED DRUGS AND SUBSTANCES ACT](/eng/acts/C-38.8)

[FOOD AND DRUGS ACT](/eng/acts/F-27)

Registration 2018-06-27

Cannabis Regulations

P.C. 2018-948 2018-06-26
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Health, pursuant to subsection 30(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19057){#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19057-ID0EOFAA}
of the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19056){#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19056-ID0ELFAA},
subsection 55(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19062){#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19062-ID0EJFAA}
of the [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19053){#fn_81200-2-417E_hc_19053-ID0EGFAA}
and subsection 139(1) and section 161 of the [Cannabis
Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#fn_81200-2-396E_hc_19011){#fn_81200-2-396E_hc_19011-ID0EDFAA},
makes the annexed [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-847626 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions --- Act and Regulations

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in the Act and in these Regulations.

  [[cannabis beverage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis beverage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means edible cannabis
      that is intended to be consumed by drinking and has a
      concentration of 3% w/w or less of THC, taking into account the
      potential to convert THCA into THC. ([boisson de
      cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}) 

  [[cannabis concentrate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis concentrate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance
      that has a concentration of greater than 3% w/w of THC, taking
      into account the potential to convert THCA into THC. ([cannabis
      sous forme de concentré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cannabis extract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis extract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance produced by

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subjecting anything referred to in item 1 of
          Schedule 1 to the Act to extraction processing, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} synthesizing a substance that is identical
          to a phytocannabinoid produced by, or found in, a cannabis
          plant; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a substance or mixture of substances that
        contains or has on it a substance produced in a manner referred
        to in paragraph (a).

      It does not include a cannabis topical or edible
      cannabis. ([extrait de cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cannabis non-solid concentrates]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis non-solid
      concentrates]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2019-206, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cannabis oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2019-206, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cannabis solid concentrates]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis solid concentrates]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-206, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cannabis topical]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis topical]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance or
      mixture of substances that contains or has on it anything referred
      to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act and that is intended
      for use, directly or indirectly, exclusively on external body
      surfaces, including hair and nails. ([cannabis pour usage
      topique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[edible cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[edible cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance or
      mixture of substances that contains or has on it anything referred
      to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act and that is intended to
      be consumed in the same manner as food. It does not include dried
      cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis plants or cannabis plant
      seeds. ([cannabis comestible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fresh cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fresh cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means freshly harvested
      cannabis leaves, flowers or buds, but does not include plant
      material that can be used to propagate cannabis. ([cannabis
      frais]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-solids containing cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-solids containing cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      substances that are in non-solid form at a temperature of 22 ± 2°C
      and that have a concentration of 3% w/w or less of THC, taking
      into account the potential to convert THCA into THC. ([substances
      qui ne sont pas solides et qui contiennent du
      cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[solids containing cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[solids containing cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      substances that are in solid form at a temperature of 22 ± 2°C and
      that have a concentration of 3% w/w or less of THC, taking into
      account the potential to convert THCA into THC. ([solides qui
      contiennent du cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions --- Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Cannabis
      Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cannabis drug licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis drug licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence for
      a drug containing cannabis. ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cannabis of only
      one of the classes set out in Schedule 4 to the Act --- or a
      cannabis accessory that contains such cannabis --- after it has
      been packaged and labelled for sale to a consumer at the retail
      level. It does not include

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis that is intended for an animal;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis accessory that contains cannabis
        that is intended for an animal; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a drug containing cannabis. ([produit du
        cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CBD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CBD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      cannabidiol. ([CBD]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CBDA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CBDA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cannabidiolic
      acid. ([ACBD]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[client]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[client]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a holder of a
      licence for sale for medical purposes, an individual who is
      registered with that holder of the licence under subsection
      282(1). ([client]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[combination product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[combination product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a product,
      consisting of a device and a prescription drug, for which a drug
      identification number has been assigned under subsection
      C.01.014.2(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([produit
      mixte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who
      is cohabiting with an individual in a conjugal relationship,
      having done so for a period of at least one year. ([conjoint de
      fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2019-206, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[constituent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[constituent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual unit of
      food that is combined as an individual unit of food with one or
      more other individual units of food to form an
      ingredient. ([constituant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[contaminated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[contaminated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
      cannabis, a cannabis accessory or an ingredient, containing or
      having on it anything --- including a micro-organism but excluding
      anything referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act ---
      that may render the cannabis, cannabis accessory or ingredient
      injurious to human health or unsuitable for human
      use. ([contaminé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[controlled substance offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[controlled substance offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an offence under Part I of the [Controlled
        Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), except subsection
        4(1) of that Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a conspiracy or an attempt to commit, being an
        accessory after the fact in relation to, or any counselling in
        relation to, an offence referred to in paragraph
        (a). ([infraction relative à une substance
        désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cultivation waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cultivation waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cannabis leaves,
      shoots or branches that are obtained during the cultivation,
      propagation or harvesting of cannabis and that are intended for
      destruction. ([déchet de culture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section
      2 of the [Food and Drugs
      Act](/eng/acts/F-27). ([instrument]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 2
      of the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27), but excludes cannabis
      that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a natural health product to which the [Natural
        Health Products Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2003-196)
        apply; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufactured or sold --- within the meaning of
        section 2 of the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27) --- and is
        not represented, for use in 

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the diagnosis, treatment, mitigation or
          prevention of a disease, disorder or abnormal physical state,
          or its symptoms, in human beings, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} restoring, correcting or modifying organic
          functions in human beings.

      It includes cannabis that is an [[active pharmaceutical
      ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection
      C.01A.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). It also includes
      cannabis that is manufactured or sold for use in a [[clinical
      trial]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section C.05.001 of
      those Regulations that is not non-therapeutic research on
      cannabis. ([drogue]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[durable life]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[durable life]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period, commencing
      on the day on which a cannabis product is packaged for sale to a
      consumer at the retail level, during which the product, when it is
      stored under conditions appropriate to that product, will retain,
      without any appreciable deterioration, normal palatability and any
      other qualities claimed for it by the holder of a licence for
      processing that manufactured the product. ([durée de
      conservation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[durable life date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[durable life date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the date on which
      the durable life of a cannabis product ends. ([date limite de
      conservation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[export permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[export permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permit issued under
      subsection 62(1) of the Act that authorizes the exportation of
      cannabis for medical or scientific purposes. ([permis
      d'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 1
      of the [Safe Food for Canadians
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-108). ([poisson]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 2
      of the [Food and Drugs
      Act](/eng/acts/F-27). ([aliment]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[food additive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[food additive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any substance the use
      of which results, or may reasonably be expected to result, in it
      or its by-products becoming a part of, or affecting the
      characteristics of, a food or edible cannabis, but does not
      include

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} anything referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule
        1 to the Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} anything that is excluded from the definition
        [[food additive]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
        B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([additif
        alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[former Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
      Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the regulations made by
      Order in Council P.C. 2016-743 of August 5, 2016 and registered as
      SOR/2016-230. ([ancien Règlement sur l'accès au cannabis à des
      fins médicales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[former Industrial Hemp Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Industrial Hemp Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the regulations made by Order in Council P.C. 1998-352 of March
      12, 1998 and registered as SOR/98-156. ([ancien Règlement sur le
      chanvre industriel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[grow area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[grow area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a site set
      out in a licence, an area of the site where cannabis plants are
      cultivated, harvested or propagated. ([zone de
      culture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hospital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hospital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in Part 8, a
      facility 

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is licensed, approved or designated by a
        province under the laws of the province to provide care or
        treatment to individuals suffering from any form of disease or
        illness; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is owned or operated by the Government of
        Canada or the government of a province and that provides health
        services. ([hôpital]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a container that
      is in direct contact with cannabis or a cannabis accessory that is
      a cannabis product or, if a wrapper is in direct contact with the
      cannabis or the cannabis accessory, with the wrapper. ([contenant
      immédiat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[import permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[import permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permit issued under
      subsection 62(1) of the Act that authorizes the importation of
      cannabis for medical or scientific purposes. ([permis
      d'importation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ingestion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ingestion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes absorption in the
      mouth. ([ingestion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis extract or a cannabis
        topical, a substance, other than anything referred to in item 1
        or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act, that is used to produce the
        cannabis extract or cannabis topical, including any substance
        used in the manufacture of that substance, and that is present
        in the final form of the cannabis extract or cannabis topical;
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of edible cannabis,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a substance, other than anything referred to
          in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act,

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} that is used to produce the edible cannabis
            if the use of the substance results, or may reasonably be
            expected to result, in the substance or its by-products
            becoming a part of, or affecting the characteristics of, the
            edible cannabis, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} that is part of a mixture of substances
            referred to in item 2 of that Schedule that is used to
            produce the edible cannabis if the use of the mixture
            results, or may reasonably be expected to result, in the
            substance or its by-products becoming a part of, or
            affecting the characteristics of, the edible cannabis, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a mixture of substances, other than anything
          referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act,

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} that is used to produce the edible cannabis
            if the use of the mixture results, or may reasonably be
            expected to result, in the mixture or its by-products
            becoming a part of, or affecting the characteristics of, the
            edible cannabis, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} that is part of a mixture of substances
            referred to in item 2 of that Schedule that is used to
            produce the edible cannabis if the use of the latter mixture
            results, or may reasonably be expected to result, in the
            former mixture or its by-products becoming a part of, or
            affecting the characteristics of, the edible
            cannabis. ([ingrédient]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence issued under
      subsection 62(1) of the Act in relation to
      cannabis. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Lists of Permitted Food Additives]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Lists of Permitted Food Additives]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([Listes des
      additifs alimentaires autorisés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marketing authorization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marketing authorization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2024-244, s. 166\]]{.Repealed}

  [[meat product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[meat product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([produit de
      viande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mineral nutrient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mineral nutrient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection D.02.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) except that it does
      not include sodium, potassium or chloride or compounds that
      include those elements. ([minéral nutritif]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[non-therapeutic research on cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-therapeutic research on cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      research that involves the distribution of cannabis to human
      participants by the holder of a licence for research and that is
      not a [[clinical trial]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
      section C.05.001 of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for which the sale
      of cannabis is authorized in accordance with section C.05.006 of
      those Regulations. This definition does not include research

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} related to the diagnosis, treatment, mitigation
        or prevention of a disease, disorder or abnormal physical state,
        or its symptoms, or the restoration or correction of organic
        functions, in human beings or animals;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} involving the distribution of cannabis that is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} represented for use in the diagnosis,
          treatment, mitigation or prevention of a disease, disorder or
          abnormal physical state, or its symptoms, or the restoration
          or correction of organic functions, in human beings or
          animals,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} manufactured or sold by any person other
          than a licence holder referred to in paragraph 2(a) of the
          [Cannabis Exemption (Food and Drugs Act)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-231) for use in the
          diagnosis, treatment, mitigation or prevention of a disease,
          disorder or abnormal physical state, or its symptoms, or in
          the restoration or correction of organic functions, in human
          beings or animals,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} referred to in any of paragraphs 1(b)
          to (d) of the [Cannabis Exemption (Food and Drugs Act)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-231), or

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} referred to in paragraph 1(f) of the
          [Cannabis Exemption (Food and Drugs Act)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-231) in respect of an
          [[experimental study]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
          subsection C.08.013(2) of the [Food and Drug
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870);

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} involving the distribution of cannabis that
        contains any substance that is referred to in column 1 of
        Schedule 5 to the Act and that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cannabis that is a cannabis
          product or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a
          cannabis product, does not meet the requirements set out in
          subsection 101.3(5.1) or (6) or section 101.31, 102.2 or
          102.3, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other cannabis, would not
          meet the requirements set out in subsection 101.3(5.1) or (6)
          or section 101.31, 102.2 or 102.3 if it were a cannabis
          product or if it were contained in a cannabis accessory that
          is a cannabis product; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} involving the participation of, or relating to,
        a young person. ([recherche non thérapeutique sur le
        cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[operations area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[operations area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
      site set out in a licence, an area of the site --- other than a
      storage area --- where cannabis is present as a result of any
      activities conducted under a licence. It includes a grow
      area. ([zone d'exploitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pest control product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pest control product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as in subsection 2(1) of the [Pest Control Products
      Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01). ([produit
      antiparasitaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pharmacist]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pharmacist]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who is
      entitled under the laws of a province to practise pharmacy and who
      is practising pharmacy in that
      province. ([pharmacien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the unit of measurement for
      type size that is known as a PostScript point and is equal to
      0.3527777778 mm. ([point]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[potential to convert CBDA into CBD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[potential to convert CBDA into CBD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the maximum amount of CBD that would be obtained if CBDA was
      converted into CBD with no further degradation of CBD. ([potentiel
      de transformation de l'ACBD en CBD]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[potential to convert THCA into THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[potential to convert THCA into THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the maximum amount of THC that would be obtained if THCA was
      converted into THC with no further degradation of THC. ([potentiel
      de transformation de l'ATHC en THC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[poultry product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[poultry product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([produit de
      volaille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescription]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescription]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([ordonnance]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[prescription drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescription drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a drug

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that contains cannabis;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is a [[prescription
        drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section A.01.010 of
        the [Food and Drug
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870); and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for which a drug identification number has been
        assigned under subsection C.01.014.2(1) of the [Food and Drug
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([drogue sur
        ordonnance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[proper name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[proper name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
      propre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reference standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reference standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a standardized
      form of cannabis that is intended to be used as a measurement base
      when testing a substance to confirm its identity, concentration,
      quality or purity for a medical, laboratory, industrial,
      educational, law administration or enforcement, or research
      purpose. ([étalon de référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[security clearance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security clearance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in
      paragraph 53(2)(g), a security clearance granted by the Minister
      under section 67 of the Act and includes, for the purpose of
      paragraph 53(2)(e), a security clearance granted under section 112
      of the former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230). ([habilitation de
      sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a holder of a
      licence, an area that is used exclusively by the holder and that
      consists of at least one building or one part of a
      building. ([lieu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[storage area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[storage area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a site
      set out in a licence, an area of the site where cannabis is
      stored. ([zone d'entreposage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sugars]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sugars]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([sucres]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[test kit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[test kit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a kit

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that contains

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} cannabis, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a reagent system or buffering agent, or
          both;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is designed to be used during the course
        of a chemical or analytical procedure to test for the presence
        or quantity of cannabis for a medical, laboratory, industrial,
        educational, law administration or enforcement, or research
        purpose; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the contents of which are not intended or
        likely to be consumed or administered. ([nécessaire
        d'essai]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      delta-9-tetrahydrocannabinol. ([THC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[THCA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[THCA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means delta-9-tetrahydrocannabinolic
      acid. ([ATHC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} )

  [[vitamin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vitamin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, except in subsection 101.3(3),
      has the same meaning as in subsection D.01.002(1) of the [Food and
      Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([vitamine]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[water activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[water activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, except in section 102.5,
      means the ratio of the water vapour pressure of a meat product,
      poultry product or fish to the vapour pressure of pure water, at
      the same temperature and pressure. ([activité de
      l'eau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of the incorporation by reference
  into these Regulations of any documents published by the Government of
  Canada, terms that are used but not defined in those documents have
  the same meaning as in these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming --- immediate container

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a cannabis
  accessory that contains edible cannabis in liquid form at a
  temperature of 22 ± 2°C and that is a cannabis product is deemed to be
  an immediate container.

## [Non-application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-847686 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- [Industrial Hemp
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-145)

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations do not
apply to a holder of a licence that is subject to the [Industrial Hemp
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-145), or to an applicant for such
a licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- holder of cannabis
drug licence

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** Subsection 10(1), sections
29 to 48 and Parts 5 and 7 do not apply to a holder of a cannabis drug
licence referred to in Part 8, or to an applicant for such a licence.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[General Authorizations and Prohibition]{.HTitleText1} {#h-847691 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities --- federal or
provincial laboratory

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Individuals who are involved in the
  testing of cannabis as a requirement of their duties at a laboratory
  that is operated by the Government of Canada or the government of a
  province are authorized to conduct the following activities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the extent necessary to conduct the testing,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to obtain cannabis by altering its chemical or
      physical properties by any means,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed viability testing, to
      obtain cannabis by cultivating it, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} to distribute cannabis to any of the following:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an individual referred to in subsection (4), or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} other individuals who are involved in the
        testing of cannabis as a requirement of their duties at a
        laboratory that is operated by the Government of Canada or the
        government of a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of producing reference standards or
    manufacturing or assembling test kits, to produce cannabis, other
    than obtain it by cultivating, propagating or harvesting it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to sell or distribute reference standards to any of
    the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for cultivation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for research,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} an individual referred to in subsection (4),

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been
      granted under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis
      or the class of cannabis that is distributed, or

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} other individuals who are involved in the
      testing of cannabis as a requirement of their duties at a
      laboratory that is operated by the Government of Canada or the
      government of a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual who conducts an activity referred to in
  subparagraph (1)(a)(ii) or (iii) or paragraph (1)(b) is also
  authorized to offer to conduct that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of an organic solvent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An individual who conducts an activity referred to in
  subparagraph (1)(a)(ii) or paragraph (1)(b) is also authorized to
  alter or offer to alter the chemical or physical properties of
  cannabis by the use of an organic solvent when conducting that
  activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities --- accredited
  laboratory

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is involved in the testing of
  cannabis as a requirement of their duties at a laboratory that is
  designated as an accredited laboratory under section 2.1 of the [Seeds
  Act](/eng/acts/S-8) is authorized to conduct the activities referred
  to in subparagraphs (1)(a)(i), (iii) and (iv), and to offer to conduct
  the activity referred to in subparagraph (1)(a)(iii), to the extent
  necessary to conduct the testing.

::: HistoricalNote

Version](section-4-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution --- analytical testing

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** An individual is authorized
to distribute cannabis to a holder of a licence for analytical testing
if the quantity of cannabis does not exceed the equivalent of 30 g of
dried cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4) of the
Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of cannabis containing caffeine

**[[5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 34(1) of the Act,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that authorizes
  the sale of cannabis may, in accordance with the licence, sell edible
  cannabis that is not a cannabis product and that contains caffeine if
  the caffeine has been introduced through the use of ingredients that
  naturally contain caffeine; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following persons may, in accordance with their
  licence or the provincial authorization, as the case may be, sell
  edible cannabis that is a cannabis product and that contains caffeine
  if the caffeine has been introduced through the use of ingredients
  that naturally contain caffeine and the total amount of caffeine in
  each immediate container of the cannabis product does not exceed
  30 mg:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
    authorizes the sale of cannabis,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale that authorizes the
    sale of cannabis products, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
    Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 3](#1143256-1203379){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143256-1203379.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of cannabis containing ethyl alcohol

- **[[5.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 34(1) of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
    authorizes the sale of cannabis may, in accordance with the licence,
    sell a cannabis extract that is not a cannabis product and that
    contains ethyl alcohol; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following persons may, in accordance with their
    licence or the provincial authorization, as the case may be, sell a
    cannabis extract that is a cannabis product and that contains ethyl
    alcohol if the cannabis extract is intended to be consumed by means
    of inhalation and meets the requirements set out in subsection
    101.3(5.1) or if it is intended to be ingested and meets the
    requirements set out in subsection 101.3(6):

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
      authorizes the sale of cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale that authorizes
      the sale of cannabis products, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis topical

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 34(1) of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
    authorizes the sale of cannabis may, in accordance with the licence,
    sell a cannabis topical that is not a cannabis product and that
    contains denatured ethyl alcohol; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following persons may, in accordance with their
    licence or the provincial authorization, as the case may be, sell a
    cannabis topical that is a cannabis product and that contains
    denatured ethyl alcohol:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
      authorizes the sale of cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale that authorizes
      the sale of cannabis products; and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Edible cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 34(1) of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
    authorizes the sale of cannabis may, in accordance with the licence,
    sell edible cannabis that is not a cannabis product and that
    contains ethyl alcohol; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following persons may, in accordance with their
    licence or the provincial authorization, as the case may be, sell
    edible cannabis that is a cannabis product and that contains ethyl
    alcohol if the concentration of ethyl alcohol does not exceed
    0.5% w/w of the edible cannabis:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
      authorizes the sale of cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale that authorizes
      the sale of cannabis products, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference standard

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 34(1) of the Act, the
  following persons are authorized to sell a reference standard that
  contains any substance that is referred to in column 1 of Schedule 5
  to the Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that
    authorizes the sale of cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing that
    authorizes the sale of cannabis; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition to sell --- voluntary recall

**[[5.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.3 .sectionLabel}** A person that is
authorized under a provincial Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the
Act to sell cannabis must not sell a cannabis product that they know is
the subject of a voluntary recall in Canada that has been commenced for
reasons respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quality of the cannabis product; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicable requirements of Part 5 or 6 are
  otherwise not being met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 3](#1143256-1203402){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143256-1203402.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference standards --- prohibition

**[[5.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.4 .sectionLabel}** A reference standard
must not be used as an ingredient, be included in a cannabis product or
be packaged and labelled for sale to a consumer at the retail level.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 4](#1381742-1382728){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381742-1382728.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference standards appealing to young
persons

**[[5.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 31 of the Act, the following persons are authorized to sell a
reference standard even if it has an appearance, shape or other sensory
attribute or a function that there are reasonable grounds to believe
could be appealing to young persons:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing that authorizes
  the sale of cannabis;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing that
  authorizes the sale of cannabis; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 4](#1381742-1382735){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381742-1382735.html"}
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Licensing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-847707 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include administering.

### [Pre-licensing Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847710 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to local authorities

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  submitting an application to the Minister for a licence for
  cultivation, a licence for processing or a licence for sale that
  authorizes the possession of cannabis, the person that intends to
  submit the application must provide a written notice to the following
  authorities in the area in which the site referred to in the
  application is located:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the local government;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the local fire authority; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the local police force or the Royal Canadian
    Mounted Police detachment that is responsible for providing policing
    services to that area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the person expects to submit the
    application;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the class and the subclass of licence that will be
    sought, the activity that the person expects to conduct under the
    licence and an indication that the activity will be conducted in
    relation to cannabis; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the site where the person proposes
    to conduct the activities and, if applicable, of each building
    within the site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Senior official

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be addressed to a senior official of
  the local authority to which it is provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[local
  government]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[local
  government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an incorporated city, metropolitan area, town,
    village or other municipality;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an authority responsible for delivering municipal
    services that are related to the activities to be conducted under
    the licence to an unincorporated city, metropolitan area, town,
    village or other municipality;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a [[band]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in
    subsection 2(1) of the [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a First Nation, Métis or Inuit government that is
    party to a self-government or land claims agreement that is given
    effect by an Act of Parliament, or a First Nation, Métis or Inuit
    government established under a provincial Act.

### [Classes of Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847731 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classes of licences

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following, among others, are established as classes of licences that
  authorize activities in relation to cannabis:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence for cultivation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a licence for processing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a licence for analytical testing;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a licence for sale;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a licence for research; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis drug licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Drug containing cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the licences referred to in
  paragraphs (1)(a), (b) and (d) authorize activities in relation to
  cannabis, other than a drug containing cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subclasses --- cultivation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following, among others, are established as
  subclasses of a licence for cultivation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence for micro-cultivation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a licence for standard cultivation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a licence for a nursery.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subclasses --- processing

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following, among others, are established as
  subclasses of a licence for processing:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence for micro-processing; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a licence for standard processing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subclasses --- sale

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A licence for sale for medical purposes is
  established as a subclass, among others, of a licence for sale.

### [Licence Content]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847754 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence --- content

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** A licence, other than a
licence referred to in paragraph 8(1)(f), must set out the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder of the licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence number;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the class of the licence and, if applicable, the
  subclass of the licence;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the site where the activity is
  authorized and, if applicable, of each building within the site;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the authorized activity at the site and, if
  applicable, the authorized activity that may be conducted at each
  building within the site;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} any conditions that the Minister considers
  appropriate;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the licence; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the licence.

### [Possession]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847765 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obtaining cannabis

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence that authorizes the
  possession of cannabis must only possess cannabis that was obtained in
  accordance with the former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230), the former [Industrial
  Hemp Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-145) or the [Industrial
  Hemp Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-145) or that is obtained
  in accordance with these Regulations or from a person that is
  authorized under a provincial Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of
  the Act to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- licence for cultivation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for cultivation is authorized
  to possess cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds that were not
  obtained in accordance with subsection (1) if the holder had submitted
  to the Minister, with the licence application, a declaration, signed
  and dated by the individual who signed and dated the application,
  indicating the quantity of such cannabis plants and cannabis plant
  seeds that they will have in their possession on the effective date of
  the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized quantity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of cannabis plants and cannabis plant
  seeds that the holder is authorized to possess under subsection (2)
  must be equal to the amount indicated in the declaration.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 4](#1143258-1203405){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143258-1203405.html"}
:::

### [Cultivation Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847773 .Subheading}

#### [Licences for Micro-cultivation and Standard Cultivation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-847774 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation is authorized to conduct those of the following
  activities that are authorized by the licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to obtain dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis
    pollen, cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds by cultivating,
    propagating and harvesting cannabis;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of testing, to obtain cannabis by
    altering its chemical or physical properties by any means; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation that is authorized to conduct the activity
  referred to in paragraph (1)(b) is also authorized to offer to conduct
  that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary activities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation that is authorized to conduct the activity
  referred to in paragraph (1)(b) is also authorized, to the extent
  necessary to conduct that activity, to conduct ancillary activities
  such as drying, trimming and milling cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation that is authorized to conduct the activity
  referred to in paragraph (1)(c) is also authorized to alter the
  chemical or physical properties of cannabis by the use of an organic
  solvent when conducting that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation whose licence authorizes the sale of cannabis is
  authorized to conduct the following activities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute dried cannabis, fresh
    cannabis, cannabis pollen, cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds
    to any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
      standard cultivation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for research,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the Minister,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
      under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or class
      of cannabis that is sold or distributed; or

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute cannabis pollen, cannabis
    plants and cannabis plant seeds to a holder of a licence for a
    nursery;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute cannabis plants and cannabis
    plant seeds, that are cannabis products, to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis;
      and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to send and deliver cannabis plants and cannabis
    plant seeds, that are cannabis products, to the purchaser of the
    products at the request of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Client's shipping address

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation sends or delivers cannabis plants and cannabis
  plant seeds under subparagraph (5)(d)(ii) the sale of such products
  under section 289, the holder must send or deliver the products to the
  client's shipping address as indicated by the holder of a licence for
  sale for medical purposes.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 5](#1143260-1203409){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143260-1203409.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 3](#1514837-1523835){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514837-1523835.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Master grower

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or standard cultivation must
  retain the services of one individual as a master grower.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities and knowledge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The master grower is responsible for the cultivation,
  propagation and harvesting of cannabis and must have sufficient
  knowledge of the provisions of the Act and these Regulations in
  relation to those activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-cultivation or
  standard cultivation may designate one individual as the alternate
  master grower who is qualified to replace the master grower.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Micro-cultivation --- threshold

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for micro-cultivation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must clearly delineate a surface area that does not
    exceed 800 m^2^ in which all the cannabis plants, including all the
    parts of the plants, must be contained; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must cultivate, propagate or harvest cannabis
    plants only from that surface area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surface area --- calculation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the surface area referred to in paragraph (1)(a)
  consists of multiple surfaces, such as surfaces arranged above one
  another, the area of each surface must be included in the calculation
  of the total surface area.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 4](#1514844-1523838){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514844-1523838.html"}
:::

:::

#### [Licence for Nursery]{.HTitleText3} {#h-847822 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence for a nursery is authorized
  to conduct those of the following activities that are authorized by
  the licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to obtain cannabis pollen, cannabis plants or
    cannabis plant seeds by cultivating, propagating and harvesting
    cannabis;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of testing, to obtain cannabis by
    altering its chemical or physical properties by any means; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for a nursery that is
  authorized to conduct the activity referred to in paragraph (1)(b) is
  also authorized to offer to conduct that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary activity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for a nursery that is
  authorized to conduct the activity referred to in paragraph (1)(b) is
  also authorized, to the extent necessary to conduct that activity, to
  conduct ancillary activities such as drying cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for a nursery that is
  authorized to conduct the activity referred to in paragraph (1)(c) is
  also authorized to alter the chemical or physical properties of
  cannabis by the use of an organic solvent when conducting that
  activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for a nursery whose licence
  authorizes the sale of cannabis is authorized to conduct the following
  activities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute cannabis pollen, cannabis
    plants and cannabis plant seeds to any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for cultivation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for research,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the Minister,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
      under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or class
      of cannabis that is sold or distributed, or

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute cannabis plants and cannabis
    plant seeds, that are cannabis products, to the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis;
      and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to send and deliver cannabis plants and cannabis
    plant seeds, that are cannabis products, to the purchaser of the
    products at the request of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Client's shipping address

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a holder of a licence for a nursery sends or
  delivers cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds under subparagraph
  (5)(c)(ii) further to the sale of such products under section 289, the
  holder must send or deliver the products to the client's shipping
  address as indicated by the holder of a licence for sale for medical
  purposes.

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Master grower

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for a nursery must retain the services of one
  individual as a master grower.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities and knowledge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The master grower is responsible for the cultivation,
  propagation and harvesting of cannabis and must have sufficient
  knowledge of the provisions of the Act and these Regulations in
  relation to those activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for a nursery may designate one
  individual as the alternate master grower who is qualified to replace
  the master grower.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obtaining cannabis plant seeds

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for a nursery that cultivates cannabis for the
  purpose of obtaining cannabis plant seeds must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} clearly delineate a total surface area that does
    not exceed 200 m^2^ in which all the budding or flowering cannabis
    plants, including all the parts of those plants, must be contained;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not possess more than 20 kg of flowering heads
    harvested from the plants referred to in paragraph (a), with the
    exception of the cannabis plant seeds; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} destroy the flowering heads --- with the exception
    of the cannabis plant seeds --- leaves and branches of the plants
    referred to in paragraph (a) within 30 days of harvesting them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surface area --- calculation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the surface area referred to in paragraph (1)(a)
  consists of multiple surfaces, such as surfaces arranged above one
  another, the area of each surface must be included in the calculation
  of the total surface area.

  ajax-after="1514851-1523845.html"}
:::

### [Processing Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847870 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities --- licence for
standard processing

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence for standard processing is
  authorized to conduct those of the following activities that are
  authorized by the licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to produce cannabis, other than obtain it by
    cultivating, propagating or harvesting it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities --- licence for
  micro-processing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of these Regulations,
  a holder of a licence for micro-processing is authorized to conduct
  those of the following activities that are authorized by the licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to produce cannabis, other than obtain it by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} synthesis, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} cultivating, propagating or harvesting it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-processing or
  standard processing that is authorized to conduct the activity
  referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (2)(b) is also authorized to offer
  to obtain cannabis by any method authorized by the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-processing or
  standard processing that is authorized to conduct the activity
  referred to in paragraph 1(b) or (2)(b) is also authorized to alter or
  offer to alter the chemical or physical properties of cannabis by the
  use of an organic solvent when conducting that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for micro-processing or
  standard processing whose licence authorizes the sale of cannabis is
  authorized to conduct the following activities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute cannabis to any of the
    following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for research,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the Minister,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
      under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or a
      class of cannabis that is sold or distributed, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute the following to a holder of
    a licence for micro-cultivation or standard cultivation:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis plants
      and cannabis plant seeds, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} reference standards;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute the following to a holder of
    a licence for a nursery:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} reference standards;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to sell and distribute cannabis products to the
    following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis;
      and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to send and deliver cannabis products to the
    purchaser of the products at the request of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person that is authorized under a provincial
      Act referred to in subsection 69(1) of the Act to sell cannabis,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Client's shipping address

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a holder of a licence for micro-processing or
  standard processing sends or delivers cannabis products under
  subparagraph (5)(e)(ii) further to the sale of such products under
  section 289, the holder must send or deliver the products to the
  client's shipping address as indicated by the holder of a licence for
  sale for medical purposes.

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43,
s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quality assurance person

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must retain the services of one
  individual as a quality assurance person who has the training,
  experience and technical knowledge related to the requirements of
  Parts 5 and 6 that are applicable to the class of cannabis in respect
  of which activities are conducted under the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- edible cannabis

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if the quality assurance
  person does not have the training, experience and technical knowledge
  related to the requirements of Parts 5 and 6 that are applicable to
  edible cannabis, the holder of a licence for processing that conducts
  activities in respect of that class of cannabis must retain the
  services of another individual who has that training, experience and
  technical knowledge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quality assurance person is responsible for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} assuring the quality of the cannabis before it is
    made available for sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} investigating every complaint received in respect
    of the quality of the cannabis and, if necessary, immediately taking
    measures to mitigate any risk; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if they suspect, on reasonable grounds, that the
    cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient presents a
    risk of injury to human health or that the applicable requirements
    of Part 5 or 6 are otherwise not being met, immediately
    investigating the matter and, if necessary, immediately taking
    measures to mitigate any risk.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for processing may designate
  one or more individuals as alternate quality assurance persons who are
  qualified to replace the quality assurance person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) do not apply in respect of a
  reference standard or a test kit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 9](#1143279-1203430){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143279-1203430.html"}
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 6](#1381753-1382771){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381753-1382771.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 8](#1514854-1523849){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514854-1523849.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20221202.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's approval

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must obtain the Minister's approval
  before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating or replacing an alternate quality
    assurance person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} replacing the quality assurance person by an
    individual, other than by the alternate quality assurance person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for processing must, for the
  purpose of obtaining the Minister's approval, submit an application
  that includes the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and date of birth of the proposed
    alternate quality assurance person or the proposed quality assurance
    person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the proposed quality assurance
    person's or the proposed alternate quality assurance person's
    qualifications in respect of the matters referred to in subsection
    19(1); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the responsible
    person referred to in section 37, indicating that all information
    provided in support of the application is correct and complete to
    the best of their knowledge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on receiving an application for
  approval, require the submission of any additional information that
  pertains to the information contained in the application and that is
  necessary for the Minister to consider the application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Micro-processing --- threshold

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for micro-processing must not possess, in a
  calendar year, cannabis of one or more classes of cannabis --- other
  than cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds --- that has been sold
  or distributed to them, the total amount of which, as determined in
  accordance with the table to this section, is equivalent to more than
  2,400 kg of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cannabis referred to in subsection (1) is exempt
  from the application of subsection 2(4) of the Act and a quantity
  referred to in column 2 of the table to this section in respect of any
  class of cannabis referred to in column 1 is deemed to be equivalent
  to 1 kg of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the holder also
  holds a licence for micro-cultivation in relation to the same site and
  the cannabis that is sold or distributed to them is exclusively from
  that site.

### [Licence for Analytical Testing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847957 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence for analytical testing is
  authorized to conduct those of the following activities that are
  authorized by the licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of testing,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to obtain cannabis by altering its chemical or
      physical properties by any means;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of producing reference standards or
    manufacturing or assembling test kits, to produce cannabis, other
    than obtain it by cultivating, propagating or harvesting it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for analytical testing that is
  authorized to conduct the activity referred to in subparagraph
  (1)(a)(ii) or paragraph (1)(b) is also authorized to offer to conduct
  that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for analytical testing that is
  authorized to conduct the activity referred to in subparagraph
  (1)(a)(ii) or paragraph (1)(b) is also authorized to alter or offer to
  alter the chemical or physical properties of cannabis by the use of an
  organic solvent when conducting that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for analytical testing is also
  authorized, for the purpose of testing, to distribute cannabis to
  another holder of a licence for analytical testing or the individuals
  referred to in section 4.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for analytical testing whose
  licence authorizes the sale of cannabis is authorized to sell and
  distribute reference standards to any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for cultivation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for research;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} another holder of a licence for analytical testing;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Minister; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
    under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or class of
    cannabis that is sold or distributed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 11](#1143286-1203438){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143286-1203438.html"}
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 7](#1381755-1382792){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381755-1382792.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Head of laboratory

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for analytical testing must retain the services of
  one individual as the head of laboratory who must work at the site set
  out in the licence and who is responsible for the testing referred to
  in sections 90 to 91.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifications

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The head of laboratory must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have sufficient knowledge of the provisions of the
    Act and these Regulations that apply to the holder of the licence
    for analytical testing to properly carry out the duties of the
    position;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have knowledge and experience relevant to the
    duties of the position; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} hold

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a diploma, certificate or credential awarded by a
      post-secondary educational institution in Canada in a field or
      occupation that is relevant to the duties of the position, such as
      chemistry, biology, pharmaceutical or scientific regulatory
      affairs, laboratory management or laboratory technician, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a diploma, certificate or credential awarded by
      a foreign educational institution in a field or occupation
      referred to in subparagraph (i) and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an [[equivalency
        assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection
        73(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-227), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an equivalency assessment issued by an
        organization or institution that is responsible for issuing
        equivalency assessments and is recognized by a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for analytical testing may
  designate one or more individuals as the alternate heads of laboratory
  who are qualified to replace the head of laboratory.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 12](#1143288-1203441){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143288-1203441.html"}
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 8](#1381760-1382802){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381760-1382802.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's approval

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for analytical testing must obtain the Minister's
  approval before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating or replacing an alternate head of
    laboratory; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} replacing the head of laboratory by an individual,
    other than by an alternate head of laboratory.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for analytical testing must,
  for the purpose of obtaining the Minister's approval, submit an
  application that includes the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and date of birth of the proposed
    alternate head of laboratory or the proposed head of laboratory;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the proposed head of laboratory's
    or the proposed alternate head of laboratory's qualifications in
    respect of the matters referred to in subsection 23(2); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the responsible
    person referred to in section 37, indicating that all information
    provided in support of the application is correct and complete to
    the best of their knowledge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on receiving an application for
  approval, require the submission of any additional information that
  pertains to the information contained in the application and that is
  necessary for the Minister to consider the application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for analytical testing must destroy the sample of
  a lot or batch of cannabis that has been distributed to them, and all
  cannabis obtained from that sample, within 90 days after completing
  the testing of the sample of the lot or batch.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sample not tested

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If testing of the sample of a lot or batch of
  cannabis distributed to the holder of the licence for analytical
  testing is not initiated within 120 days of its receipt, the holder
  must, by the end of that period, either destroy the sample or
  distribute it to another holder of a licence for analytical testing or
  to the individuals referred to in section 4.
