### [Licence for Sale for Medical Purposes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-847991 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a holder of a licence for sale for
medical purposes is authorized to conduct those of the following
activities that are authorized by the licence:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis products; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- other than clients

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a holder
  of a licence for sale for medical purposes that authorizes the sale of
  cannabis products is authorized to sell or distribute

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products to any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence, other than a licence for
      cultivation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
      under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or class
      of cannabis that is, or is contained in, a cannabis product that
      is sold or distributed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis plants and
    cannabis plant seeds, that are cannabis products, to a holder of a
    licence for micro-cultivation or standard cultivation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds, that are
    cannabis products, to a holder of a licence for a nursery; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products, other than cannabis plants and
    cannabis plant seeds, to a hospital employee if the employee's
    possession of the cannabis product is for the purpose of, and in
    connection with, their duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale for medical purposes
  must sell the cannabis products referred to in subsection (1) in the
  packaging in which they were sold or distributed to the holder.

### [Licence for Research]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848008 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence for research is authorized to
  conduct those of the following activities, that are authorized by the
  licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of research,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to produce cannabis, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to transport, send or deliver cannabis between
      the sites that are set out by the licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to sell cannabis pollen, cannabis plants and
    cannabis plant seeds to any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for cultivation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} another holder of a licence for research,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the Minister, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
      under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or class
      of cannabis that is sold.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for research that is authorized
  to conduct the activity referred to in subparagraph (1)(a)(ii) is also
  authorized to offer to obtain cannabis by any method authorized by the
  licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for research that is authorized
  to obtain cannabis by altering its chemical or physical properties by
  any means is also authorized to alter or offer to alter its chemical
  or physical properties by the use of an organic solvent when
  conducting that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administer and distribute --- research
  subject

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for research is also
  authorized, for the purpose of research, to administer and distribute
  cannabis to a research subject.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for research is also authorized
  to distribute

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis to any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} another holder of a licence for research,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the individuals referred to in section 4, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cannabis pollen, cannabis plants and cannabis plant
    seeds to the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for cultivation, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted
      under section 140 of the Act in relation to the cannabis or class
      of cannabis that is distributed.

Version](section-28-20221202.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Research not involving human participants
or animals

- **[[28.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a person,
  other than a young person, may conduct research on cannabis without a
  licence for research if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} possesses, for the purpose of research, a total
    quantity of cannabis that does not exceed the equivalent of 30 g of
    dried cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not administer or distribute cannabis to a
    human participant or to an animal; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} does not conduct the research within a
    dwelling-house or at an address specified in a registration
    certificate issued under subsection 313(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person is authorized, to the extent necessary to
  conduct the research,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to possess cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to produce cannabis, other than obtain it by
    cultivating, propagating or harvesting it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to distribute cannabis to a holder of a licence for
    analytical testing or a holder of a licence for research.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person that is authorized to conduct the activity
  referred to in paragraph (2)(b) is also authorized to alter or to
  offer to alter the chemical or physical properties of cannabis by the
  use of an organic solvent when conducting that activity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 11](#1514863-1523869){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514863-1523869.html"}
:::

### [Non-therapeutic Research on Cannabis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1382812 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements of Part 6

- **[[28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The requirements of Part 6, other
  than those set out in subsection 102(6), apply to the following
  cannabis or cannabis accessory that is administered or distributed to
  a human participant in the conduct of non-therapeutic research on
  cannabis as if the cannabis or cannabis accessory were a cannabis
  product or will become one:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis that is not a cannabis product or is
    contained in a cannabis accessory that is not a cannabis product;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis accessory that contains cannabis and
    that is not a cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to section 97

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), any reference in
  Part 6 to subsection 97(1) or (2) should be read as a reference to
  paragraph (3)(a) or (b), respectively.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variability limits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis that is not a cannabis product --- or that
  is contained in a cannabis accessory that is not a cannabis product
  --- and that is administered or distributed by the holder of a licence
  for research to human participants in the conduct of non-therapeutic
  research on cannabis must not contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis extract or a cannabis
    topical, less than 85% or more than 115% of the quantity or
    concentration of THC or CBD represented by the holder in the
    application for the licence, or for its renewal or amendment,
    related to that research; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of edible cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the quantity of THC or CBD that is represented
      by the holder in the application exceeds 5 mg, less than 85% or
      more than 115% of that quantity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the quantity of THC or CBD that is
      represented by the holder in the application exceeds 2 mg but does
      not exceed 5 mg, less than 80% or more than 120% of that quantity,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the quantity of THC or CBD that is
      represented by the holder in the application does not exceed 2 mg,
      less than 75% or more than 125% of that quantity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements --- edible cannabis

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for research that administers
  or distributes edible cannabis to human participants in the conduct of
  non-therapeutic research on cannabis must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a meat product, poultry product or fish, other than
    a food additive, was not used as an ingredient to produce the edible
    cannabis, and was not a constituent of such an ingredient, unless
    the meat product, poultry product or fish

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} was produced by a person that is authorized to
      produce it under the laws of a province or the [Safe Food for
      Canadians Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1) or was imported in accordance with
      that Act, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} had a water activity that did not exceed 0.85 at
      a temperature of 22 ± 2°C at the time the meat product, poultry
      product or fish was obtained by the holder that produced the
      edible cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} food that was produced by a holder of a licence for
    research and that was used by that holder as an ingredient to
    produce the edible cannabis, or as a constituent of such an
    ingredient, was not a meat product, poultry product or fish, and the
    sale of the food would not have been prohibited under section 4 of
    the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a food additive was not used as an ingredient to
    produce the edible cannabis unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the edible cannabis would be a food that is set
      out in column 3 of the Lists of Permitted Food Additives if the
      edible cannabis did not contain or have on it anything referred to
      in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the food additive is set out in column 1 and is
      not caffeine or caffeine citrate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the food additive is obtained from any
      corresponding source set out in column 2,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the food additive is used for any corresponding
      purpose of use set out in column 4,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the food additive does not exceed

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} any corresponding maximum level of use and any
        corresponding maximum level of residue set out in column 5, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the amount that is required to accomplish the
        purpose for which it is added, in the case where the words "Good
        Manufacturing Practice" appear in column 5 for that food
        additive in the food, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} any other corresponding condition set out in
      column 5 is met; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the edible cannabis was not irradiated unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the edible cannabis would be a food that is
      listed in item 3 or 4, column 1, of the table to Division 26 of
      Part B of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) if the edible
      cannabis did not contain or have on it anything that is referred
      to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder that produced the edible
      cannabis satisfied the requirements set out in paragraphs
      B.26.003(2)(a) and (b) and subsection B.26.004(1) of the [Food and
      Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) in respect of
      the edible cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vitamins and mineral nutrients

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the conditions set out in subparagraphs (4)(c)(i)
  to (vi) are met, the holder of a licence for research whose licence
  authorizes the production of cannabis may use a vitamin or mineral
  nutrient as an ingredient to produce edible cannabis that is
  administered or distributed to a human participant in the conduct of
  non-therapeutic research on cannabis and that is not a cannabis
  product or is contained in a cannabis accessory that is not a cannabis
  product.

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Irradiated edible cannabis

**[[28.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.2 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
licence for research that administers or distributes irradiated edible
cannabis to human participants in the conduct of non-therapeutic
research on cannabis must ensure that the requirement set out in
subsection 42(1) has been met in addition to the conditions set out in
paragraph 28.1(4)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 10](#1381767-1382851){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381767-1382851.html"}

  ajax-after="1514865-1523872.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28.2-20221202.html "Link to previous version of section 28.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of cannabis

- **[[28.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph
  9(1)(a)(i) or (iii) of the Act, a human participant to whom cannabis
  was distributed in the conduct of non-therapeutic research on cannabis
  is authorized to return the cannabis to the holder of the licence for
  research from which the cannabis was received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parcel --- requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The participant who is returning cannabis under
  subsection (1) must, if they are sending or having delivered a total
  quantity of cannabis that exceeds the equivalent of 30 g of dried
  cannabis,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prepare the parcel in a manner that ensures the
    security of its contents, such that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it will not open or permit the escape of its
      contents during handling and transportation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is sealed so that it cannot be opened without
      the seal being broken, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it prevents its contents from being identified
      without it being opened; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use a method that ensures the tracking and
    safekeeping of the parcel during transportation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 10](#1381767-1382864){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381767-1382864.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping --- returned cannabis

**[[28.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.4 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for research that receives cannabis from a human participant under
section 28.3 must retain a document that contains the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the participant;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the cannabis is
  received and, if that location is different from the site or sites at
  which the cannabis was produced, the address of the site or sites, if
  known;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis is received;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is received;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
  applicable, the brand name; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the lot or batch number of the
  cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 10](#1381767-1382875){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381767-1382875.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of returned cannabis

**[[28.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.5 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for research must destroy any cannabis that has been returned to them by
a human participant under section 28.3 no later than 90 days after the
day on which the non-therapeutic research on cannabis ends.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 10](#1381767-1382880){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381767-1382880.html"}
:::

### [Refusal, Suspension and Revocation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848038 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue, renew or amend ---
other grounds

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 62(7)(h) of the Act, other grounds for refusing to issue,
renew or amend a licence are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is required to hold a security
  clearance under section 50 in respect of an application does not hold
  such a security clearance;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the renewal or amendment of a licence,
  the holder of the licence does not hold a cannabis licence issued
  under subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1),
  if it is required;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for cultivation, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by another licence for cultivation;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for micro-cultivation, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by a licence for standard processing and a licence for
  micro-cultivation;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for standard cultivation, the site proposed in the application would
  be authorized by a licence for micro-processing and a licence for
  standard cultivation;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for a nursery, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by a licence for processing and a licence for a nursery;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for processing, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by another licence for processing;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for processing, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by a licence for processing and a licence for a nursery;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for micro-processing, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by a licence for standard cultivation and a licence for
  micro-processing;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance or amendment of a licence
  for standard processing, the site proposed in the application would be
  authorized by a licence for micro-cultivation and a licence for
  standard processing;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a licence for processing, the applicant
  has, in the past 10 years, been convicted of an offence under the
  [Safe Food for Canadians Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1) or an Act referred to
  in subsection 374(2) of the [Safe Food for Canadians
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-108); and

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a licence for research that would
  authorize activities in relation to non-therapeutic research on
  cannabis, the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the use of the cannabis proposed in the application
    presents a risk of injury to the health of a human participant or
    other person that cannot reasonably be mitigated, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the objectives of the non-therapeutic research on
    cannabis that are set out in the application will not be achieved.

::: HistoricalNote

  ajax-after="1143294-1203461.html"}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension --- other circumstance

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 64(1)(b) of the Act, other circumstances for the suspension of
a licence are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is required to hold a security
  clearance under section 50 in respect of the licence does not hold
  such a security clearance;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a licence has failed to pay a fee in
  relation to the licence, or any other licence issued to that holder
  that is fixed by an order made under subsection 142(1) of the Act;

- [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a licence has failed to submit to the
  Minister a statement of cannabis revenue in accordance with an order
  made under subsection 142(1) of the Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a licence does not hold a cannabis
  licence issued under subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), if it is required;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis licence issued to the holder under
  subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) is
  suspended under subsection 23(2) of that Act; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a licence for research that authorizes
  activities in relation to non-therapeutic research on cannabis, the
  Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the use of the cannabis in the conduct of the
    non-therapeutic research on cannabis presents a risk of injury to
    the health of a human participant or other person that cannot
    reasonably be mitigated, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the objectives of the non-therapeutic research on
    cannabis will not be achieved.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- other circumstances

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 65(h) of the Act, other circumstances for the revocation of a
licence are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence has requested, in writing,
  the revocation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence has been suspended and not reinstated
  because the reasons for the suspension still exist or the holder of
  the licence has not demonstrated to the Minister that the suspension
  is unfounded;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} since the issuance of the licence, a security
  clearance in respect of the licence has been refused;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence no longer holds the
  cannabis licence issued under subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), if it is required;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for processing, the holder
  has, since its issuance, been convicted of an offence under the [Safe
  Food for Canadians Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1) or an Act referred to in
  subsection 374(2) of the [Safe Food for Canadians
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-108); and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a licence for research that authorizes
  activities in relation to non-therapeutic research on cannabis, the
  Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the use of the cannabis in the conduct of the
    non-therapeutic research on cannabis presents a risk of injury to
    the health of a human participant or other person that cannot
    reasonably be mitigated, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the objectives of the non-therapeutic research on
    cannabis will not be achieved.

### [Changes Relating to Licence]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848062 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must submit an application for an amendment to the licence if they
propose to make any of the following changes:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change to the name of the holder of the licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change to the address of the site or building
  within the site where the activity is authorized; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a change to the authorized activity at the site or
  the authorized activity that may be conducted at each building within
  the site.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's approval

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence that proposes to make a change to the site plan
  that would require physical security measures to be carried out in
  order to comply with Part 4 must obtain the Minister's approval before
  making the change.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence must, for the purpose of
  obtaining the Minister's approval, submit an application that includes
  the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the change;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the proposed site plan; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the responsible
    person referred to in section 37, indicating that all information
    provided in support of the application is correct and complete to
    the best of their knowledge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on receiving an application for
  approval, require the submission of any additional information that
  pertains to the information contained in the application and that is
  necessary for the Minister to consider the application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification --- various changes

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must notify the Minister of any of the following
  changes within five days after the change occurs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change to the mailing address, telephone number,
    email address or facsimile number of the holder;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change to the site plan, other than a change
    referred to in subsection 33(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the replacement of an individual who must hold a
    security clearance referred to in any of paragraphs 50(b) to
    (g), (i) and (j) or the addition of another such individual, other
    than an individual who is designated as an alternate for the
    position of quality assurance person referred to in section 19; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a holder of a licence for
    cultivation, a licence for processing or a licence for sale, a
    change to the organizational security plan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification --- content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification must include the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the change; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the responsible
    person referred to in section 37, indicating that all information
    provided in support of the notification is correct and complete to
    the best of their knowledge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a change referred to in paragraph
  (1)(c), the notification must also include the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and date of birth of the individual who
    replaced an individual referred to in any of paragraphs 50(b) to
    (g), (i) and (j) or who was added as another such individual; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the individual replaced a director or officer
    referred to in subparagraph 50(b)(i) or (c)(i) or was added as such
    a director or officer, the certificate of incorporation or other
    amended incorporating instrument of the corporation or cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification to local authorities

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for cultivation, a licence for processing or a
  licence for sale that authorizes the possession of cannabis must,
  within 30 days after the issuance, amendment, suspension,
  reinstatement or revocation of the licence, provide a written notice
  to the local authorities referred to in paragraphs 7(1)(a) to (c) in
  the area in which the site set out in the licence is located and
  provide a copy of the notice to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder of the licence and the
    address of the site set out in the licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the applicable event referred to
    in subsection (1) and its effective date and, in the case of an
    amendment to the licence, details of the amendment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Senior official

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be addressed to a senior official of
  the local authority to which it is provided.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of activities

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence that intends to cease conducting all the
  activities authorized by the licence --- whether before or on the date
  of expiry of the licence --- must provide the Minister with a written
  notice to that effect at least 30 days before the day on which those
  activities cease.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be signed and dated by the
  responsible person referred to in section 37 and contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which activities are expected to cease;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the manner in which any cannabis
    remaining at the site as of the date referred to in paragraph (a)
    will be disposed of by the holder of the licence, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis will be sold or distributed, in
      whole or in part, the name and address of the person to which it
      will be sold or distributed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it will be destroyed, in whole or in part,
      the day on which and the location at which the destruction is to
      take place;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the holder's
    records, reports, electronic data and other documents that are
    required to be retained under the Act by the holder will be retained
    after activities have ceased; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and, if
    applicable, facsimile number and email address of a person from
    which the Minister may obtain further information after activities
    have ceased.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Update

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} After having ceased the activities, the holder must
  submit to the Minister an update of the information referred to in
  paragraphs (2)(a) to (d), if it differs from the information submitted
  in the notice under subsection (1). The update must be signed and
  dated by the responsible person referred to in section 37.

### [General Requirements Relating to Authorized Activities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848115 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsible person

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must retain the services of one individual as the
  responsible person who has the authority to bind the holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibilities and knowledge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The responsible person is responsible for the
  activities conducted under the licence and must have sufficient
  knowledge of the provisions of the Act and these Regulations that
  apply to the holder of the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence may designate one individual as
  the alternate responsible person who is qualified to replace the
  responsible person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Head of security

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, other than a licence for analytical testing or a
  licence for research, must retain the services of one individual as
  the head of security who is responsible for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensuring that the applicable physical security
    measures set out in Part 4 are complied with; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the organizational security plan of the holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence, other than a licence for
  analytical testing or a licence for research, may designate one
  individual as the alternate head of security who is qualified to
  replace the head of security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approved site

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must only conduct activities that are authorized
  by the licence at the site and, if applicable, the building within the
  site, set out in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the possession of
  cannabis for the purpose of antimicrobial treatment or destruction or
  the distribution of cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dwelling-house

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must not conduct any activity that is authorized by the licence at a
dwelling-house.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outdoor activities

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must not produce cannabis --- other than obtain cannabis by cultivating,
propagating or harvesting it --- or test, store, package or label
cannabis outdoors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Antimicrobial treatment

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, other than a licence for analytical testing, may
  conduct antimicrobial treatment of cannabis at a location other than
  the site set out in the licence only if the cannabis is subsequently
  returned to the site set out in the licence or distributed in
  accordance with these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Irradiation of edible cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, in the case of the irradiation
  of edible cannabis by a holder of a licence for processing, the
  requirement set out in subsection (1) applies in addition to the
  conditions set out in section 102.6.

Version](section-42-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence is authorized to destroy cannabis only

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a method that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} complies with all federal, provincial and
      municipal environmental protection legislation applicable to the
      location where it is to be destroyed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} does not result in any individual being exposed
      to cannabis smoke or cannabis vapour;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the presence of at least one employee of the
    holder of the licence; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the cannabis is destroyed at a
    location other than the site set out in the licence, if the holder
    of the licence ensures that the cannabis that is at the location is,
    at all times, in the presence of at least one employee of the
    licence holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) and (c) do not apply in cases where
  the holder of a licence destroys cultivation waste.

  ajax-after="1514871-1523887.html"}
:::

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43,
s. 16\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organizational security plan --- update

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence,
other than a holder of a licence for analytical testing or a licence for
research, must on request of the Minister, update and submit the
organizational security plan.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, other than a licence for analytical testing, must
  establish and maintain a system of control that permits the rapid and
  complete recall of every lot or batch of cannabis that has been sold
  or distributed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall simulation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least once every 12 months, conduct a recall
    simulation based on the system of control;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after completing the recall simulation, prepare a
    document that sets out the details of how it was conducted and the
    results; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} retain the document for at least two years after
    the day on which the recall simulation is completed.

- [SOR/2019-206, s. 19](#1143302-1203492){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143302-1203492.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-46-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safekeeping during distribution

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must take any steps that are necessary to ensure the safekeeping of
cannabis when distributing it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification of holder of a licence

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must include their name, as set out in the licence, in all the means by
which they identify themself in relation to cannabis, including
advertising, purchase orders, shipping documents and invoices.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Security Clearances]{.HTitleText1} {#h-848165 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part.

[[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include a federal or
    provincial Crown corporation. ([personne morale]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include an incorporated
    entity. ([société de personnes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement for security clearance

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** The following individuals
must hold a security clearance:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who holds a licence for cultivation,
  processing or sale;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation that holds a licence for
  cultivation, processing or sale,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of the corporation,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any individual who exercises, or is in a position
    to exercise, direct control over the corporation,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of any corporation or
    cooperative that exercises, or is in a position to exercise, direct
    control over the corporation,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any individual who is a partner in a partnership
    that exercises, or is in a position to exercise, direct control over
    the corporation, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of any corporation that
    is a partner in a partnership that exercises, or is in a position to
    exercise, direct control over the corporation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cooperative that holds a licence for
  cultivation, processing or sale,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of the cooperative,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any individual who exercises, or is in a position
    to exercise, direct control over the cooperative,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of any corporation or
    cooperative that exercises, or is in a position to exercise, direct
    control over the cooperative,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any individual who is a partner in a partnership
    that exercises, or is in a position to exercise, direct control over
    the cooperative, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of any corporation that
    is a partner in a partnership that exercises, or is in a position to
    exercise, direct control over the cooperative;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a partnership that holds a licence for
  cultivation, processing or sale,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any individual who is a partner,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of any corporation or
    cooperative that is a partner,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any individual who is a partner in a partnership
    that exercises, or is in a position to exercise, direct control over
    the partnership that holds the licence, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the directors and officers of any corporation that
    is a partner in a partnership that exercises, or is in a position to
    exercise, direct control over the partnership that holds the
    licence;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for cultivation, processing
  or sale, the responsible person referred to in section 37;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for cultivation, processing
  or sale, the head of security referred to in section 38;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for cultivation, the master
  grower referred to in section 12 or 15;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for processing, the quality
  assurance person referred to in section 19;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has been designated as an alternate
  for a position referred to in any of paragraphs (e) to (h); and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has been specified by name by the
  Minister under subsection 67(2) of the Act or who occupies a position
  that has been specified by the Minister under that subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** Only the following
individuals may submit an application for a security clearance:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is required to hold a security
  clearance;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual who will be required to hold a security
  clearance if an application for a licence, or for its renewal or
  amendment, that has been filed with the Minister results in the
  issuance, renewal or amendment of the licence;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual who will be required to hold a security
  clearance if a pending business transaction is completed;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has been selected for a position
  referred to in any of paragraphs 50(e) to (h) or as an alternate for
  such a position; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has been selected for a position
  that has been specified by the Minister under subsection 67(2) of the
  Act or who has been notified that the Minister intends to specify
  them, by name or position, under that subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Checks

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, at any
time, conduct checks that are necessary to determine whether an
applicant for, or the holder of, a security clearance poses a risk to
public health or public safety, including the risk of cannabis being
diverted to an illicit market or activity. Such checks include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a check of the applicant's or holder's criminal
  record; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a check of the relevant files of law enforcement
  agencies that relate to the applicant or holder, including
  intelligence gathered for law enforcement purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grant of security clearance

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  granting a security clearance, the Minister must, taking into account
  any licence conditions that he or she imposes under subsection 62(10)
  of the Act, determine that the applicant does not pose an unacceptable
  risk to public health or public safety, including the risk of cannabis
  being diverted to an illicit market or activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Factors that the Minister may consider to determine
  the level of risk posed by the applicant include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances of any events or convictions that
    are relevant to the determination, the seriousness of those events
    or convictions, their number and frequency, the date of the most
    recent event or conviction and any sentence or other disposition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether it is known, or there are reasonable
    grounds to suspect, that the applicant

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is or has been involved in, or contributes or has
      contributed to, an activity that is prohibited by, or conducted in
      contravention of, any of the provisions of Division 1 of Part 1 of
      the Act --- other than paragraphs 8(1)(a) to (e) --- or
      Subdivision E of Division 2 of Part 1 of the Act,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is or has been involved in, or contributes or
      has contributed to, an activity that is prohibited by, or
      conducted in contravention of, any of the provisions of Part I of
      the [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) ---
      other than subsection 4(1) --- or subsection 32(1) or (2) of that
      Act,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is or has been involved in, or contributes or
      has contributed to, an activity that is prohibited by, or
      conducted in contravention of, any provision of the [Criminal
      Code](/eng/acts/C-46) relating to fraud, corruption of public
      officials, terrorism financing, counterfeiting or laundering the
      proceeds of crime,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} is or has been involved in, or contributes or
      has contributed to, an offence involving an act of violence or the
      threat of violence,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} is or has been a member of a [[criminal
      organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection
      467.1(1) of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46), or is or has been
      involved in, or contributes or has contributed to, the activities
      of such an organization,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} is or has been a member of an organization that
      is known to be involved in or to contribute to --- or in respect
      of which there are reasonable grounds to suspect its involvement
      in or contribution to --- activities directed toward, or in
      support of, acts of violence or the threat of violence, or is or
      has been involved in, or contributes or has contributed to, the
      activities of such an organization,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} is or has been associated with an individual
      who

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is known to be involved in or to contribute to
        --- or in respect of whom there are reasonable grounds to
        suspect their involvement in or contribution to --- activities
        referred to in subparagraphs (i) to (iii), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} is a member of an organization referred to in
        subparagraph (v) or (vi), or

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} has conspired to commit

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an offence under any of the provisions of the
        [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46) referred to in subparagraph
        (iii),

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an offence referred to in subparagraph (iv), or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} an offence under any of sections 467.11 to
        467.13 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether there are reasonable grounds to suspect
    that the applicant could be induced to commit an act --- or to aid
    or abet any person to commit an act --- that might constitute a risk
    to public health or public safety;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} whether there are reasonable grounds to believe
    that the applicant's activities, including their financial
    activities, pose a risk to the integrity of the control of the
    production and distribution of cannabis under the Act;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the applicant has had a security clearance
    suspended or cancelled;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} whether there are reasonable grounds to believe the
    applicant has, now or in the past, submitted false or misleading
    information, or false or falsified documents, to the Minister; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} whether an entity has refused to issue a security
    clearance to the applicant --- or has suspended or cancelled one ---
    and the reason for the refusal, suspension or cancellation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outstanding criminal charge

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister refuses
to consider an application for a security clearance because there is an
outstanding criminal charge against the applicant that may be relevant
to the determination of the risk under subsection 53(1), the Minister
must provide the applicant, and any affected holder of or applicant for
a licence, with notice of the refusal in writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to grant security clearance

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  Minister intends to refuse to grant a security clearance, the Minister
  must provide the applicant with a notice that sets out the reason for
  the proposed refusal and that specifies the period of time within
  which they may make written representations to the Minister. The
  period must start on the day on which the notice is provided and must
  be not less than 20 days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister refuses to grant the security
  clearance, the Minister must provide the applicant, and any affected
  holder of or applicant for a licence, with notice of the refusal in
  writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity period

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must establish a validity period for a security clearance in
  accordance with the level of risk to public health or public safety
  --- including the risk of cannabis being diverted to an illicit market
  or activity --- posed by the applicant, but the period must not exceed
  five years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of validity period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the validity period of a security clearance is
  less than five years, the Minister may subsequently extend the period
  to a total of five years if the Minister, taking into account any
  licence conditions that he or she imposes under subsection 62(10) of
  the Act, determines that the holder does not pose an unacceptable risk
  to public health or public safety, including the risk of cannabis
  being diverted to an illicit market or activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The factors that the Minister may consider to
  determine the level of risk posed by the holder include those set out
  in subsection 53(2).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to notify --- offences

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a security
clearance must, without delay, provide the Minister with notice in
writing if they are charged with, or found guilty of, an offence under
the Act, the [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or
the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of security clearance

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  suspending a security clearance, the Minister must have reasonable
  grounds to believe that the risk to public health or public safety
  posed by the holder, including the risk of cannabis being diverted to
  an illicit market or activity, has become unacceptable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of suspension

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A suspension takes effect as soon as the Minister
  provides the holder with a notice in writing that sets out the reasons
  for the suspension and that specifies the period of time within which
  they may make written representations to the Minister. The period
  starts on the day on which the notice is provided and must be not less
  than 20 days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to holder of licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, without delay after suspending a
  security clearance, also provide any affected holder of or applicant
  for a licence with notice of the suspension in writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of security clearance

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must reinstate a suspended security clearance if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for the suspension no longer exist or
    the holder of the security clearance demonstrates to the Minister
    that the suspension was unfounded; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister determines, taking into account any
    licence conditions that he or she imposes under subsection 62(10) of
    the Act, that the holder of the security clearance does not pose an
    unacceptable risk to public health or public safety, including the
    risk of cannabis being diverted to an illicit market or activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The factors that the Minister may consider to
  determine the level of risk posed by the holder of the security
  clearance include those set out in subsection 53(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of reinstatement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, without delay after reinstating a
  security clearance, provide the holder of the security clearance, and
  any affected holder of or applicant for a licence, with notice of the
  reinstatement in writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of security clearance

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  security clearance may not be cancelled unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is suspended and the period within which the
    holder may make representations in respect of the suspension has
    expired; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has determined that the holder poses
    an unacceptable risk to public health or public safety, including
    the risk of cannabis being diverted to an illicit market or
    activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The factors that the Minister may consider to
  determine the level of risk posed by the holder include those set out
  in subsection 53(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of cancellation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, without delay after cancelling a
  security clearance, provide the holder, and any affected holder of or
  applicant for a licence, with notice of the cancellation in writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineligibility --- new application

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister refuses
to grant or cancels a security clearance, the individual who has been
refused a security clearance or the former holder may submit a new
application for a security clearance only if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a period of five years has elapsed since the day on
  which the refusal or cancellation occurred; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change has occurred in the circumstances that led
  to the refusal or cancellation.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Physical Security Measures]{.HTitleText1} {#h-848277 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Licences Requiring Increased Security Measures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848278 .Subheading}

#### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848279 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security measures

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  security measures set out in sections 63 to 72 apply in respect of the
  sites set out in the following licences:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence for standard cultivation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a licence for standard processing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a licence for sale that authorizes the possession
    of cannabis; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis drug licence if the amount of cannabis
    that is sold or distributed to the holder of the licence is, as
    determined in accordance with the table to section 21, equivalent to
    more than 600 kg of dried cannabis per calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with security measures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence referred to in subsection (1)
  must ensure that the security measures are complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalency

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The cannabis referred to in paragraph (1)(d) is
  exempt from the application of subsection 2(4) of the Act and a
  quantity referred to in column 2 of the table to section 21 in respect
  of any class of cannabis referred to in column 1 is deemed to be
  equivalent to 1 kg of dried cannabis.

#### [Site Design]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848291 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Site design

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** The site must be designed
in a manner that prevents unauthorized access.

#### [Perimeter of Site]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848294 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visual monitoring

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  perimeter of the site must be monitored at all times by visual
  recording devices to detect any attempted or actual unauthorized
  access to the site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visual recording devices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The devices must, in the conditions under which they
  are used, be capable of making a visible recording of any attempted or
  actual unauthorized access.

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43,
s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 65"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43,
s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 66"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Operations Areas and Storage Areas]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848311 .Subheading}

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43,
s. 18\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restricted access

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Access
  to each operations area and storage area must be restricted to
  individuals whose presence in the area is required by their duties.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 19](#1514879-1523894){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514879-1523894.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 68"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Physical barrier

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** Each operations area and
storage area must be surrounded by a physical barrier that prevents
unauthorized access.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visual monitoring

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  operations area and storage area must be monitored at all times by
  visual recording devices to detect illicit conduct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- grow area

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For a grow area, only the entry and exit points of
  the area must be monitored by the devices.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visual recording devices

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The devices must, in the conditions under which they
  are used, be capable of making a visible recording of any illicit
  conduct.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Intrusion detection system

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  operations area and storage area must be secured by means of an
  intrusion detection system that operates at all times and that allows
  for the detection of any attempted or actual unauthorized access to
  the area, any unauthorized movement in the area and any attempted or
  actual tampering with the system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- grow area

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The intrusion detection system is not required to
  detect unauthorized movement in a grow area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Monitoring and response

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  intrusion detection system referred to in section 71 must be monitored
  at all times.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appropriate measures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence must determine the
  appropriate measures to be taken if any occurrence referred to in
  section 70 or 71 is detected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of detected occurrences

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If any such occurrence is detected, the holder of the
  licence must ensure that a document is retained that contains the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date and time of the occurrence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the measures taken in response to it and the date
    and time when they were taken.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
referred to in subsection 62(1) must retain

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following visual recordings made under section 64
  or 70 for at least one year after the day on which they are made:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if they are made using a visual recording device
    that is activated by motion, the visual recordings depicting
    movements only, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they are made using a visual recording device
    that does not have this feature, all of those recordings; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the document referred to in subsection 72(3) for at
  least two years after the day on which it is prepared.

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 20](#1514880-1523901){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514880-1523901.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Other Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848347 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Micro-cultivation, micro-processing and
nurseries

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence for
micro-cultivation, micro-processing or a nursery must ensure that the
following security measures are complied with in respect of the site set
out in the licence:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the site is designed in a manner that prevents
  unauthorized access;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the site is surrounded by a physical barrier that
  prevents unauthorized access;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} storage areas are surrounded by a physical barrier
  that prevents unauthorized access; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} access to each storage area is restricted to
  individuals whose presence in the area is required by their duties.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Analytical testing

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence for
analytical testing must ensure that the following security measures are
complied with in respect of the site set out in the licence:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} storage areas are surrounded by a physical barrier
  that prevents unauthorized access; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} access to each storage area is restricted to
  individuals whose presence in the area is required by their duties.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis drug licences

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  amount of cannabis that is sold or distributed to the holder of a
  cannabis drug licence is not equivalent, as determined in accordance
  with the table to section 21, to more than 600 kg of dried cannabis
  per calendar year, the holder must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they only conduct analytical testing, the
    security measures set out in paragraphs 75(a) and (b) are complied
    with in respect of the site set out in the licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the security measures set out in
    paragraphs 74(a) to (d) are complied with in respect of the site set
    out in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cannabis referred to in subsection (1) is exempt
  from the application of subsection 2(4) of the Act and a quantity
  referred to in column 2 of the table to section 21 in respect of any
  class of cannabis referred to in column 1 is deemed to be equivalent
  to 1 kg of dried cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Research

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence for
research must ensure that operations areas at the site set out in the
licence are designed in a manner that prevents unauthorized access.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Exemptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848367 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions --- operations area

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence referred to in subsection 62(1) is exempt from the
  application of subsections 70(1), 71(1) and 72(1) in respect of any
  operations area if no activities conducted under the licence are in
  progress in the operations area and no cannabis is present in the
  operations area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- cannabis drug licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a cannabis drug licence is exempt from
  the application of this Part in respect of the site set out in the
  licence if they are securely storing cannabis in accordance with the
  Directive on Physical Security Requirements for Controlled Substances
  and Drugs Containing Cannabis, as amended from time to time and
  published by the Government of Canada on its website.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 21](#1514882-1523908){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514882-1523908.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Good Production Practices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-848392 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848393 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[78.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78.1 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[acceptable level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[acceptable level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a level of a
    biological, chemical or physical hazard that does not present a risk
    of contamination of cannabis or anything that will be used as an
    ingredient. ([niveau acceptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[control measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[control measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a measure that can be
    applied to prevent or eliminate any biological, chemical or physical
    hazard that presents a risk of contamination of cannabis or anything
    that will be used as an ingredient, or to reduce the hazard to an
    acceptable level. ([mesure de contrôle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[critical control point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[critical control point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a step at which
    the application of a control measure is essential to prevent or
    eliminate any biological, chemical or physical hazard that presents
    a risk of contamination of cannabis or anything that will be used as
    an ingredient, or to reduce the hazard to an acceptable
    level. ([point de contrôle critique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sanitary condition]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sanitary condition]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a condition that
    does not present a risk of contamination, allergen
    cross-contamination or introduction of an extraneous substance to
    cannabis or anything that will be used as an
    ingredient. ([conditions hygiéniques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 20](#1143304-1203501){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143304-1203501.html"}
:::

### [General Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1203502 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, distribution and exportation ---
cannabis

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must not sell, distribute or export cannabis unless the applicable
requirements set out in sections 80 to 88.94 have been met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 20](#1143304-1203506){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143304-1203506.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 79"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- person not holding a
licence

**[[79.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.1 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
this Part do not apply to any activity that a person conducts in respect
of anything that will be used as an ingredient unless the activity is
conducted by a holder of a licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 20](#1143304-1203510){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143304-1203510.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- holder of licence for
analytical testing or research

**[[79.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.2 .sectionLabel}** Sections 80 to 87.1
do not apply to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for analytical testing; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for research, except in respect
  of non-therapeutic research on cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 20](#1143304-1203514){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143304-1203514.html"}
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 14](#1381775-1382951){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381775-1382951.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79.2-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 79.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- reference standard

**[[79.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.3 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
this Part do not apply in respect of a reference standard and anything
that will be used as an ingredient in the production of a reference
standard.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 14](#1381775-1382955){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381775-1382955.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standard operating procedures

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** Cannabis and anything
that will be used as an ingredient must be produced, packaged, labelled,
distributed, stored, sampled and tested in accordance with standard
operating procedures that are designed to ensure that those activities
are conducted in accordance with the applicable requirements of this
Part and Part 6.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 20](#1143304-1203518){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143304-1203518.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pest control product

- **[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis must not be treated with a
  pest control product unless the product is registered for use on
  cannabis under the [Pest Control Products Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01) or is
  otherwise authorized for use under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- edible cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), edible cannabis may be
  treated during the course of production with a pest control product
  referred to in subparagraph 3(1)(b)(ii) of the [Pest Control Products
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1253).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 21](#1143306-1203525){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143306-1203525.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-81-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 81"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sanitizers, agronomic inputs and non-food
chemical agents

**[[81.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81.1 .sectionLabel}** Any sanitizer,
agronomic input or non-food chemical agent that is present at a site
must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be properly and clearly identified;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be suitable for its intended use and not present a
  risk of contamination of cannabis or anything that will be used as an
  ingredient; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be handled and used in a manner that does not present
  a risk of contamination of cannabis or anything that will be used as
  an ingredient and that is in accordance with the manufacturer's
  instructions.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203532){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203532.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** Cannabis and anything
that will be used as an ingredient must be stored under conditions that
maintain their quality.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203536){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203536.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-82-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 82"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** Cannabis and anything
that will be used as an ingredient must be distributed in a manner that
maintains their quality.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203540){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203540.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-83-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 83"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Building or part of building

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** Any building or part of a
building where cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient
is produced, packaged, labelled, stored or tested must be designed,
constructed and maintained in a manner that permits those activities to
be conducted appropriately and under sanitary conditions and, in
particular, that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} permits the building or part of the building to be
  kept clean and orderly;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} permits the effective cleaning of all surfaces in the
  building or part of the building;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prevents the contamination of the cannabis or thing
  that will be used as an ingredient; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prevents the introduction of an extraneous substance
  to the cannabis or thing that will be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203548){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203548.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-84-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 84"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}System --- filtration and ventilation

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  building or part of a building where cannabis or anything that will be
  used as an ingredient is produced, packaged, labelled, stored or
  tested must be equipped with a system that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} filters air to prevent the escape of odours
    associated with cannabis plant material to the outdoors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provides natural or mechanical ventilation with
    sufficient air exchange to provide clean air and to remove unclean
    air in order to prevent the contamination of the cannabis or thing
    that will be used as an ingredient;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is accessible and, if necessary for its cleaning,
    maintenance or inspection, is capable of being disassembled;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is capable of withstanding repeated cleaning; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} functions in accordance with its intended use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- cultivation, propagation
  or harvesting of cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply in respect of any
  building or part of a building where the only activities being
  conducted in respect of cannabis are its cultivation, propagation or
  harvesting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- cultivation, propagation
  or harvesting of anything used as an ingredient

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) to (e) do not apply in respect of
  any building or part of a building where the only activities being
  conducted in respect of anything that will be used as an ingredient
  are its cultivation, propagation or harvesting.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203562){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203562.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-85-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 85"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supply of water

- **[[85.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any system that supplies water to a
  site must be appropriate for any activity being conducted in respect
  of cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cross-connection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any system that supplies potable water to a site must
  not be cross-connected with any other system, unless measures are
  taken to eliminate any risk of contamination of cannabis or anything
  that will be used as an ingredient as a result of the
  cross-connection.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203569){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203569.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lighting

- **[[85.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any building or part of a building
  where cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient is
  produced, packaged, labelled, stored or tested must be equipped with
  natural or artificial lighting that is appropriate for the activity
  being conducted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Light fixtures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any light fixtures in the building or part of the
  building where the activities referred to in subsection (1) are
  conducted must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and,
    if necessary to prevent contamination of the cannabis or thing that
    will be used as an ingredient, repeated sanitizing; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not present a risk of contamination of the cannabis
    or thing that will be used as an ingredient in the event of
    breakage.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 22](#1143308-1203578){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143308-1203578.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equipment

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis and anything that will be
  used as an ingredient must be produced, packaged, labelled, stored,
  sampled and tested using equipment that is designed, constructed,
  maintained, operated and arranged in a manner that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} permits the effective cleaning of its surfaces;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} permits it to function in accordance with its
    intended use;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} is accessible and, if necessary for its cleaning,
    maintenance or inspection, is capable of being easily disassembled;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prevents the contamination of the cannabis or thing
    that will be used as an ingredient;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prevents the introduction of an extraneous
    substance to the cannabis or thing that will be used as an
    ingredient; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} protects the cannabis or thing that will be used as
    an ingredient against allergen cross-contamination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conveyances

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis and anything that will be used as an
  ingredient must be distributed using a conveyance that is designed,
  constructed, maintained and operated in a manner that prevents the
  contamination of the cannabis or thing that will be used as an
  ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(d) and (e) do not apply to the outdoor
  cultivation, propagation or harvesting of cannabis or anything that
  will be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 23](#1143310-1203585){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143310-1203585.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-86-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 86"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sanitation program

- **[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis and anything that will be
  used as an ingredient must be produced, packaged, labelled,
