  distributed, stored, sampled and tested in accordance with a
  sanitation program that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} procedures for effectively cleaning the building or
    part of the building in which those activities are conducted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} procedures for effectively cleaning the equipment
    and conveyances used in those activities;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} procedures for handling any substance used in those
    activities; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all requirements, in respect of the health and
    hygienic behaviour of the personnel who are involved in those
    activities, that are necessary to ensure that those activities are
    conducted in sanitary conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply to the outdoor
  cultivation, propagation or harvesting of cannabis or anything that
  will be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 24](#1143317-1203595){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143317-1203595.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-87-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 87"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hand cleaning and hand sanitizing
stations and lavatories

- **[[87.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  necessary to prevent the contamination of cannabis or anything that
  will be used as an ingredient, a site must be equipped with hand
  cleaning and hand sanitizing stations and lavatories that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are appropriately equipped and adequate in number
    and size for the number of individuals using them;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are located so that they are readily accessible to
    the individuals using them; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} are capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and,
    as necessary, repeated sanitizing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hand cleaning and hand sanitizing
  stations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The hand cleaning and hand sanitizing stations must
  permit the effective cleaning and sanitization of hands.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lavatories

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The lavatories must be located and maintained so that
  they do not present any risk of contamination of cannabis or anything
  that will be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203607){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203607.html"}
:::

### [Additional Requirements --- Holder of Licence for Processing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1203608 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quality assurance

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every investigation in respect of the matters
    referred to in paragraphs 19(2)(b) and (c) is conducted under the
    responsibility of the quality assurance person referred to in
    section 19;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if necessary following an investigation, the
    quality assurance person immediately causes measures to be taken to
    mitigate any risk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} cannabis and anything that will be used as an
    ingredient are produced, packaged, labelled, distributed, stored,
    sampled and tested using methods and procedures that, prior to their
    implementation, have been approved by the quality assurance person;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis extract or edible
    cannabis, the quality assurance person approves the preventive
    control plan referred to in section 88.94 prior to its
    implementation; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} every lot or batch of cannabis is approved by the
    quality assurance person before it is made available for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Activities under the responsibility of
  quality assurance person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The activities assigned to the quality assurance
  person under paragraphs (1)(b) to (e) can also be conducted under the
  responsibility of that person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203617){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203617.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 22](#1514884-1523922){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514884-1523922.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-88-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 88"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Competencies and qualifications

**[[88.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.1 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for processing must ensure that any individual who conducts activities
in relation to edible cannabis or anything that will be used as an
ingredient in the production of edible cannabis has the competencies and
qualifications that are necessary to conduct those activities at the
site set out in the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203621){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203621.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temperature and humidity

- **[[88.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must ensure that the temperature
  and humidity of any building or part of a building where cannabis or
  anything that will be used as an ingredient is produced, packaged,
  labelled, stored or tested are maintained at levels that are
  appropriate for the activity being conducted with the cannabis or
  thing that will be used as an ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heating, cooling or humidity-control
  system

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the building or part of the building is equipped
  with a heating, cooling or humidity-control system, the holder of the
  licence must ensure that the system

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if necessary to prevent contamination of the
    cannabis or thing that will be used as an ingredient, is equipped
    with instruments to control and indicate the temperature and
    humidity levels;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is accessible and, if necessary for its cleaning,
    maintenance or inspection, is capable of being disassembled;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is capable of withstanding repeated cleaning; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} functions in accordance with its intended use.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203632){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203632.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incompatible activities

- **[[88.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must ensure that physical or other
  effective means are used to separate incompatible activities in order
  to prevent contamination of cannabis or anything that will be used as
  an ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of food

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing must not
  produce, package, label or store cannabis at a site set out in the
  licence if food that is to be sold is also produced, packaged or
  labelled at that site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), a holder of a licence for
  processing may produce, package, label or store cannabis in a building
  within a site where food that is to be sold is produced, packaged or
  labelled if the food is not produced, packaged or labelled in the same
  building.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203641){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203641.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separation of cannabis and ingredients
from contaminants

**[[88.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.4 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for processing must ensure that physical or other effective means are
used to separate cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient
from anything that presents a risk of contamination of the cannabis or
thing that will be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203645){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203645.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredients --- risk of injury to human
health

**[[88.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.5 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for processing must ensure that anything that will be, or was intended
to be, used as an ingredient that presents a risk of injury to human
health is identified as such and is stored in a designated area within
the site.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203649){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203649.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Potable water

- **[[88.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must ensure that any water that
  might come into contact with a cannabis extract, a cannabis topical,
  edible cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient is
  potable and, if the water is not potable, must ensure that it does not
  present a risk of contamination of the cannabis extract, cannabis
  topical, edible cannabis or thing that will be used as an ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Steam and ice from potable water

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing must ensure that
  any steam or ice that might come into contact with a cannabis extract,
  a cannabis topical, edible cannabis or anything that will be used as
  an ingredient is made from water that meets the requirements of
  subsection (1) and, if the steam or ice does not meet those
  requirements, must ensure that it does not present a risk of
  contamination of the cannabis extract, cannabis topical, edible
  cannabis or thing that will be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203656){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203656.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No presence of animals

**[[88.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.7 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for processing must ensure that no animal is present in any building or
part of a building where cannabis or anything that will be used as an
ingredient is produced, packaged, labelled or stored.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203660){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203660.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Land --- risk of contamination

**[[88.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.8 .sectionLabel}** If any land that
forms part of a site set out in a licence for processing, or any land
that is located near such a site, presents a risk of contamination of
cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient, the holder of
the licence must take measures to eliminate the risk.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203664){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203664.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal and disposal of contaminated
materials and waste

- **[[88.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must ensure that any building or
  part of a building where cannabis or anything that will be used as an
  ingredient is produced, packaged, labelled or stored has means for the
  removal and disposal of contaminated materials and waste and, if
  necessary to prevent contamination of the cannabis or thing that will
  be used as an ingredient, that the building or part of the building is
  equipped with a drainage, sewage and plumbing system that functions in
  accordance with its intended use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Frequency and manner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence must ensure that
  contaminated materials and waste are removed and disposed of at a
  frequency that is sufficient to prevent contamination of the cannabis
  or thing that will be used as an ingredient and in a manner that does
  not present a risk of contamination of the cannabis or thing that will
  be used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203671){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203671.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conveyances and equipment

**[[88.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.91 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a
licence for processing must ensure that any conveyance or equipment that
is used at the site set out in the licence to handle any contaminated
materials or any waste, unless that conveyance or equipment does not
come into contact with those materials or waste,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is used only for that purpose;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is identified as being reserved for that purpose; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} meets the applicable requirements of section 86.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203678){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203678.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clothing, footwear and protective
coverings

**[[88.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.92 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a
licence for processing must ensure that any individual who enters or is
in any building or part of a building where cannabis or anything that
will be used as an ingredient is produced, packaged, labelled, stored,
sampled or tested wears clothing, footwear and protective coverings,
including gloves, a hairnet, a beard net and a smock, that are in good
condition, clean and in sanitary condition and that are appropriate for
the activity being conducted with the cannabis or thing that will be
used as an ingredient.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203682){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203682.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification and analysis of hazards

- **[[88.93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.93
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing
  that produces a cannabis extract or edible cannabis must identify and
  analyze the biological, chemical and physical hazards that present a
  risk of contamination of the cannabis or anything that will be used as
  an ingredient in the production of the cannabis extract or edible
  cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prevention, elimination and reduction
  of hazards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence must prevent, eliminate or
  reduce to an acceptable level the hazards referred to in
  subsection (1) by using control measures that are shown by evidence to
  be effective, including any treatment or process.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203689){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203689.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preventive control plan

- **[[88.94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.94
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing
  that conducts activities in relation to a cannabis extract or edible
  cannabis must prepare, retain, maintain and implement a written
  preventive control plan for any activity they conduct in respect of
  the cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient in the
  production of the cannabis extract or edible cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of preventive control plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The preventive control plan must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures for ensuring that the
    applicable requirements of sections 101.3, 101.4, 102, 102.2, 102.3,
    102.5 and 102.6 are met;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to the applicable requirements of these
    Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the biological, chemical and
      physical hazards that are identified under subsection 88.93(1)
      that present a risk of contamination of the cannabis extract,
      edible cannabis or anything that will be used as an ingredient in
      the production of the cannabis extract or edible cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the control measures for
      preventing, eliminating or reducing to an acceptable level the
      hazards referred to in subparagraph (i) and the evidence that the
      control measures are effective,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the critical control points,
      the related control measures and the evidence that the control
      measures are effective,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a description of the critical limits for each
      critical control point,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for monitoring the critical
      control points in relation to their critical limits,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the corrective action procedures for each
      critical control point,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for verifying that the
      implementation of the preventive control plan results in
      compliance with these Regulations, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} documents that substantiate that the
      preventive control plan has been implemented with respect to
      subparagraphs (i) to (vii); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} supporting documents that show evidence of the
    information recorded under paragraph (a) and subparagraphs (b)(i) to
    (vii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each document referred to in subparagraph
  (2)(b)(viii) must be retained for at least two years after the day on
  which it is prepared.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 25](#1143324-1203709){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143324-1203709.html"}
:::

### [Testing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848440 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale and exportation --- cannabis product

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must not sell or export a cannabis product unless the applicable
requirements set out in sections 90 to 92 have been met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Testing for phytocannabinoids

- **[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Testing for the quantity or
  concentration, as the case may be, of THC, THCA, CBD and CBDA must be
  conducted on each lot or batch of cannabis, other than cannabis plants
  or cannabis plant seeds, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is or will become a cannabis product; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is or will be contained in a cannabis accessory
    that is or will become a cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of testing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The testing must be conducted on the final form of
  the cannabis, either before or after it --- or the cannabis accessory
  that contains it --- is packaged and labelled as a cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 26](#1143326-1203718){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143326-1203718.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Testing for contaminants

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Testing for microbial and chemical
  contaminants --- other than residues of a pest control product or its
  components or derivatives --- must be conducted on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each lot or batch of cannabis --- other than
    cannabis plants, cannabis plant seeds or edible cannabis --- that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is or will become a cannabis product, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is or will be contained in a cannabis accessory
      that is or will become a cannabis product; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each lot or batch of cannabis --- other than
    cannabis plant seeds --- that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is used to produce the cannabis referred to in
      paragraph (a), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is used to produce edible cannabis that is or
      will become a cannabis product, or that is or will be contained in
      a cannabis accessory that is or will become a cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of testing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The testing on a lot or batch of cannabis must be
  conducted as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the testing referred to in paragraph (1)(a) must be
    conducted on the final form of the cannabis, either before or after
    it --- or the cannabis accessory that contains it --- is packaged
    and labelled as a cannabis product; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the testing referred to in paragraph (1)(b) must be
    conducted after the final step in the production process during
    which the contaminants referred to in subsection (1) could have been
    introduced or could be concentrated, whichever is later.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tolerance limits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The results of the testing referred to in
  subsection (1) must enable a determination of whether the
  contaminants, if any, are or will be within the tolerance limits
  referred to in subsection 93(3) or 94(2) or section 101.1, as the case
  may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 26](#1143326-1203735){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143326-1203735.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 91"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution and disintegration testing

- **[[91.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that contains cannabis --- is or
  will become a cannabis product to which subsection 95(1) applies,
  testing must be conducted on each lot or batch of the cannabis or
  cannabis accessory to determine whether the requirements referred to
  in that subsection are, or will be, met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of testing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The testing must be conducted on the final form of
  the cannabis, either before or after it --- or the cannabis accessory
  that contains it --- is packaged and labelled as a cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 26](#1143326-1203742){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143326-1203742.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Testing method

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Testing that is conducted under
  sections 90 to 91.1 --- or to determine whether the applicable
  requirements in Part 6 are, or will be, met --- must be conducted
  using validated methods on a representative sample of each lot or
  batch of cannabis or cannabis accessory that contains cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A portion of the sample referred to in subsection (1)
  must be retained for at least one year after the date of the last sale
  of any portion of the lot or batch.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sufficient quantity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The portion of the sample retained under
  subsection (2) must be of sufficient quantity to enable a
  determination of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the lot or batch meets the requirements of
    section 81, subsection 93(3), 94(2) or 95(1) or section 101.1, as
    applicable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the quantity or concentration of THC, THCA, CBD and
    CBDA.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 26](#1143326-1203753){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143326-1203753.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-92-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 92"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Cannabis Products]{.HTitleText1} {#h-848458 .Part}

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848459 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- residues of pest
control products

**[[92.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.1 .sectionLabel}** In this Part, a
reference to residues of a pest control product includes the residues of
any component or derivative of the pest control product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203757){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203757.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Residues of pest control products ---
cannabis plants and seeds

**[[92.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.2 .sectionLabel}** Cannabis plants or
cannabis plant seeds that are cannabis products --- or that are
contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product --- must
not contain or have on them residues of a pest control product that is
registered for use on cannabis under the [Pest Control Products
Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01), or that is otherwise authorized for use under
that Act, unless the residues are within any maximum residue limits that
are specified in relation to cannabis under section 9 or 10 of that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203761){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203761.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dried and fresh cannabis

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Dried
  cannabis or fresh cannabis that is a cannabis product --- or that is
  contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product --- must
  not contain or have on it anything other than anything referred to in
  item 1 of Schedule 1 to the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Residues of pest control products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), cannabis that is referred to
  in that subsection may contain or have on it residues of a pest
  control product that is registered for use on cannabis under the [Pest
  Control Products Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01), or that is otherwise
  authorized for use under that Act, if the residues are within any
  maximum residue limits that are specified in relation to cannabis
  under section 9 or 10 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Microbial and chemical contaminants

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), cannabis that is referred to
  in that subsection may contain or have on it microbial or chemical
  contaminants if the contaminants are within generally accepted
  tolerance limits for human use that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} established in a publication referred to in
    Schedule B to the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appropriate for the intended use and any reasonably
    foreseeable use of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}More stringent limit applies

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If there are generally accepted tolerance limits
  referred to in subsection (3) that apply in respect of the residues of
  a pest control product referred to in subsection (2) for which a
  maximum residue limit has been specified in relation to cannabis under
  the [Pest Control Products Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01), the more stringent
  limit applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203774){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203774.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-93-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 93"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis used in production

- **[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis that is referred to in item
  1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act and that is used in the production of
  the following cannabis must not contain or have on it residues of a
  pest control product that is registered for use on cannabis under the
  [Pest Control Products Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01), or that is otherwise
  authorized for use under that Act, unless the residues are within any
  maximum residue limits that are specified in relation to cannabis
  under section 9 or 10 of that Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis extract that will become a cannabis
    product or that will be contained in a cannabis accessory that will
    become a cannabis product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis topical that will become a cannabis
    product or that will be contained in a cannabis accessory that will
    become a cannabis product; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} edible cannabis that will become a cannabis product
    or that will be contained in a cannabis accessory that will become a
    cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Edible cannabis --- microbial and
  chemical contaminants

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Cannabis that is referred to in item 1 or 3 of
  Schedule 1 to the Act and that is used in the production of edible
  cannabis must not, if the edible cannabis will become a cannabis
  product or will be contained in a cannabis accessory that will become
  a cannabis product, contain or have on it microbial or chemical
  contaminants unless the contaminants are within generally accepted
  tolerance limits for human use that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} established in a publication referred to in
    Schedule B to the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appropriate for a product that is to be ingested.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203786){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203786.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-94-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 94"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution and disintegration

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  discrete unit of a cannabis product that is intended for ingestion or
  nasal, rectal or vaginal use must meet, if the form of the unit is
  similar to a dosage form for which a dissolution or disintegration
  test is set out in a publication referred to in Schedule B to the
  [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27), the requirements of the test or,
  if there is more than one applicable test, the requirements of any
  such test that is suitable for demonstrating that the cannabis product
  will perform as intended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to edible cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203793){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203793.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-95-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 95"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC --- discrete unit

- **[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 97(1), each
  discrete unit of a cannabis product that is intended for ingestion or
  nasal, rectal or vaginal use must not contain a quantity of THC that
  exceeds 10 mg, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into
  THC.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to edible cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203800){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203800.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-96-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 96"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variability limits

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cannabis extract, or a cannabis topical, that is a cannabis product
  --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis
  product --- must not contain, in respect of any quantity or
  concentration of THC or CBD that is displayed on the label, less than
  85% or more than 115% of that quantity or concentration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Edible cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Edible cannabis that is a cannabis product --- or
  that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product
  --- must not contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a quantity of THC or CBD that is displayed on
    the label exceeds 5 mg, less than 85% or more than 115% of that
    quantity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a quantity of THC or CBD that is displayed on
    the label exceeds 2 mg but does not exceed 5 mg, less than 80% or
    more than 120% of that quantity; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a quantity of THC or CBD that is displayed on
    the label does not exceed 2 mg, less than 75% or more than 125% of
    that quantity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203810){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203810.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-97-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 97"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variability limits --- divisible cannabis
products

- **[[97.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a cannabis product that is not in discrete units is represented as
  being able to be divided into discrete units, each represented unit
  must not contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of THC that is less than 75% or more
    than 125% of the quantity of THC in each of the other represented
    units, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into THC;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of CBD that is less than 75% or more
    than 125% of the quantity of CBD in each of the other represented
    units, taking into account the potential to convert CBDA into CBD.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Divisible units

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a cannabis product is in discrete units that are
  represented as being able to be divided into discrete sub-units, each
  represented subunit must not contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of THC that is less than 75% or more
    than 125% of the quantity of THC in each of the other represented
    subunits, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into
    THC; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of CBD that is less than 75% or more
    than 125% of the quantity of CBD in each of the other represented
    subunits, taking into account the potential to convert CBDA into
    CBD.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203821){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203821.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Products that must not be sold or
distributed

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** The following cannabis
products must not be sold or distributed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product that is intended to be used in the
  area of the human eye bounded by the supraorbital and infraorbital
  ridges, including the eyebrows, the skin underlying the eyebrows, the
  eyelids, the eyelashes, the conjunctival sac of the eye, the eyeball
  and the soft tissue that lies below the eye and within the
  infraorbital ridge; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product that is intended to be used on
  damaged or broken skin or to penetrate the skin barrier other than by
  absorption.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203827){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203827.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-98-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 98"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple units

**[[98.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98.1 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for
a holder of a licence to sell or distribute a cannabis extract, a
cannabis topical or edible cannabis that is a cannabis product --- or
that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product ---
if the immediate container contains multiple discrete units, unless the
properties of each unit, including size but excluding flavour and
colour, as applicable, are consistent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 27](#1143328-1203831){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143328-1203831.html"}
:::

### [Dried and Fresh Cannabis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848482 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition of THC or THCA

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** THC or THCA must not be
added to dried or fresh cannabis that will become a cannabis product or
that is, or will be, contained in a cannabis accessory that will become
a cannabis product.

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2025-43, s. 23\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-100-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 100"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cannabis Extracts and Cannabis Topicals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848487 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Things injurious to health

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cannabis extract, or a cannabis topical, that is a cannabis product
  --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis
  product --- must not contain or have on it anything that may cause
  injury to the health of the user when the cannabis product is used as
  intended or in a reasonably foreseeable way.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not, in respect of a cannabis
  extract that is intended to be combusted and inhaled, prohibit
  anything that may cause injury as a result of the intended combustion
  and inhalation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Things that do not cause injury

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), a cannabis
  extract or a cannabis topical does not contain or have on it anything
  that may cause injury to the health of the user by reason only that it
  contains or has on it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} anything referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1
    to the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} residues of a pest control product that is
    registered for use on cannabis under the [Pest Control Products
    Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01), or that is otherwise authorized for use
    under that Act, if the residues are within any maximum residue
    limits that are specified in relation to cannabis under section 9 or
    10 of that Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} microbial or chemical contaminants --- other than
    residues of a pest control product referred to in paragraph (b) ---
    if the contaminants are within generally accepted tolerance limits
    for human use that are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} established in a publication referred to in
      Schedule B to the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} appropriate for the intended use and any
      reasonably foreseeable use of the cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203846){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203846.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-101-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 101"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Microbial and chemical contaminants

**[[101.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.1 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis extract,
or a cannabis topical, that is a cannabis product --- or that is
contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product --- must
not contain or have on it microbial or chemical contaminants unless the
contaminants are within generally accepted tolerance limits for human
use that are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} established in a publication referred to in Schedule
  B to the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} appropriate for the intended use and any reasonably
  foreseeable use of the cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203852){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203852.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC

**[[101.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.2 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis extract,
or a cannabis topical, that is a cannabis product --- or that is
contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product --- must
not contain a quantity of THC that exceeds 1000 mg per immediate
container, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into THC.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203856){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203856.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis extract --- content

- **[[101.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A cannabis extract that is a
  cannabis product --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that
  is a cannabis product --- must not contain any ingredients other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} carrier substances;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} flavouring agents; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} substances that are necessary to maintain the
    quality or stability of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited ingredients

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following substances must not be used as
  ingredients to produce a cannabis extract referred to in subsection
  (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} substances that are listed in column 1 of the table
    in Schedule 2 to the [Tobacco and Vaping Products
    Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sugars or [[sweeteners]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or
    [[sweetening agents]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are
    defined in subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- vitamins

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 2(a), a vitamin may be used as an
  ingredient to maintain the quality or stability of the cannabis
  extract referred to in subsection (1) if it is used in an amount that
  does not exceed what is necessary to maintain the quality or stability
  of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Naturally occurring substances

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An ingredient that is used to produce the cannabis
  extract referred to in subsection (1) may contain a substance referred
  to in subsection (2) only if that substance is naturally present in
  the ingredient at a level that is not above the naturally occurring
  level for that ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted ingredients --- inhaled
  cannabis extract

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An ingredient --- other than a flavouring agent ---
  must not be used to produce a cannabis extract referred to in
  subsection (1) that is intended to be consumed by means of inhalation
  unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a standard for the ingredient is set out in a
    publication referred to in Schedule B to the [Food and Drugs
    Act](/eng/acts/F-27); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ingredient complies with the standard.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ethyl alcohol --- inhaled cannabis
  extract

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} A cannabis extract referred to in subsection (1)
  that is intended to be consumed by means of inhalation may contain the
  following quantities of ethyl alcohol if the net weight of the
  cannabis extract in each immediate container of the cannabis product
  does not exceed 7.5 g:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it is in discrete units, 10 mg or less per unit;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it is not in discrete units,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 10 mg or less, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} more than 10 mg, if the immediate container does
      not permit the extract to be easily removed from the container and
      contains an integrated mechanism that dispenses no more than 10 mg
      of ethyl alcohol per activation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ethyl alcohol --- ingested cannabis
  extract

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A cannabis extract referred to in subsection (1) that
  is intended to be ingested may contain ethyl alcohol if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight of the cannabis extract in each
    immediate container of the cannabis product does not exceed 7.5 g,
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the net weight of the cannabis
    extract in each immediate container of the cannabis product exceeds
    7.5 g, the concentration of ethyl alcohol does not exceed 0.5% w/w
    of the cannabis extract.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203880){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203880.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 24](#1514887-1523934){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514887-1523934.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-101.3-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 101.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis topical --- denatured ethyl
alcohol

**[[101.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.31 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis
topical that is a cannabis product or that is contained in a cannabis
accessory that is a cannabis product may contain denatured ethyl
alcohol.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 25](#1514889-1523938){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514889-1523938.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uniform distribution --- cannabinoids and
terpenes

**[[101.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.4 .sectionLabel}** The cannabinoids
and terpenes in a cannabis extract, or a cannabis topical, that is a
cannabis product --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that
is a cannabis product --- must be uniformly distributed throughout the
cannabis extract or cannabis topical.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203884){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203884.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis extract --- external body
surfaces

**[[101.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.5 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis extract
that is a cannabis product --- or that is contained in a cannabis
accessory that is a cannabis product --- must not be represented for
use, directly or indirectly, on external body surfaces, including hair
and nails.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203888){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203888.html"}
:::

### [Edible Cannabis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1203889 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredients --- edible cannabis

- **[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Edible cannabis that is a cannabis
  product --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a
  cannabis product --- must not contain any ingredients other than food
  and food additives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporarily marketed foods and
  supplemented foods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following foods must not be used as an ingredient
  to produce edible cannabis referred to in subsection (1) and must not
  be a constituent of such an ingredient:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food that is described in a Temporary Marketing
    Authorization Letter issued under subsection B.01.054(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a [[supplemented food]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    within the meaning of section B.01.001 of those Regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a food in respect of which the
    [[manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section
    A.01.010 of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870), is exempt from the
    application of those Regulations in accordance with section 32 or 33
    of the Regulations Amending the Food and Drug Regulations and the
    Cannabis Regulations (Supplemented Foods), as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meat products, poultry products and
  fish

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A meat product, poultry product or fish, other than a
  food additive, must not be used as an ingredient to produce edible
  cannabis referred to in subsection (1) --- and must not be a
  constituent of such an ingredient --- unless the meat product, poultry
  product or fish

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been produced by a person that is authorized to
    produce it under the laws of a province or the [Safe Food for
    Canadians Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1) or has been imported in accordance
    with that Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a water activity that does not exceed 0.85 at a
    temperature of 22 ± 2°C at the time the meat product, poultry
    product or fish is obtained by the holder of the licence for
    processing that is producing the edible cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Self-produced food

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing that produces a
  food may use it as an ingredient to produce edible cannabis referred
  to in subsection (1) --- or as a constituent of such an ingredient ---
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food is not a meat product, poultry product or
    fish; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sale of the food would not be prohibited under
    section 4 of the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food additives

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing may use a food
  additive as an ingredient to produce edible cannabis referred to in
  subsection (1) only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the edible cannabis would be a food that is set out
    in column 3 of the Lists of Permitted Food Additives, if the edible
    cannabis did not contain or have on it anything referred to in item
    1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food additive is set out in column 1 and is not
    caffeine or caffeine citrate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the food additive is obtained from any
    corresponding source set out in column 2;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the food additive is used for any corresponding
    purpose of use set out in column 4;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the food additive does not exceed

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any corresponding maximum level of use and any
      corresponding maximum level of residue set out in column 5, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount that is required to accomplish the
      purpose for which it is added, in the case where the words "Good
      Manufacturing Practice" appear in column 5 for that food additive
      in the food; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any other corresponding condition set out in column
    5 is met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vitamins and mineral nutrients

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A vitamin or mineral nutrient must not be used as an
  ingredient to produce edible cannabis referred to in subsection (1)
  unless its use is permitted under subsection (5).

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-251, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203921){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203921.html"}
- [SOR/2022-169, s. 30](#1371074-1371603){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1371074-1371603.html"}
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 15](#1381777-1382957){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381777-1382957.html"}
- [SOR/2024-244, s. 168](#1497520-1505120){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1497520-1505120.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-102-20221202.html "Link to previous version of section 102"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited things

- **[[102.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Edible cannabis that is a cannabis
  product --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a
  cannabis product --- must not contain or have on it anything in a
  quantity that would cause the sale of the edible cannabis to be
  prohibited under any of paragraphs 4(1)(a) to (d) of the [Food and
  Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27) if the edible cannabis were a food to which
  that Act applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not poisonous, harmful or adulterated

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Edible cannabis does not have a poisonous or harmful
  substance in or on it, within the meaning of paragraph 4(1)(a) of the
  [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27), and is not adulterated, within
  the meaning of paragraph 4(1)(d) of that Act, by reason only that it
  contains or has on it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} anything referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1
    to the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} residues of a pest control product that is
    registered for use on cannabis under the [Pest Control Products
    Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01), or is otherwise authorized for use under
    that Act, if the residues are within any maximum residue limits that
    are specified in relation to cannabis under section 9 or 10 of that
    Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} microbial or chemical contaminants --- other than
    residues of a pest control product referred to in paragraph (b) ---
    if the contaminants are within generally accepted tolerance limits
    for human use that are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} established in a publication referred to in
      Schedule B to the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} appropriate for a product that is to be
      ingested; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a food additive, if the conditions set out in
    subparagraphs 28.1(4)(c)(i) to (vi) or the requirements set out in
    paragraphs 102(5)(a) to (f), as the case may be, are met in respect
    of the use of the food additive as an ingredient to produce the
    edible cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203933){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203933.html"}
- [SOR/2024-244, s. 169](#1497522-1505124){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1497522-1505124.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-102.1-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 102.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Caffeine

**[[102.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.2 .sectionLabel}** Edible cannabis
that is a cannabis product --- or that is contained in a cannabis
accessory that is a cannabis product --- must not contain or have on it
caffeine unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the caffeine has been introduced through the use of
  ingredients that naturally contain caffeine; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total amount of caffeine in each immediate
  container of the cannabis product does not exceed 30 mg.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203939){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203939.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ethyl alcohol

**[[102.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.3 .sectionLabel}** Edible cannabis
that is a cannabis product --- or that is contained in a cannabis
accessory that is a cannabis product --- must not contain or have on it
ethyl alcohol unless the concentration of ethyl alcohol does not exceed
0.5% w/w of the edible cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203943){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203943.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis products requiring refrigeration

**[[102.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.4 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
for a holder of a licence to sell or distribute edible cannabis that is
a cannabis product --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that
is a cannabis product --- if the unopened immediate container must be
stored at or below 4°C to prevent the cannabis product from becoming
contaminated before its durable life date.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203947){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203947.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hermetically sealed containers

- **[[102.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a holder of a
  licence to sell or distribute edible cannabis that is a cannabis
  product --- or that is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a
  cannabis product --- in a hermetically sealed container if any
  component of the edible cannabis has a pH that exceeds 4.6 and a water
  activity that exceeds 0.85 at a temperature of 22 ± 2°C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in subsection (1).

  [[hermetically sealed container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hermetically sealed container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      container that, due to its design, is secure against the entry of
      micro-organisms, including spores. ([contenant hermétiquement
      scellé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[water activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[water activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the ratio of the
      water vapour pressure of the component to the vapour pressure of
      pure water, at the same temperature and pressure. ([activité de
      l'eau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203956){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203956.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Irradiation

**[[102.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.6 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a
licence for processing must not irradiate edible cannabis unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the edible cannabis would be a food that is listed in
  item 3 or 4, column 1, of the table to Division 26 of Part B of the
  [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) if the
  edible cannabis did not contain or have on it anything that is
  referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder satisfies the requirements set out in
  paragraphs B.26.003(2)(a) and (b) and subsection B.26.004(1) of those
  Regulations in respect of the edible cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203962){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203962.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC

**[[102.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.7 .sectionLabel}** Subject to
subsection 97(2), edible cannabis that is a cannabis product --- or that
is contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product --- must
not contain a quantity of THC that exceeds 10 mg per immediate
container, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into THC.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 28](#1143330-1203966){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143330-1203966.html"}
:::

**[[102.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.8 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2025-43, s. 26\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-102.8-20221202.html "Link to previous version of section 102.8"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cannabis Accessory or Component]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848492 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contamination

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis accessory
that is a cannabis product, or that is packaged with a cannabis product,
must not be contaminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1203970){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1203970.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-103-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 103"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flavour

**[[103.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103.1 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis
accessory that is a cannabis product, or that is packaged with a
cannabis product, must not impart a characterizing flavour to the
cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1203974){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1203974.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dispensing limit

**[[103.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103.2 .sectionLabel}** Subject to
subsection 97(1), each activation of the following cannabis accessories
must not dispense a quantity of cannabis extract that contains greater
than 10 mg of THC, taking into account the potential to convert THCA
into THC:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product and
  that dispenses a cannabis extract that is intended for ingestion or
  nasal, rectal or vaginal use; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis accessory that is packaged with, and that
  is intended to dispense, a cannabis extract that is a cannabis product
  and that is intended for ingestion or nasal, rectal or vaginal use.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1203980){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1203980.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Psychological effects, abuse liability
and toxicity

- **[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  component of a cannabis product --- other than a component that is
  anything referred to in item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the Act --- and a
  cannabis accessory that is packaged with a cannabis product must not,
  through any means other than heating or combustion, and when used as
  intended or in a reasonably foreseeable way,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} alter or enhance the psychological effects derived
    from the cannabis product in a manner that may cause injury to the
    health of the user;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} increase the potential for abuse liability of the
    cannabis product; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} increase the toxicity of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not prohibit the presence of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ethyl alcohol in or on a cannabis product referred
    to in subsections 101.3(5.1) or (6) or sections 101.31 or 102.3 if
    the conditions set out in those provisions, as the case may be, are
    met; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} caffeine in or on a cannabis product referred to in
    section 102.2 if the conditions set out in that section are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1203992){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1203992.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 27](#1514892-1523944){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514892-1523944.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-104-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 104"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 6.1]{.HLabel1}[Promotion]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1203993 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- prescription drug and
combination product

**[[104.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.1 .sectionLabel}** Sections 104.11 to
104.16 do not apply to a prescription drug or a combination product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1203997){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1203997.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flavours

**[[104.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.11 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote a cannabis extract --- or a cannabis accessory that contains
a cannabis extract --- under subsections 17(2) to (6) of the Act in a
manner that could cause a person to believe that the cannabis extract or
the cannabis accessory has a flavour set out in column 1 of Schedule 3
to the [Tobacco and Vaping Products Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5), other than
the flavour of cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204001){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204001.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health and cosmetic benefits

- **[[104.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited to promote
  cannabis, a cannabis accessory or a service related to cannabis under
  subsections 17(2) to (6) of the Act if there are reasonable grounds to
  believe that the promotion could create the impression that health or
  cosmetic benefits may be derived from the service or the use of the
  cannabis or the cannabis accessory.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- medical devices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to a
  medical device in respect of which a licence has been issued under
  subsection 36(1) of the [Medical Devices
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-282).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204008){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204008.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Energy value and amount of nutrient

- **[[104.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited to promote edible
  cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that contains edible cannabis ---
  under subsections 17(2) to (6) of the Act by communicating information
  about the energy value referred to in item 2 of the table to section
  132.22 or the amount of any nutrient referred to in items 3 to 15 of
  that table or in items 5 to 37 of the table to section B.01.402 of the
  [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- nutrition facts table

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), edible cannabis or a cannabis
  accessory that contains edible cannabis may be promoted by reproducing
  the nutrition facts table that is required to be included on the label
  of any container in which the edible cannabis or the cannabis
  accessory is packaged in accordance with these Regulations using
  smaller, larger or identical dimensions and spacing.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204015){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204015.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dietary requirements

**[[104.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.14 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote edible cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that contains
edible cannabis --- under subsections 17(2) to (6) of the Act if there
are reasonable grounds to believe that the promotion could create the
impression that the edible cannabis or accessory is intended

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to meet the particular dietary requirements of an
  individual

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} who has a physical or physiological condition as a
    result of a disease, disorder or injury, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for whom a particular effect, including weight
    loss, is to be obtained by a controlled intake of food; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to meet the dietary requirements of young persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204023){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204023.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alcoholic beverages

**[[104.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.15 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote cannabis, a cannabis accessory or a service related to
cannabis under subsections 17(2) to (6) of the Act if there are
reasonable grounds to believe that the promotion could associate the
cannabis, the cannabis accessory or the service with an alcoholic
beverage.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204027){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204027.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tobacco products and vaping products

**[[104.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.16 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote cannabis, a cannabis accessory or a service related to
cannabis under subsections 17(2) to (6) of the Act if there are
reasonable grounds to believe that the promotion could associate the
cannabis, the cannabis accessory or the service with a [[tobacco
product]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the [Tobacco
and Vaping Products Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5), or a vaping product to which
that Act applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204031){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204031.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place where young persons are not
permitted

**[[104.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.17 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote cannabis, a cannabis accessory or a service related to
cannabis under paragraphs 17(2)(b) and (3)(b) of the Act in such a
manner that the promotion may be audible or visible from outside a place
where young persons are not permitted by law.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204035){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204035.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of brand elements

**[[104.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.18 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote cannabis, a cannabis accessory or a service related to
cannabis under subsection 17(6) of the Act in a manner that results in
the same brand element being displayed more than once on a thing
referred to in that subsection or in more than one brand element being
displayed on the thing.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204039){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204039.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public place frequented mainly by young
persons

**[[104.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.19 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to promote cannabis, a cannabis accessory or a service related to
cannabis under subsection 17(6) of the Act by displaying a brand element
of cannabis, of a cannabis accessory or of a service related to cannabis
on any thing that is in a school, a public playground, a daycare
facility or any other public place frequented mainly by young persons or
that is visible from such a place.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204043){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204043.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dimensions of brand element

**[[104.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104.2 .sectionLabel}** A brand element
referred to in subsection 17(6) of the Act must meet the following
requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the surface area must be smaller than or equal to
  300 cm^2^; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the height of any letter, character or number must be
  smaller than or equal to 4 cm.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 29](#1143332-1204049){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143332-1204049.html"}
:::

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Packaging and Labelling]{.HTitleText1} {#h-848497 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848498 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of edible
      cannabis, has the same meaning as in subsection B.01.001(1) of the
      [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
      usuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Common Names for Ingredients and Components Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Common Names for Ingredients and Components
      Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document entitled *Common
      Names for Ingredients and Components*, prepared by the Canadian
      Food Inspection Agency and published on its website, as amended
      from time to time. ([Document sur les noms usuels d'ingrédients et
      de constituants]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[daily value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[daily value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a nutrient set out in column 1
        of Part 1 of the [[Table of Daily
        Values]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection
        B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870), the quantity set
        out in column 3; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a nutrient set out in column 1
        of Part 2 of the table referred to in paragraph (a), the
        quantity set out in column 4. ([valeur
        quotidienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[energy value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[energy value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
      cannabis product, the amount of energy made available to a
      person's body when the chemical components of the cannabis
      product, including protein, fat, carbohydrate and alcohol, are
      metabolized following ingestion of the cannabis product by the
      person. ([valeur énergétique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[expiry date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[expiry date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the date, expressed at
      minimum as a year and month, that is the end of the stability
      period of a cannabis product. ([date limite
      d'utilisation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[exterior display surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[exterior display surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area on
      the exterior surface of an immediate container to which a label is
      applied and that is visible under customary conditions of purchase
      or use. ([espace extérieur d'affichage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[exterior surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[exterior surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a label or an
      image. ([surfaces extérieures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      B.01.400(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([lipides]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[food allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[food allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([allergène
      alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[food allergen source, gluten source and added sulphites statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[food allergen source, gluten source and added sulphites
      statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a statement appearing on the
      label of any container in which edible cannabis --- or a cannabis
      accessory that contains edible cannabis --- that is a cannabis
      product is packaged that indicates the source of a food allergen
      or gluten that is present in the cannabis product or the presence
      in the cannabis product of added sulphites in an amount of
      10 p.p.m. or more. ([mention des sources d'allergènes alimentaires
      ou de gluten et des sulfites ajoutés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gluten]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gluten]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([gluten]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-206, s. 30\]]{.Repealed}

  [[INCI name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[INCI name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Cosmetic
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._869). ([appellation
      INCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include inserts or leaflets
      referred to in subsection 132(1), nor does it include a panel
      referred to in subsection 132.27(1). ([étiquette]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[p.p.m.]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[p.p.m.]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means parts per million by
      weight. ([p.p.m.]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[principal display panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[principal display panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 2(2) of the [Consumer Packaging and
      Labelling Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._417). ([espace
      principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[saturated fatty acids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[saturated fatty acids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[saturated
      fat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[saturates]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[saturated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([acides gras
      saturés, graisses saturées, gras saturés, lipides
      saturés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [saturés]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[standardized cannabis symbol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[standardized cannabis symbol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      symbol set out in the document entitled Standardized Cannabis
      Symbol, as amended from time to time and published by the
      Government of Canada on its website. ([symbole normalisé du
      cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sugars-based ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sugars-based ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([ingrédient à base
      de sucres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sulphites]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sulphites]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means one or more of the
      following food additives:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} potassium bisulphite;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} potassium metabisulphite;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sodium bisulphite;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sodium dithionite;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} sodium metabisulphite;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} sodium sulphite;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} sulphur dioxide; and

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} sulphurous acid. ([sulfites]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[trans fatty acids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trans fatty acids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[trans
      fat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[trans]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([acides gras
      trans]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}, [graisses
      trans]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}, [gras trans]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}, [lipides trans]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [trans]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of sections 112 to 117, 121 and
  132.13, subsections 132.27(2) to (7) and (9) and sections 132.28 to
  132.32, [[panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a panel referred to in
  subsection 132.27(1).

::: HistoricalNote

  ajax-after="1358386-1362615.html"}

  ajax-after="1514894-1523948.html"}
