### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848506 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement --- sale and distribution of
cannabis product

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must not sell or distribute a cannabis product
  unless the applicable requirements set out in sections 108 to 136 have
  been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement --- exportation of cannabis
  product

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence must not export a cannabis
  product unless the requirements set out in paragraph 123(1)(a) and
  subparagraphs 123(1)(c)(ii) and (iv) have been met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- shipping container

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** The requirements set
out in this Part do not apply in respect of a shipping container.

### [Packaging --- Cannabis Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848514 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immediate container

- **[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  immediate container in which a cannabis product, other than a cannabis
  plant or cannabis plant seeds, is packaged must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be opaque or translucent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prevent contamination of the cannabis;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis, or a cannabis
    accessory that contains dried cannabis, keep the cannabis dry;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have a security feature that provides reasonable
    assurance to consumers that it has not been opened prior to receipt;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of a child resistant package
    under subsections C.01.001(2) to (4) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} not contain more than the equivalent of 30 g of
    dried cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4) of
    the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- paragraph (1)(a)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(a), the immediate container in
  which dried cannabis or fresh cannabis is packaged may be transparent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 29](#1514899-1523967){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514899-1523967.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-108-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 108"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis plant --- not budding or
flowering

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cannabis plant must not be budding or flowering at the time of
  packaging.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis plant --- container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The container in which a cannabis plant is packaged
  must not contain more than four cannabis plants.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis plant seeds --- immediate
container

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** The immediate container
in which cannabis plant seeds are packaged must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep the cannabis plant seeds dry; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} not contain more than the equivalent of 30 g of dried
  cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4) of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Brand element

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, the interior surface and exterior
surface of any container in which a cannabis product is packaged must
not display any brand element.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Image

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided under the Act, any other Act of Parliament or any provincial
Act, the interior surface, exterior surface and panel of any container
in which a cannabis product is packaged must not display any image.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 31](#1143340-1204095){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143340-1204095.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-112-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 112"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uniform colour

- **[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided under
  the Act, any other Act of Parliament or any provincial Act, the colour
  of the interior surface, exterior surface and panel of any container
  in which a cannabis product is packaged must be one uniform colour.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the following may be
  different:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the colour of each surface and the panel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the colour of the container and the lid or cap of
    the container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Colour --- other requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The colour of the interior surface, exterior surface
  and panel must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must not have the lustre of metal or have
    metallic properties in the ink, such as Pantone Metallics or Pantone
    Premium Metallics;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must not be fluorescent, have fluorescent
    properties in the ink or have pigments that absorb ultraviolet
    energy and transmit it as a longer wavelength, such as the Pantone
    800 series; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must create a contrast with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the yellow colour of the background of the health
      warning message, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the red colour of the standardized cannabis
      symbol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an interior surface that is made of metal may be
    the colour of the metal; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an exterior surface of an immediate container that
    is made of metal, excluding the label or any image, may be the
    colour of the metal.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 32](#1143342-1204103){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143342-1204103.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 30](#1514901-1523973){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514901-1523973.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-113-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 113"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2019-206, s. 33\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-114-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 114"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Texture

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided under
  the Act, any other Act of Parliament or any provincial Act, the
  interior surface, exterior surface and panel of any container in which
  a cannabis product is packaged and any covering of such a container
  must have a smooth texture without any raised features, embossing,
  decorative ridges, bulges or other irregularities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the features of a
  container that are necessary to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} facilitate the opening and closing of the
    container; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} assist visually impaired individuals.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 34](#1143350-1204107){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143350-1204107.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-115-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 115"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hidden features

- **[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  interior surface, exterior surface and panel of any container in which
  a cannabis product is packaged and any covering of such a container
  must not include any hidden feature that is designed to change the
  appearance of the container, covering or panel, such as heat-activated
  ink or a feature that is visible only through technological means,
  except a bar code or a feature that is used to prevent counterfeiting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Feature designed to change surface area

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 132.27, the interior surface and
  exterior surface of any container in which a cannabis product is
  packaged and any covering of such a container must not include any
  feature that is designed to change the surface area of the container
  or covering, such as a fold-out panel.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 35](#1143352-1204114){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143352-1204114.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 31](#1514903-1523977){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514903-1523977.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-116-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 116"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scent and sound

**[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** The interior surface,
exterior surface and panel of any container in which a cannabis product
is packaged and any covering of such a container must not be capable of
emitting a scent or sound.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 35](#1143352-1204118){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143352-1204118.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-117-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 117"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Covering --- brand element

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** The covering of any
container in which a cannabis product is packaged must not display any
brand element.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Covering --- image or information

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided under the Act, any other Act of Parliament or any provincial
Act, the covering of any container in which a cannabis product is
packaged must not display any image or information.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Covering --- transparent and colourless

**[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided under the Act, any other Act of Parliament or any provincial
Act, the covering of any container in which a cannabis product is
packaged must be transparent and colourless.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cut-out window

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  interior surface, exterior surface and panel of any container in which
  a cannabis product is packaged must not include any cut-out window.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), any container in which dried
  cannabis, fresh cannabis or cannabis plant seeds are packaged may
  include cut-out windows.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 36](#1143354-1204122){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143354-1204122.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 32](#1514908-1523983){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514908-1523983.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-121-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 121"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bar code

- **[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One
  or two bar codes may be displayed, only once each, on any container in
  which a cannabis product is packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Colour

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every bar code must be printed in black and white.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 33](#1514910-1523990){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514910-1523990.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-122-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 122"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wrapper

**[[122.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122.1 .sectionLabel}** A wrapper may be
used with respect to a cannabis product only if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is in direct contact with the cannabis or the
  cannabis accessory and with one or both of the following:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the immediate container of the cannabis product,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a wrapper that is in direct contact with the
    cannabis or the cannabis accessory; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is required to maintain the quality or stability
  of the cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 37](#1143356-1204130){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143356-1204130.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging requirements --- other
Regulations

**[[122.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122.2 .sectionLabel}** The following
immediate container and wrappers must meet the requirements set out in
Division 23 of Part B of the [Food and Drug
Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) and subparagraphs
186(a)(i), (ii) and (v) to (vii) of the [Safe Food for Canadians
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-108) as if the cannabis that the
immediate container contains or with which the wrappers are in direct
contact were a food for the purposes of that Division and those
subparagraphs:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the immediate container in which edible cannabis ---
  or a cannabis accessory that contains edible cannabis --- that is a
  cannabis product is packaged;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any wrapper that is in direct contact with edible
  cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that contains edible cannabis ---
  that is a cannabis product; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any wrapper that is in direct contact with a cannabis
  extract that is intended for ingestion --- or a cannabis accessory
  that contains cannabis extract intended for ingestion --- that is a
  cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 37](#1143356-1204137){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143356-1204137.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity --- cannabis extract

**[[122.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122.3 .sectionLabel}** The immediate
container of a cannabis extract that is a cannabis product must not
contain more than 90 mL of extract that is in non-solid form at a
temperature of 22 ± 2°C.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 37](#1143356-1204141){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143356-1204141.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outermost container

- **[[122.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The outermost container in which a
  cannabis product is packaged must not contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} food;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} more than one class of cannabis set out in Schedule
    4 to the Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} more than one immediate container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- multiple immediate
  containers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(c), the outermost container may
  contain more than one immediate container if the following
  requirements are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis or fresh cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the outermost container,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of section 124, if it
        contains cannabis that is in discrete units and that is not
        intended to be consumed by means of inhalation,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of section 124.1, if it
        contains cannabis that is in discrete units and that is intended
        to be consumed by means of inhalation, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} despite section 125, meets the requirements of
        section 124.1 if it contains cannabis that is not in discrete
        units, as if it were in discrete units;

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the immediate containers meet the requirements
      of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} section 124, if they contain cannabis that is
        in discrete units and that is not intended to be consumed by
        means of inhalation,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} section 124.1, if they contain cannabis that is
        in discrete units and that is intended to be consumed by means
        of inhalation, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} section 125, if they contain cannabis that is
        not in discrete units;

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis contained in the
      outermost container does not exceed the equivalent of 30 g of
      dried cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4)
      of the Act, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the properties of the cannabis are consistent in
      all immediate containers;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis extract,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the outermost container,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of section 132.1, if it
        contains cannabis that is in discrete units and that is not
        intended to be consumed by means of inhalation,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of section 132.11, if it
        contains cannabis that is in discrete units and that is intended
        to be consumed by means of inhalation, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} despite section 132.12, meets the requirements
        of section 132.11 if it contains cannabis that is not in
        discrete units, as if it were in discrete units;

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the immediate containers meet the requirements
      of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} section 132.1, if they contain cannabis that is
        in discrete units and that is not intended to be consumed by
        means of inhalation,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} section 132.11, if they contain cannabis that
        is in discrete units and that is intended to be consumed by
        means of inhalation, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} section 132.12, if they contain cannabis that
        is not in discrete units;

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis contained in the
      outermost container does not exceed the equivalent of 30 g of
      dried cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4)
      of the Act, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the properties of the cannabis are consistent in
      all the immediate containers;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis topical,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the outermost container,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of section 132.15, if it
        contains cannabis that is in discrete units, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} despite section 132.16, meets the requirements
        of section 132.15 if it contains cannabis that is not in
        discrete units, as if it were in discrete units;

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the immediate containers meet the requirements,
      of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} section 132.15, if they contain cannabis that
        is in discrete units, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} section 132.16, if they contain cannabis that
        is not in discrete units;

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis contained in the
      outermost container does not exceed the equivalent of 30 g of
      dried cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4)
      of the Act, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the properties of the cannabis are consistent in
      all the immediate containers;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of edible cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the outermost container,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of section 132.18, if it
        contains cannabis that is in discrete units, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} despite section 132.19, meets the requirements
        of section 132.18 if it contains cannabis that is not in
        discrete units, as if it were in discrete units;

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the immediate containers meet the requirements,
      of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} section 132.18, if they contain cannabis that
        is in discrete units, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} section 132.19, if they contain cannabis that
        is not in discrete units;

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis contained in the
      outermost container does not exceed the equivalent of 30 g of
      dried cannabis, as determined in accordance with subsection 2(4)
      of the Act, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the properties of the cannabis are consistent in
      all the immediate containers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- "unit"

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} References to "unit" are to be read as references to
  "immediate container"

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subparagraph (2)(a)(i), in
    paragraphs 124(1)(b) and (c) and 124.1(b) and (c);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subparagraph (2)(b)(i), in
    paragraphs 132.1(1)(b) and (c) and 132.11(b) and (c);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of clause (2)(c)(i)(A), in
    paragraphs 132.15(b) and (c);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of clause (2)(c)(i)(B), in
    paragraphs 132.15(b), (c), (e) and (g);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of clause (2)(d)(i)(A), in
    paragraph 132.18(1)(b); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of clause (2)(d)(i)(B), in
    paragraphs 132.18(1)(b), (d), and (h).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 37](#1143356-1204159){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143356-1204159.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 34](#1514912-1524041){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514912-1524041.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-122.4-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 122.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control measures for dispensing cannabis
extract

- **[[122.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The immediate container of a
  cannabis extract that is a cannabis product and that is not in
  discrete units must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not permit the extract to be easily poured or drunk
    directly from the container; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contain an integrated dispensing mechanism that
    dispenses no more than 10 mg of THC per activation, taking into
    account the potential to convert THCA into THC, if the cannabis
    extract

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is in liquid form at a temperature of 22 ± 2°C,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is not intended to be consumed only by means of
      inhalation, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} contains at least 10 mg of THC, taking into
      account the potential to convert THCA into THC.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- integrated
  dispensing mechanism

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply to an immediate
  container in which a cannabis accessory referred to in paragraph
  103.2(a) is packaged.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 37](#1143356-1204171){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143356-1204171.html"}
:::

### [Labelling --- Cannabis Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848579 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following information must be included on the label that is applied to
  any container in which a cannabis product is packaged:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis plant or cannabis plant
      seeds, the holder of a licence for cultivation that cultivated the
      cannabis plant or cannabis plant seeds, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other cannabis product, the
      holder of a licence for processing that manufactured the product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to the
    Act to which the cannabis that is in the immediate container
    belongs;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the product

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the brand name,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the lot number, preceded by one of the following
      designations:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} "Lot number",

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} "Lot no.",

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} "Lot", or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} "(L)",

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the recommended storage conditions, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the packaging date;

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the warning "KEEP OUT OF REACH OF CHILDREN / [TENIR
    HORS DE LA PORTÉE DES ENFANTS]{.otherLang lang="fr"}";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} one of the health warning messages set out in the
    document entitled Cannabis Health Warning Messages, as amended from
    time to time and published by the Government of Canada on its
    website, that applies to the cannabis product; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis product that contains THC
    in a concentration greater than 10 μg/g, taking into account the
    potential to convert THCA into THC, the standardized cannabis symbol
    that must be obtained from the Minister in the form of an electronic
    file.

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- packaging date

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subparagraph (1)(c)(iv), the packaging date
  that is included on the label that is applied to the container may
  differ from the date on which the cannabis product is packaged if the
  product is packaged no more than seven days before or after the
  packaging date that is included on the label.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- multiple immediate
  containers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an outermost container that contains
  multiple immediate containers in accordance with subsection 122.4(2),
  the outermost container is exempt from the requirement of subparagraph
  (1)(c)(iv).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rotation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The health warning messages referred to in paragraph
  (1)(e) must be displayed in rotation on each type of container of each
  brand name of the cannabis product that is packaged in a year, so that
  each health warning message is displayed, to the extent possible, on
  equal numbers of containers of that product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- sections 26 and 27
  of Act

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Sections 26 and 27 of the Act do not apply with
  respect to the name and email address that are included on the label
  in accordance with paragraph (1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote

  ajax-after="1143358-1204189.html"}

  ajax-after="1514914-1524053.html"}

Version](section-123-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 123"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amended health warning message

- **[[123.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 123(1)(e), a
  health warning message that applies to a cannabis product, as set out
  in the document entitled Cannabis Health Warning Messages as it read
  immediately before the day on which the amended version of the
  document is published by the Government of Canada, may be used on the
  label that is applied to any container in which a cannabis product is
  packaged for a period of 12 months following the day on which the
  amended version of the document is published.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rotation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The health warning messages referred to in
  subsection (1) must be displayed in rotation on each type of container
  of each brand name of the cannabis product that is packaged during the
  12-month period referred to in subsection (1), so that each health
  warning message is displayed, to the extent possible, on equal numbers
  of containers of that product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- holder of a licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence that is authorized to sell or
  distribute cannabis is, in respect of the sale or distribution of
  cannabis products, exempt from the application of subsection 106(1) of
  these Regulations and from section 25 of the Act if the cannabis
  products are packaged and labelled in accordance with subsections (1)
  and (2) and the other applicable provisions of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- other person

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person, other than a holder of a licence, that is
  authorized to sell cannabis is, in respect of the sale of cannabis
  products, exempt from the application of section 25 of the Act if
  those products are packaged and labelled in accordance with
  subsections (1) and (2) and the other applicable provisions of these
  Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 36](#1514921-1524064){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514921-1524064.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry date

- **[[123.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The label of a container in which
  cannabis other than edible cannabis is packaged must not include an
  expiry date unless the holder of the licence for processing that
  manufactured the cannabis product has data that establishes the
  stability period during which, after the cannabis is packaged in
  accordance with these Regulations and stored under its recommended
  storage conditions,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis or fresh cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it maintains not less than 80% and not more than
      120% of its THC content and CBD content, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the microbial and chemical contaminants it
      contains or has on it remain within the limits referred to
      subsection 93(3); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis extract or a cannabis
    topical,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it maintains its THC content and CBD content
      within the variability limits referred to in subsection 97(1), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the microbial and chemical contaminants it
      contains or has on it remain within the limits referred to in
      section 101.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No expiry date --- edible cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The label of a container in which edible cannabis is
  packaged must not include an expiry date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stability period --- retention of
  document

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence for processing that
  manufactured the cannabis product must, if they include an expiry date
  on the label of the container, retain a document that contains the
  data referred to in subsection (1) for at least two years after the
  day on which the last sale or distribution of any portion of the lot
  or batch of the cannabis product with that expiry date takes place,
  other than for destruction.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 36](#1514921-1524079){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514921-1524079.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wrapper

- **[[123.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The interior and exterior surface of
  a wrapper must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not display any brand element;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not display any image or information;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be one uniform colour, which may be different for
    each surface;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} not be fluorescent, have fluorescent properties in
    the ink or have pigments that absorb ultraviolet energy and transmit
    it as a longer wavelength, such as the Pantone 800 series;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} have a smooth texture without any embossing or
    decorative ridges;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} not include any hidden feature that is designed to
    change the appearance of the wrapper, such as heat-activated ink or
    a feature that is visible only through technological means; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} not be capable of emitting a scent or sound.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Images or information

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), images or information
  authorized under the Act, any other Act of Parliament, any provincial
  Act or their regulations may be displayed on the interior or exterior
  surface of the wrapper.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standardized cannabis symbol

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), the standardized cannabis
  symbol that must be obtained from the Minister in the form of an
  electronic file must be clearly and prominently displayed on the
  exterior surface of any wrapper if the concentration of THC in the
  cannabis that is in direct contact with the wrapper or that is in the
  cannabis accessory that is in direct contact with the wrapper is
  greater than 10 μg/g, taking into account the potential to convert
  THCA into THC.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The standardized cannabis symbol must meet the
  following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be at least 1.27 cm by 1.27 cm in size;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be displayed with a white border of at
    least 2 points on all sides; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a change is made to the size of the symbol, its
    dimensions must be proportional vertically and horizontally.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dried cannabis or fresh cannabis ---
discrete units and not intended for inhalation

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  the case of dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- or a cannabis
  accessory that contains dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- that is
  in discrete units and is not intended to be consumed by means of
  inhalation, the label of any container in which the cannabis product
  is packaged must also include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of dried cannabis or
    fresh cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of dried cannabis or
    fresh cannabis in each unit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 38\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of THC, in milligrams, that each unit
    could yield, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into
    THC, preceded by "Total THC per unit";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 38\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, that each
    discrete unit could yield, taking into account the potential to
    convert CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total CBD per unit"; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC on label

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of THC that is included, in accordance
  with paragraph (1)(e), on the label of a container in which is
  packaged dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory
  that contains dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- that is intended
  for ingestion or nasal, rectal or vaginal use must not exceed 10 mg.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dried cannabis or fresh cannabis ---
discrete units and intended for inhalation

**[[124.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.1 .sectionLabel}** In the case of
dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that
contains dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- that is in discrete units
and is intended to be consumed by means of inhalation, the label of any
container in which the cannabis product is packaged must also include
the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of dried cannabis or fresh
  cannabis;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of dried cannabis or fresh
  cannabis in each unit;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 39\]]{.Repealed}

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of THC, in milligrams per gram,
  that the dried cannabis or fresh cannabis could yield, taking into
  account the potential to convert THCA into THC, preceded by "Total
  THC";

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 39\]]{.Repealed}

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of CBD, in milligrams per gram,
  that the dried cannabis or fresh cannabis could yield, taking into
  account the potential to convert CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total
  CBD"; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- not
in discrete units

**[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** In the case of dried
cannabis or fresh cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that contains
dried cannabis or fresh cannabis --- that is not in discrete units, the
label of any container in which the cannabis product is packaged must
also include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of dried cannabis or fresh
  cannabis;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 40\]]{.Repealed}

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of THC, in milligrams per gram,
  that the dried cannabis or fresh cannabis could yield, taking into
  account the potential to convert THCA into THC, preceded by "Total
  THC"; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 40\]]{.Repealed}

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of CBD, in milligrams per gram,
  that the dried cannabis or fresh cannabis could yield, taking into
  account the potential to convert CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total
  CBD".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis plants

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
cannabis plant, the number of plants in the container must be included
on the label that is applied to any container in which a cannabis
product is packaged.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis plant seeds

**[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** In the case of cannabis
plant seeds, the number of seeds in the container must be included on
the label that is applied to any container in which a cannabis product
is packaged.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of information --- general
requirement

- **[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  information that is included on a label must be in English and in
  French, except for the INCI name and the European Union (EU) technical
  name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation --- required information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} All information that is required to be included on a
  label must be clearly and prominently displayed and readily
  discernible under the customary conditions of purchase and use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other required information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} All information that is required to be included on a
  label, other than the brand name, the standardized cannabis symbol and
  the health warning message must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subparagraph (e)(ii), it must be in a
    regular weight and width standard sans serif font, without italics,
    in the colour black and with leading of at least 7 points;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be in one single font type;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subparagraph (e)(iv), it must be in a
    type size of at least 6 points and smaller than the type size used
    for the health warning message;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it must be on a white background that extends at
    least 6 points on all sides away from the information; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the information required under
    paragraphs 124(1)(e) and (g), 124.1(e) and (g), 125(c) and (e),
    132.1(1)(e) and (g), 132.11(e) and (g), 132.12(1)(c) and (e),
    132.15(e) and (g), 132.16(c) and (e), 132.18(1)(d), (f), (h) and (j)
    and 132.19(1)(c) and (e), it must be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} displayed on the principal display panel or, if
      there are separate principal display panels for English and
      French, on each principal display panel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in bold type,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} at least 6 points away from any other
      information, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in a type size of at least 6 points and equal in
      size or smaller than the type size used for the health warning
      message.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Brand name

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The brand name that is required to be included on a
  label must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be in a type size that is smaller than or
    equal to the type size used for the health warning message;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must not be a colour that has the lustre of
    metal or has metallic properties in the ink, such as Pantone
    Metallics or Pantone Premium Metallics;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must not be a colour that is fluorescent, has
    fluorescent properties in the ink or has pigments that absorb
    ultraviolet energy and transmit it as a longer wavelength, such as
    the Pantone 800 series; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it must be displayed only once on the principal
    display panel, or if there are separate principal display panels for
    English and French, only once on each principal display panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standardized cannabis symbol

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The standardized cannabis symbol that is required to
  be included on a label must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must appear in the upper left 25% of the
    principal display panel, or if there are separate principal display
    panels for English and French, in the upper left 25% of each
    principal display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be at least 1.27 cm by 1.27 cm in size;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must be displayed with a white border of at
    least 2 points on all sides;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it must be oriented in such a manner that its text
    is readable from left to right when the container is displayed or
    visible under the customary conditions of purchase and use; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if a change is made to the size of the symbol, its
    dimensions must be proportional vertically and horizontally.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health warning message

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The health warning message that is required to be
  included on a label must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be displayed on the principal display panel
    or, if there are separate principal display panels for English and
    French, on each principal display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraphs (c) and (d), it must be in a
    regular weight and width standard sans serif font, without italics,
    in the colour black and with leading of at least 8 points;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the word "WARNING" must be in upper case letters
    and bold type;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the first sentence must be in sentence case letters
    and bold type;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the second sentence must be in sentence case
    letters;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} it must be in the same font type as that used for
    the information referred to in subsection (3);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} it must be in a type size of at least 7 points and
    the type size must be equal to or larger than the type size used for
    the brand name;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} it must be within a black border that is a solid
    line of at least 1 point and that has an inset of at least 6 points
    on all sides between the message and the border;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the background colour must be yellow with the CMYK
    value (C=0 M=0 Y=100 K=0);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the message must be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} left-justified without hyphenation, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} oriented in such a manner that its text is
      readable from left to right when the container is displayed or
      visible under the customary conditions of purchase and use; and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-206, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case where there is one principal display
    panel, there must be at least 3 points between the English and
    French message.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attribution

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Any attribution to the source of the health warning
  message that is included on the label must meet the following
  requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} only the following attributions may be displayed:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if there is one principal display panel, the
      words "Health Canada / [Santé Canada]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if there are separate principal display panels
      for English and French, the words "Health Canada" for the English
      display panel and the words "[Santé Canada]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"
      for the French display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be in the same font type as that used for
    the information referred to in subsection (3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must be in a type size of at least 6 points and
    smaller than the type size used for the health warning message; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it must be inside the border of the health warning
    message and be displayed in the bottom right corner below the health
    warning message text, with leading of at least 7 points.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other information

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Any other information that is included on the label
  must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be in regular weight and width standard
    sans serif font, without italics, and in black or white colour; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be in a type size that is smaller than or
    equal to the type size used for the information referred to in
    subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Brand element

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A label may include only one brand element, other
  than a brand name, if that brand element meets the following
  requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be displayed only once on the principal
    display panel or, if there are separate principal display panels for
    English and French, only once on each principal display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must not be a colour that has the lustre of
    metal or has metallic properties in the ink, such as Pantone
    Metallics or Pantone Premium Metallics;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must not be a colour that is fluorescent, has
    fluorescent properties in the ink or has pigments that absorb
    ultraviolet energy and transmit it as a longer wavelength, such as
    the Pantone 800 series;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the brand element is an image, its surface area
    must be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the standardized cannabis
      symbol must be included on the label in accordance with paragraph
      123(1)(f), smaller than or equal to the surface area of the
      standardized cannabis symbol, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, smaller than or equal to 25%
      of the principal display panel and smaller than or equal to the
      surface area within the border that surrounds the health warning
      message that is included on the label in accordance with paragraph
      (6)(h); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the brand element is text only, its type size
    must be smaller than or equal to the type size used for the health
    warning message.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Image

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The label may include an image that is printed in
  black and white and that provides instructions on how to open the
  container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of information on irradiation
  --- edible cannabis

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Information that is required to be included on a
  label under paragraph 132.18(1)(p) or 132.19(1)(k) must be displayed
  on the principal display panel or, if there are separate principal
  display panels for English and French, on each principal display
  panel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation resembling standardized
cannabis symbol

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** A representation, such
as an illustration, sign, mark, symbol or design, that so closely
resembles the standardized cannabis symbol that it is likely to be
mistaken for that symbol must not appear on any container in which a
cannabis product is packaged.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 45(F)](#1143404-1204266){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143404-1204266.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insert or leaflet

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  container in which a cannabis product is packaged may include, or be
  accompanied by, one or more inserts or leaflets, as long as the insert
  or leaflet does not present a risk of contamination of the cannabis
  product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} All information that is included on an insert or
  leaflet must be in English and French, except for the INCI name and
  the European Union (EU) technical name.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 42](#1514939-1524106){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514939-1524106.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-132-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 132"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis extract --- discrete units and
not intended for inhalation

- **[[132.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a cannabis extract
  --- or a cannabis accessory that contains a cannabis extract --- that
  is in discrete units and is not intended to be consumed by means of
  inhalation, the label of any container in which the cannabis product
  is packaged must also include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis extract;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis extract
    in each unit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 43\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of THC, in milligrams, that each unit
    could yield, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into
    THC, preceded by "Total THC per unit";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 43\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, that each unit
    could yield, taking into account the potential to convert CBDA into
    CBD, preceded by "Total CBD per unit";

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a list of the ingredients of the cannabis extract;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of any food allergen that is present in
    the cannabis extract, except as a result of cross-contamination;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the cannabis product in terms of
    its common name or in terms of its function; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC on label

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of THC that is included on the label, in
  accordance with paragraph (1)(e), of a container in which is packaged
  a cannabis extract --- or in which is packaged a cannabis accessory
  that contains a cannabis extract --- that is intended for ingestion or
  nasal, rectal or vaginal use must not exceed 10 mg.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis extract --- discrete units and
intended for inhalation

**[[132.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.11 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
cannabis extract --- or a cannabis accessory that contains a cannabis
extract --- that is in discrete units and is intended to be consumed by
means of inhalation, the label of any container in which the cannabis
product is packaged must also include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis extract;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis extract in
  each unit;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of THC, in milligrams per gram,
  that the cannabis extract could yield, taking into account the
  potential to convert THCA into THC, preceded by "Total THC";

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of CBD, in milligrams per gram,
  that the cannabis extract could yield, taking into account the
  potential to convert CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total CBD";

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a list of the ingredients of the cannabis extract;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of any food allergen that is present in the
  cannabis extract, except as a result of cross-contamination;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the cannabis product in terms of its
  common name or in terms of its function; and

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

- [SOR/2025-43, s. 44](#1514944-1524112){.wb-lbx

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis extract --- not in discrete
units

- **[[132.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a cannabis extract
  --- or a cannabis accessory that contains a cannabis extract --- that
  is not in discrete units, the label of any container in which the
  cannabis product is packaged must also include the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis extract;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 45\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of THC, in milligrams per gram,
    that the cannabis extract could yield, taking into account the
    potential to convert THCA into THC, preceded by "Total THC";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 45\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of CBD, in milligrams per gram,
    that the cannabis extract could yield, taking into account the
    potential to convert CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total CBD";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis accessory that contains a
    cannabis extract intended for ingestion or nasal, rectal or vaginal
    use or that is packaged with and is intended to dispense the
    extract,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of THC, in milligrams, that each
      activation of the accessory dispenses, taking into account the
      potential to convert THCA into THC, preceded by "Total THC per
      activation", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, that each
      activation of the accessory dispenses, taking into account the
      potential to convert CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total CBD per
      activation";

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a list of the ingredients of the cannabis extract;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the name of any food allergen that is present in
    the cannabis extract, except as a result of cross-contamination;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the cannabis product in terms of
    its common name or in terms of its function; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC on label

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of THC that is included on the label in
  accordance with subparagraph (1)(f)(i) must not exceed 10 mg.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flavours --- cannabis extract

- **[[132.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited to display on a
  cannabis extract that is a cannabis product or on a cannabis accessory
  that contains a cannabis extract and that is a cannabis product --- or
  on the package of such a cannabis product, on an insert or leaflet
  that is included in a container in which such a cannabis product is
  packaged or that accompanies that container or on the label or panel
  of a container in which such a cannabis product is packaged --- an
  indication or illustration, including a brand element, that could
  cause a person to believe that the cannabis product has a flavour set
  out in column 1 of Schedule 3 to the [Tobacco and Vaping Products
  Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5), other than the flavour of cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- name and email
  address

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to the
  name and email address that are included on the label in accordance
  with paragraph 123(1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204325){.wb-lbx

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of ingredients --- cannabis extract

- **[[132.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The list of ingredients of a
  cannabis extract --- or of a cannabis accessory that contains a
  cannabis extract --- must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the word "Ingredients" in the English version and
    the word "[Ingrédients]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in the French version
    must appear at the beginning of the list;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no intervening printed, written or graphic material
    is to appear between the word referred to in paragraph (a) and the
    first ingredient in the list; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the ingredients must be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} set out in descending order of their proportion
      of the cannabis extract by weight, determined before the
      ingredients are combined to form the extract,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of vitamins referred to in
      subsection 101.3(3), set out by their chemical name,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, set out by their common name
      or chemical name, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} separated from other ingredients by a comma.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredients in proportion of 1% or less

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subparagraph (1)(c)(i), ingredients that are
  present in a proportion of 1% or less of the cannabis extract may be
  listed in any order after the ingredients that are present in a
  proportion of more than 1% of the cannabis extract.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- flavouring agent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(c), in the case where the
  cannabis extract contains one flavouring agent, it may be shown
  individually at the end of the list of ingredients by the name
  "flavouring agent" and in the case where the cannabis extract contains
  more than one flavouring agent, they may be shown collectively at the
  end of the list of ingredients by the name "flavouring agents".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No individual listing of flavouring
  agent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If flavouring agents are shown collectively by the
  name "flavouring agents" under subsection (3), a flavouring agent must
  not be shown individually in the list of ingredients.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204343){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204343.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis topical --- discrete units

**[[132.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.15 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
cannabis topical --- or a cannabis accessory that contains a cannabis
topical --- that is in discrete units, the label of any container in
which the cannabis product is packaged must also include the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis topical;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis topical in
  each unit;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 47\]]{.Repealed}

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} either the quantity of THC, in milligrams, or the
  concentration of THC, in milligrams per gram, that each unit could
  yield, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into THC,
  preceded by "Total THC per unit";

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 47\]]{.Repealed}

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} either the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, or the
  concentration of CBD, in milligrams per gram, that each unit could
  yield, taking into account the potential to convert CBDA into CBD,
  preceded by "Total CBD per unit";

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a list of the ingredients of the cannabis topical;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the cannabis product in terms of its
  common name or in terms of its function; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204357){.wb-lbx

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis topical --- not in discrete
units

**[[132.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.16 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
cannabis topical --- or a cannabis accessory that contains a cannabis
topical --- that is not in discrete units, the label of any container in
which the cannabis product is packaged must also include the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, in grams, of the cannabis topical;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 48\]]{.Repealed}

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} either the quantity of THC, in milligrams, or the
  concentration of THC, in milligrams per gram, that the cannabis
  topical could yield, taking into account the potential to convert THCA
  into THC, preceded by "Total THC";

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 48\]]{.Repealed}

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} either the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, or the
  concentration of CBD, in milligrams per gram, that the cannabis
  topical could yield, taking into account the potential to convert CBDA
  into CBD, preceded by "Total CBD";

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a list of the ingredients of the cannabis topical;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the cannabis product in terms of its
  common name or in terms of its function; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of ingredients -- cannabis topical

- **[[132.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The list of ingredients of a
  cannabis topical --- or of a cannabis accessory that contains a
  cannabis topical --- must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the word "Ingredients" in the English version and
    the word "[Ingrédients]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in the French version
    must appear at the beginning of the list;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no intervening printed, written or graphic material
    is to appear between the term referred to in paragraph (a) and the
    first ingredient in the list; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the ingredients are to be separated from other
    ingredients by a comma and shown in descending order of their
    proportion of the cannabis topical by weight, determined before the
    ingredients are combined to form the cannabis topical, as follows:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by their INCI name,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if an ingredient has no INCI name, by its
      chemical name,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a botanical, by specifying at
      least the genus and species portions of its INCI name or, if it
      has no INCI name, by its chemical name, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if an ingredient is included in the schedule to
      the [Cosmetic Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._869), by
      its EU technical name set out in column 1 of that schedule, or by
      the appropriate INCI name set out in column 2 of that schedule and
      the appropriate French equivalent set out in column 3 of that
      schedule.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredients in proportion of 1% or less

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(c), ingredients that are
  present in a proportion of 1% or less and all colouring agents of the
  cannabis topical may be listed in any order after the ingredients that
  are present in a proportion of more than 1% of the cannabis topical.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fragrance and flavour

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The word "parfum" or "aroma", respectively, may be
  inserted at the end of the list of ingredients to indicate that an
  ingredient has been added to the cannabis topical to produce a
  fragrance or flavour.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[botanical]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[botanical]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an ingredient that is directly
  derived from a plant and that has not been chemically modified before
  it is used in the production of a cannabis topical.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Edible cannabis --- discrete units

- **[[132.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of edible cannabis ---
  or a cannabis accessory that contains edible cannabis --- that is in
  discrete units, the label of any container in which the cannabis
  product is packaged must also include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the edible cannabis is in solid form, its net
    weight, in grams, and in any other case, its net volume, in
    millilitres;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of THC, in milligrams, that each unit
    could yield, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into
    THC, preceded by "Total THC per unit";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of THC, in milligrams, that the edible
    cannabis could yield, taking into account the potential to convert
    THCA into THC, preceded by "Total THC";

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, that each unit
    could yield, taking into account the potential to convert CBDA into
    CBD, preceded by "Total CBD per unit";

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of CBD, in milligrams, that the edible
    cannabis could yield, taking into account the potential to convert
    CBDA into CBD, preceded by "Total CBD";

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a list of the ingredients of the edible cannabis,
    including constituents, if any;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the source of any food allergen or gluten present
    in the edible cannabis, except as a result of cross-contamination,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a food allergen source, gluten source and
      added sulphites statement, if the food allergen or gluten

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is, or is present in, an ingredient that is not
        shown in the list of ingredients, but is not a constituent of
        that ingredient or present in a constituent of that ingredient,
        or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} is, or is present in, a constituent and neither
        the constituent nor the ingredient in which it is present is
        shown in the list of ingredients, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in all other cases, either in the list of
      ingredients or in a food allergen source, gluten source and added
      sulphites statement;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the sulphites that are present in the edible
    cannabis in an amount of 10 p.p.m. or more,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if at least one sulphite is required to be shown
      in the list of ingredients under these Regulations, in the list of
      ingredients, or in the list of ingredients and in a food allergen
      source, gluten source and added sulphites statement, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, in the list of ingredients,
      in a food allergen source, gluten source and added sulphites
      statement or in both;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} a nutrition facts table that contains only the
    information set out in column 1 of the table to section 132.22,
    expressed using a description set out in column 2, in the unit set
    out in column 3 and in the manner set out in column 4;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} the common name of the cannabis product;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} if the edible cannabis is irradiated under section
    102.6, the symbol set out in subsection B.01.035(5) of the [Food and
    Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) and one of the
    following statements or a statement that has the same meaning:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "treated with radiation",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "treated by irradiation", or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} "irradiated"; and

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} if an irradiated food referred to in column 1 of
    the table to Division 26 of Part B of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) is an ingredient or
    constituent of the edible cannabis and constitutes 10% or more of
    the edible cannabis, the statement "irradiated" preceding any
    mention of the ingredient or constituent on the label.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC on label

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of THC that is included on the label of
  an immediate container in accordance with paragraph (1)(f) must not
  exceed 10 mg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient not required to be listed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(k), if one or more constituents
  of an ingredient are required by these Regulations to be listed in a
  list of ingredients, the ingredient is not required to be listed if
  all constituents of the ingredient are shown in the list by their
  common names and in accordance with subparagraphs 132.21(1)(c)(i) and
  (ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of cross-contamination

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(l), the source of a food
  allergen or gluten must be shown on the label if it includes a
  declaration alerting consumers that, due to a risk of
  cross-contamination, the edible cannabis may contain the source of a
  food allergen or gluten.

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if at least one sulphite is required to be shown
      in the list of ingredients under these Regulations, in the list of
      ingredients, or in the list of ingredients and in a food allergen
      source, gluten source and added sulphites statement, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, in the list of ingredients,
      in a food allergen source, gluten source and added sulphites
      statement or in both;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a nutrition facts table that contains only the
    information set out in column 1 of the table to section 132.22,
    expressed using a description set out in column 2, in the unit set
    out in column 3 and in the manner set out in column 4;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the common name of the cannabis product;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if the edible cannabis is irradiated under section
    102.6, the symbol set out in subsection B.01.035(5) of the [Food and
    Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) and one of the
    following statements or a statement that has the same meaning:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "treated with radiation",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "treated by irradiation", or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} "irradiated"; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if an irradiated food referred to in column 1 of
    the table to Division 26 of Part B of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) is an ingredient or
    constituent of the edible cannabis and constitutes 10% or more of
    the edible cannabis, the statement "irradiated" preceding any
    mention of the ingredient or constituent on the label.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity of THC on label

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of THC that is included on the label in
  accordance with paragraph (1)(c) must not exceed 10 mg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient not required to be listed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(f), if one or more constituents
  of an ingredient are required by these Regulations to be listed in a
  list of ingredients, the ingredient is not required to be listed if
  all constituents of the ingredient are shown in the list by their
  common names and in accordance with subparagraphs 132.21(1)(c)(i) and
  (ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of cross-contamination

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(g), the source of a food
  allergen or gluten must be shown on the label if it includes a
  declaration alerting consumers that, due to a risk of
  cross-contamination, the edible cannabis may contain the source of a
  food allergen or gluten.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Durable life date required

- **[[132.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of edible cannabis
  having a durable life of 90 days or less, the durable life date must
  be shown on the label of any container in which the edible cannabis is
  packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Format of durable life date

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any durable life date on the label of any container
  in which edible cannabis is packaged must be shown in accordance with
  subsections B.01.007(4) and (5) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of ingredients -- edible cannabis

- **[[132.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The list of ingredients of edible
  cannabis --- or of a cannabis accessory that contains edible cannabis
  --- must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the word "Ingredients" in the English version and
    the word "[Ingrédients]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in the French version
    must appear at the beginning of the list;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no intervening printed, written or graphic material
    is to appear between the word referred to in paragraph (a) and the
    first ingredient in the list;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the ingredients and constituents must be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} set out in descending order of their proportion
      of the edible cannabis by weight, determined before the
      ingredients and the constituents are combined to form the edible
      cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} separated from other ingredients or constituents
      by a comma, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} set out by the applicable name set out in
      column 2 of Table 1 of the Common Names for Ingredients and
      Components Document or, if none applies, by their common name;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the constituents of an ingredient must be shown

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} set out in parentheses, immediately after the
      ingredient, unless the source of a food allergen or gluten is set
      out immediately after the ingredient, in which case the
      constituent of the ingredient must be set out immediately after
      that source,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} set out in descending order of their proportion
      of the ingredient by weight, determined before they are combined
      to form the edible cannabis, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} separated from other constituents by a comma;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the source of a food allergen or gluten must be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} set out in parentheses,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} set out immediately after an ingredient that is
      shown in that list, if the food allergen or gluten

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is the ingredient,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} is present in the ingredient, but is not a
        constituent of or present in a constituent of that ingredient,
        or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} is, or is present in, a constituent of the
        ingredient and the constituent is not shown in the list of
        ingredients,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} set out immediately after the constituent that
      is shown in the list, if the food allergen or gluten is that
      constituent or is present in that constituent, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} separated by a comma from other sources of a
      food allergen or gluten that is shown for the same ingredient or
      constituent;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} sulphites must be shown

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} set out by one of the common names "sulfites",
      "sulfiting agents", "sulphites" or "sulphiting agents", or
      individually by the applicable name set out in the applicable
      item, column 1 of the applicable Table of the Common Names for
      Ingredients and Components Document,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the name "sodium dithionite",
      "sulphur dioxide" or "sulphurous acid", set out by that name,
      followed by one of the common names "sulfites", "sulfiting
      agents", "sulphites" or "sulphiting agents" in parentheses, unless

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the word "sulfite" or "sulphite" appears in the
        common name of another sulphite in the list,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} one of the common names "sulfites", "sulfiting
        agents", "sulphites" or "sulphiting agents" is set out in
        parentheses following another sulphite in the list, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} one of the common names "sulfites", "sulfiting
        agents", "sulphites" or "sulphiting agents" is shown in a food
        allergen source, gluten source and added sulphites statement on
        the label, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} set out at the end of the list where they may
      be shown in any order with the other ingredients that are shown at
      the end of that list in accordance with subsection (3) or in
      parentheses immediately after the ingredient of which they are a
      constituent; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the edible cannabis contains one or more
    sugars-based ingredients,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the word "Sugars" in the English version of the
      list and the word "[Sucres]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in the French
      version of the list must appear

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} despite subparagraph (c)(i), in descending
        order of the proportion of all the sugars-based ingredients in
        the edible cannabis by weight, determined before they are
        combined to form the edible cannabis, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} separated from other ingredients by a comma,
        and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each sugars-based ingredient must be shown

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} set out in parentheses, immediately following
        the word "Sugars" in the English version of the list and the
        word "[Sucres]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in the French version of
        the list,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} set out in descending order of its proportion
        of the edible cannabis by weight, determined before it is
        combined to form the edible cannabis, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} separated from other sugars-based ingredients
        by a comma.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- ingredients and
  constituents shown collectively

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(c), the ingredients and the
  constituents set out in column 1 of Table 2 of the Common Names for
  Ingredients and Components Document may be shown collectively in the
  list of ingredients by the common name set out in column 2 of that
  Table, unless one of the ingredients or constituents in column 1 is
  shown separately in the list of ingredients by its common name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- ingredients at the end of
  the list

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subparagraph (1)(c)(i), the ingredients
  referred to in subsection B.01.008.2(4) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870), regardless of their
  proportion, may be listed at the end of the list of ingredients, in
  any order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- source of food allergen
  or gluten

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(e), the source of the food
  allergen or gluten is not required to be set out in parentheses
  immediately after the ingredient or constituent, as the case may be,
  if the source of the food allergen or gluten appears

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the list of ingredients

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} as part of the common name of the ingredient or
      constituent, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in parentheses, in accordance with subparagraph
      (1)(e)(i), immediately after another ingredient or constituent; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the food allergen source, gluten source and
    added sulphites statement.
