[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nutrition facts table

- **[[132.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The percentage of the daily value
  for a nutrient shown in the nutrition facts table on the label of any
  container in which edible cannabis is packaged must be established on
  the basis of the amount, by weight, of the nutrient per immediate
  container of edible cannabis, rounded off in the applicable manner set
  out in column 4 of the table to this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not a significant source of a nutrient

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Information with respect to a nutrient set out in
  column 1 of the table to this section that may be expressed as "0" in
  the nutrition facts table may be omitted from that table if it
  includes the statement "Not a significant source of (naming each
  nutrient that is omitted from the nutrition facts table in accordance
  with this subsection)".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 130, the nutrition facts table must
  be presented in accordance with the format specified in the applicable
  figure in the Directory of Nutrition Facts Table Formats for Edible
  Cannabis, as amended from time to time and published by the Government
  of Canada on its website, having regard to matters such as order of
  presentation, dimensions, spacing and use of upper and lower case
  letters and bold type.

  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"
  scope="row">Immediate container size</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">“Per container
  (naming the amount of edible cannabis in the immediate container)”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">The size is
  expressed per immediate container in grams or millilitres.</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><p>The size is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is 0.1 g or more or

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 10 g or more or

  “Total Calories” or “Calories, Total”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">The value is
  expressed in calories per immediate container.</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><p>The value is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 5
  calories, to the nearest multiple of 1 calorie;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 5 calories or more

  and</p></li>

  calories, to the nearest multiple of 10 calories.</p></li>
  </ul></td>

  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"
  scope="row">Amount of fat</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">“Fat”, “Total

  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"><p>The amount is
  expressed</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in grams per immediate
  container; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> as a percentage of the daily
  value per immediate container.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> The amount is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 0.5 g, to
  the nearest multiple of 0.1 g;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.5 g or more but

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if it is more than 5 g, to

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in all other cases, to the

  </ul></li>

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.5 g or more but

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if it is more than 5 g, to

  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><ul>

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.5 g or more, to

  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in grams per immediate
  container; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> as a percentage of the daily
  value per immediate container.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> The amount is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 0.5 g, to
  0 g; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.5 g or more, to

  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> The percentage is rounded

  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the amount is declared as
  “0 g”, to 0%; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in all other cases, to the
  nearest multiple of 1%.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">11</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"
  scope="row">Amount of sugars</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">“Sugars”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"><p>The amount is
  expressed</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in grams per immediate
  container; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> as a percentage of the daily
  value per immediate container.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> The amount is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 0.5 g, to
  0 g; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.5 g or more, to
  the nearest multiple of 1 g.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> The percentage is rounded
  off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the amount is declared as
  “0 g”, to 0%; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in all other cases, to the
  nearest multiple of 1%.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">12</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"
  scope="row">Amount of protein</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">“Protein”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">The amount is
  expressed in grams per immediate container.</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><p>The amount is rounded
  off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 0.5 g, to
  the nearest multiple of 0.1 g; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.5 g or more, to
  the nearest multiple of 1 g.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">13</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"
  scope="row">Amount of potassium</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft">“Potassium”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top alignleft"><p>The amount is
  expressed</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in milligrams per immediate
  container; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> as a percentage of the daily
  value per immediate container.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top alignleft"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> The amount is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 5 mg, to
  0 mg;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 5 mg or more but
  less than 50 mg, to the nearest multiple of 10 mg;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if it is 50 mg or more but
  less than 250 mg, to the nearest multiple of 25 mg; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> if it is 250 mg or more, to
  the nearest multiple of 50 mg.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> The percentage is rounded
  off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the amount is declared as
  “0 mg”, to 0%; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in all other cases, to the
  nearest multiple of 1%.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom alignleft">14</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom alignleft" scope="row">Amount of
  calcium</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom alignleft">“Calcium”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom alignleft"><p>The amount is
  expressed</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in milligrams per immediate
  container; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> as a percentage of the daily
  value per immediate container.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom alignleft"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> The amount is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 5 mg, to
  0 mg;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 5 mg or more but
  less than 50 mg, to the nearest multiple of 10 mg;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if it is 50 mg or more but
  less than 250 mg, to the nearest multiple of 25 mg; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> if it is 250 mg or more, to
  the nearest multiple of 50 mg.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> The percentage is rounded
  off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the amount is declared as
  “0 mg”, to 0%; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in all other cases, to the
  nearest multiple of 1%.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight alignleft">15</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight alignleft" scope="row">Amount of iron</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight alignleft">“Iron”</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight alignleft"><p>The amount is expressed</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in milligrams per immediate
  container; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> as a percentage of the daily
  value per immediate container.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  <td class="alignleft"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> The amount is rounded off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if it is less than 0.05 mg,
  to 0 mg;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if it is 0.05 mg or more but
  less than 0.5 mg, to the nearest multiple of 0.1 mg;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if it is 0.5 mg or more but
  less than 2.5 mg, to the nearest multiple of 0.25 mg; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> if it is 2.5 mg or more, to
  the nearest multiple of 0.5 mg.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> The percentage is rounded
  off</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the amount is declared as
  “0 mg”, to 0%; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in all other cases, to the
  nearest multiple of 1%.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of source of food allergen

- **[[132.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The source of a food allergen
  required to be shown in the list of ingredients or in the food
  allergen source, gluten source and added sulphites statement under
  paragraph 132.18(1)(l) or 132.19(1)(g) must be set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a food allergen from a food referred to in one
    of paragraphs (a), (b) and (e) of the definition [[food
    allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that food, by the name of the food as shown in the
    applicable paragraph, expressed in the singular or plural;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a food allergen from the food referred to in
    paragraph (c) of the definition [[food
    allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that food, by the name "sesame", "sesame seed" or
    "sesame seeds";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a food allergen from a food referred to in
    paragraph (d) or (f) of the definition [[food
    allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that food, by the name of the food as shown in the
    applicable paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for a food allergen from the food referred to in
    paragraph (g) of the definition [[food
    allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that food, by the name "soy", "soya", "soybean" or
    "soybeans";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for a food allergen from a food referred to in one
    of paragraphs (h) to (j) of the definition [[food
    allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that food, by the common name of the food set out in
    the applicable item, column 2 of the applicable Table of the Common
    Names for Ingredients and Components Document; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for a food allergen from the food referred to in
    paragraph (k) of the definition [[food
    allergen]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that food, by the name "mustard", "mustard seed" or
    "mustard seeds".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of source of gluten

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The source of gluten required to be shown in the list
  of ingredients or in the food allergen source, gluten source and added
  sulphites statement under paragraph 132.18(1)(l) or 132.19(1)(g) must
  be set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for gluten from the grain of a cereal referred to
    in one of subparagraphs (a)(i) to (v) of the definition
    [[gluten]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the
    [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or
    derived from that grain, by the name of the cereal as shown in the
    applicable subparagraph; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for gluten from the grain of a hybridized strain
    created from one or more of the cereals referred to in subparagraphs
    (a)(i) to (v) of the definition [[gluten]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
    subsection B.01.010.1(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) or derived from that
    grain, by the names of the cereals as shown in the applicable
    subparagraphs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration on risk of
cross-contamination

**[[132.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.24 .sectionLabel}** If the label of
the container in which edible cannabis is packaged includes a
declaration alerting consumers that, due to a risk of
cross-contamination, the edible cannabis may contain the source of a
food allergen or gluten, the declaration must meet the following
requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be shown immediately after the food allergen
  source, gluten source and added sulphites statement or, if there is
  none, immediately after the list of ingredients, and must appear on
  the same continuous surface as the statement, if any, and the list of
  ingredients; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} no intervening printed, written or graphic material
  is to appear between it and the list of ingredients or statement that
  immediately precedes it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204548){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204548.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of food allergen statement

- **[[132.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A food allergen source, gluten
  source and added sulphites statement must meet the following
  requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the word "Contains" in the English version and the
    word "[Contient]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in the French version must
    appear at the beginning of the list;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no intervening printed, written or graphic material
    is to appear between the word referred to in paragraph (a) and the
    rest of the statement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must appear on the same continuous surface as
    the list of ingredients; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it must include, even if any of the following
    information is also shown in the list of ingredients,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the source of each food allergen that is present
      in the edible cannabis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each source of any gluten that is present in the
      edible cannabis, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} one of the common names "sulfites", "sulfiting
      agents", "sulphites" or "sulphiting agents", if the total amount
      of sulphites present in the edible cannabis is 10 p.p.m. or more.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No duplication

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(d), the following information
  is not required to be shown in the statement more than once:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the same source of a food allergen;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the same source of gluten; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one of the common names "sulfites", "sulfiting
    agents", "sulphites" or "sulphiting agents".

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204565){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204565.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constituents not required to be shown on
label

- **[[132.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Constituents of ingredients or of
  classes of ingredients set out in the table to subsection B.01.009(1)
  of the [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870)
  are not required to be shown on the label of a container in which
  edible cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory that contains edible
  cannabis --- that is a cannabis product is packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preparation or mixture

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if a preparation or
  mixture set out in the table to subsection B.01.009(2) of the [Food
  and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) is used to
  produce edible cannabis, the ingredients and constituents of the
  preparation or mixture are not required to be shown on the label of
  the container in which edible cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory
  that contains edible cannabis --- that is a cannabis product is
  packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common name

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a preparation or mixture set out in the table to
  subsection B.01.009(2) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) is used to produce
  edible cannabis and the preparation or mixture has one or more of the
  ingredients or constituents listed in subsection B.01.009(3) of the
  [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870), those
  ingredients or constituents must be shown by their common names in the
  list of the ingredients of the edible cannabis to which they are added
  as if they were ingredients of that edible cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constituents required to be shown in
  list of ingredients

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), if any of the
  constituents listed in subsection B.01.009(4) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) is contained in an
  ingredient of edible cannabis set out in a table referred to in
  subsection (1) or (2), that constituent must be shown in the list of
  ingredients.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204576){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204576.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immediate container

- **[[132.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A label of an immediate container in
  which a cannabis product is packaged may extend beyond the exterior
  display surface, or a peel-back or accordion panel may be applied to
  the immediate container, to ensure that all the required information
  to be included on the label in accordance with these Regulations is
  displayed on the label or panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Label or panel not easily removed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The label that extends beyond the exterior display
  surface and the panel must be applied in a manner that they cannot be
  easily removed from the immediate container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Panel

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The panel must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be able to be resealed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withstand repeated openings and closings without
    detaching from the immediate container under customary conditions of
    use; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} include any of the following information that is
    not included on the label:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to
      the Act to which the cannabis that is in the immediate container
      belongs,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the recommended storage conditions,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the packaging date,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 51\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 51\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the list of ingredients of the cannabis product,
      including constituents, if any,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis or fresh
      cannabis, the net weight,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis extract,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, including the net weight of
        cannabis extract in each unit, if the cannabis extract is in
        discrete units,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of THC and CBD that is dispensed
        with each activation of any cannabis accessory that is packaged
        with or contains the cannabis extract, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the name of any food allergen that is present
        in the product,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis topical, its net
      weight, including the net weight of cannabis topical in each unit,
      if the cannabis topical is in discrete units, and

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} in the case of edible cannabis,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the edible cannabis is in solid form, its
        net weight, and in any other case, its net volume,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the durable life date,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the source of any food allergen or gluten
        present in the edible cannabis, except as a result of
        cross-contamination,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} sulphites that are present in the edible
        cannabis in an amount of 10 p.p.m. or more, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the nutrition facts table.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- packaging date

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subparagraph 132.27(3)(c)(iii), the
  packaging date that is included on the panel may differ from the date
  on which the cannabis product is packaged if the product is packaged
  no more than seven days before or after the packaging date that is
  included on the panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- information on panel

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information included on the panel must be shown
  in accordance with the provisions of these Regulations with respect to
  a label as if the panel were a label for the purposes of those
  provisions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Brand element

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The panel must not display any brand element.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement on location of information

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The label of an immediate container in which a
  cannabis product is packaged and to which a panel is applied must
  include a statement that clearly indicates the location of any
  information required under these Regulations that is not included on
  the label.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Image

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The label referred to in subsection (6) may include
  an image that is printed in black and white and that provides
  instructions on how to open the panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on exterior display surface

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the information that is required under
  these Regulations, the label referred to in subsection (6) may include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} bar codes, in accordance with section 122;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a brand element, in accordance with subsection (9);
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an image, in accordance with subsection 130(10).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- brand element

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraphs 130(9)(d) and (e), a brand element
  included on a label that extends beyond the exterior display surface
  or on a label of a container to which a panel is applied must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the brand element is an image, be 1.27 cm by
    1.27 cm in size or smaller; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the brand element is text only, be in a type
    size that is 7 points or smaller.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited representation --- health and
cosmetic benefits

**[[132.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.28 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to make an express or implied representation, including by way of a
brand element, on a cannabis product --- or on the package of a cannabis
product, on an insert or leaflet that is included in a container in
which such a cannabis product is packaged or that accompanies that
container or on the label or panel of a container in which such a
cannabis product is packaged --- if there are reasonable grounds to
believe that the representation could create the impression that health
or cosmetic benefits may be derived from the use of the cannabis
product.

::: HistoricalNote

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited representation --- energy
value and amount of nutrient

- **[[132.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.29
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited to make an express
  or implied representation, including by way of a brand element, on
  edible cannabis that is a cannabis product or on a cannabis accessory
  that contains edible cannabis and that is a cannabis product --- or on
  the package of such a cannabis product, on an insert or leaflet that
  is included in a container in which such a cannabis product is
  packaged or that accompanies that container or on the label or panel
  of a container in which such a cannabis product is packaged ---
  concerning the energy value referred to in item 2 of the table to
  section 132.22 or the amount of any nutrient referred to in items 3 to
  15 of that table or in items 5 to 37 of the table to section B.01.402
  of the [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- nutrition facts
  table

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, subsection (1) does not limit
  the application of paragraphs 132.18(1)(n) and 132.19(1)(i).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited representation --- dietary
requirements

**[[132.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.3 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited to
make an express or implied representation, including by way of a brand
element, on edible cannabis that is a cannabis product or on a cannabis
accessory that contains edible cannabis and that is a cannabis product
--- or on the package of such a cannabis product, on an insert or
leaflet that is included in a container in which such a cannabis product
is packaged or that accompanies that container or on the label or panel
of a container in which such a cannabis product is packaged --- if there
are reasonable grounds to believe that the representation could create
the impression that the cannabis product is intended

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to meet the particular dietary requirements of an
  individual

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} who has a physical or physiological condition as a
    result of a disease, disorder or injury, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for whom a particular effect, including weight
    loss, is to be obtained by a controlled intake of food; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to meet the dietary requirements of young persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204646){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204646.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 54](#1514982-1524164){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514982-1524164.html"}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited representation --- alcoholic
beverages

**[[132.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.31 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to make an express or implied representation, including by way of a
brand element, on a cannabis product --- or on the package of a cannabis
product, on an insert or leaflet that is included in a container in
which such a cannabis product is packaged or that accompanies that
container or on the label or panel of a container in which such a
cannabis product is packaged --- if there are reasonable grounds to
believe that the representation could associate the cannabis product
with an alcoholic beverage.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204650){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204650.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 55](#1514984-1524151){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514984-1524151.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-132.31-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 132.31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited representation --- tobacco
products and vaping products

**[[132.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.32 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited
to make an express or implied representation, including by way of a
brand element, on a cannabis product --- or on the package of a cannabis
product, on an insert or leaflet that is included in a container in
which such a cannabis product is packaged or that accompanies that
container or on the label or panel of a container in which such a
cannabis product is packaged --- if there are reasonable grounds to
believe that the representation could associate the cannabis product
with a [[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section
2 of the [Tobacco and Vaping Products Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5), or a
vaping product to which that Act applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204654){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204654.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 56](#1514986-1524154){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514986-1524154.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-132.32-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 132.32"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- name and email
address

**[[132.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.33 .sectionLabel}** Section 132.28,
subsection 132.29(1) and sections 132.3 to 132.32 do not apply with
respect to the name and email address that are included on the label in
accordance with paragraph 123(1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204658){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204658.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standardized cannabis symbol on cannabis
product intended for inhalation

- **[[132.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132.34
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The standardized cannabis symbol
  that must be obtained from the Minister in the form of an electronic
  file must be clearly and prominently displayed on the outer surface of
  a cannabis accessory that contains a cannabis extract and that is a
  cannabis product intended to be consumed by means of inhalation if the
  cannabis extract contains THC in a concentration greater than 10 μg/g,
  taking into account the potential to convert THCA into THC.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The standardized cannabis symbol must meet the
  following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be at least 1.27 cm by 1.27 cm in size;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must be displayed with a white border of at
    least 2 points on all sides; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a change is made to the size of the symbol, its
    dimensions must be proportional vertically and horizontally.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 46](#1143406-1204668){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143406-1204668.html"}
:::

### [Cannabis Product Accuracy Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848723 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net weight and volume

**[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** The net weight and
volume that must be included on the label of a cannabis product in
accordance with sections 124, 124.1, 125, 132.1, 132.11, 132.12, 132.15,
132.16, 132.18 and 132.19 must be within the tolerance limits set out
for that product in the document entitled Tolerance Limits for the Net
Weight and Volume Declared on Cannabis Product Labelling, as amended
from time to time and published by the Government of Canada on its
website.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 47](#1143408-1204672){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143408-1204672.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-133-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 133"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of discrete units

**[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** The number of discrete
units in a container that is labelled in accordance with sections 124,
124.1, 132.1, 132.11, 132.15 and 132.18 must be equal to the number
specified on the label.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 47](#1143408-1204676){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143408-1204676.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-134-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 134"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of immediate containers

**[[134.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134.1 .sectionLabel}** The number of
immediate containers in an outermost container that is labelled in
accordance with subparagraphs 122.4(2)(a)(i), (b)(i), (c)(i) and (d)(i)
must be equal to the number of immediate containers specified on the
label.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 47](#1143408-1204680){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143408-1204680.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 57](#1514988-1524157){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514988-1524157.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-134.1-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 134.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of cannabis plants

**[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** The number of cannabis
plants in a container that is labelled in accordance with section 128
must be equal to the number specified on the label.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of cannabis plant seeds

**[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** The number of cannabis
plant seeds in a container that is labelled in accordance with section
129 must be equal to the number specified on the label.

### [Labelling --- Cannabis Other than Cannabis Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848732 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement --- cannabis other than a
cannabis product

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** Unless the requirements
in section 138 have been met,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence for processing, a holder of a
  licence for analytical testing and the individuals referred to in
  section 4 must not sell or distribute a reference standard; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence must not export a reference
  standard or sell, distribute or export any other cannabis that is not
  a cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 17](#1381780-1382977){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381780-1382977.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-137-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 137"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

- **[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  label that includes the following information must be applied to any
  container that contains cannabis, other than a cannabis product:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    holder of the licence that sells, distributes or exports the
    cannabis or, in the case where an individual referred to in section
    4 sells or distributes a reference standard, the name, telephone
    number and email address of the laboratory where that individual
    carries out their duties; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the lot number, preceded by one of the following
      designations:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} "Lot number",

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} "Lot no.",

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} "Lot", or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} "(L)", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the packaging date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- name and email
  address

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 26 and 27 of the Act do not apply with
  respect to the name and email address that are included on the label
  in accordance with paragraph (1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 48](#1143410-1204697){.wb-lbx

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Drugs Containing Cannabis]{.HTitleText1} {#h-848745 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848746 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[brand name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brand name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
    commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
    usuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include
    administering. ([distribuer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[establishment licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[establishment licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence issued
    under section C.01A.008 of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([licence
    d'établissement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hospital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hospital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is licensed, approved or designated by a
      province under the laws of the province to provide care or
      treatment to individuals or animals suffering from any form of
      disease or illness; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is owned or operated by the Government of
      Canada or the government of a province and that provides health
      services. ([hôpital]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licensed dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licensed dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a [[licensed
    dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
    [Narcotic Control Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041),
    that specializes in the destruction of
    [[narcotics]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1)
    of those Regulations. ([distributeur autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([praticien]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[qualified person in charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualified person in charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    individual referred to in section 150. ([responsable
    qualifié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Security Directive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Security Directive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Directive on
    Physical Security Requirements for Controlled Substances and Drugs
    Containing Cannabis, as amended from time to time and published by
    the Government of Canada on its website. ([Directive en matière de
    sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[senior person in charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[senior person in charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the individual
    referred to in section 149. ([responsable
    principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 49](#1143412-1204700){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143412-1204700.html"}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Licence]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848760 .Subheading}

#### [Activities]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848761 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized activities

- **[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a cannabis drug licence is authorized
  to conduct the following activities if they are authorized by the
  licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} possessing cannabis obtained in accordance with
    these Regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} producing or selling a drug containing cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary activities --- production

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a cannabis drug licence that authorizes
  the production of a drug containing cannabis is also authorized to
  distribute and offer to produce the drug.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary activities --- sale

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a cannabis drug licence that authorizes
  the sale of a drug is also authorized to distribute the drug.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this section,
  [[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include obtaining cannabis by
  cultivating, propagating or harvesting it.

**[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2019-206, s. 50\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-141-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 141"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of organic solvent

**[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a holder of a cannabis drug licence
that authorizes the production of a drug containing cannabis is also
authorized to alter or offer to alter the chemical or physical
properties of the cannabis by the use of an organic solvent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

- **[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a holder
  of a cannabis drug licence that authorizes the sale of a drug
  containing cannabis may sell or distribute the drug only to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} another holder of a cannabis drug licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual referred to in section 4.

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a research licence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a holder of an analytical testing licence;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a licence from which the holder of
    the cannabis drug licence received the drug, if the drug is sold or
    distributed for the purpose of its return;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a licence or a licensed dealer, if the
    drug is sold or distributed for the purpose of its destruction;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a pharmacist;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a practitioner;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a hospital employee;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption in relation to the
    drug has been granted under section 140 of the Act; or

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a cannabis drug licence that authorizes
  the sale of a drug containing cannabis may only sell the drug if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they have received a written order that specifies
    the name and quantity of the drug to be supplied and is signed and
    dated

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drug to be provided to a
      hospital employee or a practitioner in a hospital, by the
      pharmacist in charge of the hospital's pharmacy or by a
      practitioner authorized by the individual in charge of the
      hospital to sign the order, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the person to which the
      drug is to be sold; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they have verified the signature, if it is unknown
    to them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- pharmacist and
  practitioner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a holder of a cannabis drug
  licence to sell or distribute any drug containing cannabis to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a pharmacist who is named in a notice issued under
    subsection 181(2) or (4); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a practitioner who is named in a notice issued
    under subsection 189(2) or (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply to a holder of a
  cannabis drug licence to which the Minister has issued a notice of
  retraction issued under

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 182(1) in respect of the pharmacist
    referred to in paragraph (3)(a); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 190(1) in respect of a practitioner
    referred to in paragraph (3)(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presence of qualified person in charge

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a cannabis
drug licence may conduct an activity in relation to cannabis, other than
its destruction or antimicrobial treatment, at the licensed site only if
the qualified person in charge or an alternate qualified person in
charge is present at the site.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Antimicrobial treatment

**[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a cannabis
drug licence may conduct antimicrobial treatment of cannabis at a
location other than the licensed site only if the cannabis is
subsequently returned to the licensed site or distributed in accordance
with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 58](#1514990-1524161){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514990-1524161.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-145-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 145"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction --- holder of a cannabis drug
licence

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a cannabis drug licence is authorized to destroy cannabis
  only

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a method that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} complies with all federal, provincial and
      municipal environmental protection legislation applicable to the
      location where it is to be destroyed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} does not result in any individual being exposed
      to cannabis smoke or cannabis vapour;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the presence of a witness referred to in
    paragraph (3)(a) and another witness referred to in subsection (3);
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the cannabis is destroyed at a
    location other than the licensed site, if the holder ensures that
    the cannabis that is at the location is, at all times, in the
    presence of at least one witness referred to in paragraph (3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction --- licensed dealer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The licensed dealer is authorized to destroy cannabis
  only

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a method that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} complies with all federal, provincial and
      municipal environmental protection legislation applicable to the
      location at which it is to be destroyed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} does not result in any individual being exposed
      to cannabis smoke or cannabis vapour; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the presence of a witness referred to in
    paragraph (4)(a) and another witness referred to in subsection (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Witness --- holder of a cannabis drug
  licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following individuals are qualified to witness
  the destruction of cannabis by a holder of a cannabis drug licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the senior person in charge, the qualified person
    in charge or the alternate qualified person in charge; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Witness --- licensed dealer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following individuals are qualified to witness
  the destruction of cannabis by a licensed dealer:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [[qualified person in
    charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
    [Narcotic Control Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041), or
    an alternate qualified person in charge designated under subsection
    9.2(2) of those Regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the licensed dealer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement by witnesses

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For each instance that they destroy cannabis, the
  holder of a cannabis drug licence or the licensed dealer, as the case
  may be, must obtain a statement signed and dated by two of the
  witnesses referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (2)(b) stating that they
  witnessed the destruction and that the cannabis was destroyed in
  accordance with a method referred to in paragraph (1)(a) or (2)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} With respect to cannabis that they destroy, the
  holder of a cannabis drug licence or the licensed dealer, as the case
  may be, must prepare a record that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, its form and
    quantity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drug containing cannabis, the
    brand name of the drug and its strength per unit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the cannabis
    is destroyed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a brief description of the method and the date of
    destruction; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the names of the individuals who witnessed the
    destruction and were qualified to do so under paragraph (1)(b) or
    (2)(b) and the basis on which they were qualified under
    subsection (3) or (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a cannabis drug licence and the
  licensed dealer must retain the record and the statement for at least
  two years after the day on which the cannabis is destroyed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-169, s. 30](#1136231-1208626){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1136231-1208626.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-146-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 146"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Licence Issuance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848850 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence --- content

**[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis drug licence
must set out the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder of the licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence number;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of the site where the activity is
  authorized and, if applicable, of each building within the site;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the authorized activity at the site and, if
  applicable, the authorized activity that may be conducted at each
  building within the site;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the security measures to be implemented to comply
  with the requirements of either the Security Directive or Part 4;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} any conditions that the Minister considers
  appropriate;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the licence; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a cannabis
drug licence must submit an application to amend the licence if they
propose to make any of the following changes:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change to the name of the holder;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change to the authorized activity at the site or
  the authorized activity at each building within the site; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a change to the security measures implemented to
  comply with the requirements of either the Security Directive or Part
  4.

#### [Individuals in Charge]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848866 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Senior person in charge

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
cannabis drug licence must retain the services of one individual as a
senior person in charge who has overall responsibility for the
management of the activities, with respect to cannabis, that are
authorized under the licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualified person in charge

- **[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a cannabis drug licence must retain the services of one
  individual as a qualified person in charge who is responsible for
  supervising the activities with respect to cannabis that are
  authorized under the licence and for ensuring, on behalf of the
  holder, that those activities comply with these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate qualified person in charge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a cannabis drug licence may designate an
  individual as an alternate qualified person in charge who is
  authorized to replace the qualified person in charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifications

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Only an individual who meets the following
  requirements may be a qualified person in charge or an alternate
  qualified person in charge:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they work at the site specified in the licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are entitled to practise a profession that is
      relevant to their duties, such as that of pharmacist,
      practitioner, pharmacy technician or laboratory technician,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} hold a diploma, certificate or credential that
      is awarded by a post-secondary educational institution in Canada
      in a field or occupation that is relevant to their duties, such as
      pharmacy, medicine, dentistry, veterinary medicine, pharmacology,
      chemistry, biology, pharmacy technician, laboratory technician,
      pharmaceutical regulatory affairs or supply chain management or
      security, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} hold a diploma, certificate or credential that
      is awarded by a foreign educational institution in a field or
      occupation referred to in subparagraph (ii) and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an [[equivalency
        assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection
        73(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-227), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an equivalency assessment issued by an
        organization or institution that is responsible for issuing
        equivalency assessments and is recognized by a province;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} they have sufficient knowledge of and experience
    with the use and handling of drugs to properly carry out their
    duties; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} they are familiar with the provisions of the Act
    and these Regulations that apply to the holder of the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a cannabis drug licence may retain the
  services of an individual as a qualified person in charge or designate
  an alternate qualified person in charge who does not meet any of the
  requirements of paragraph (3)(b) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no other individual working at the site specified
    in the licence meets those requirements;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those requirements are not necessary for the
    activities that are authorized under the licence; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individual possesses knowledge --- acquired
    from a combination of education, training and work experience ---
    that is sufficient to enable them to properly carry out their
    duties.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineligibility

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual is not eligible to be a senior person in charge, a
  qualified person in charge or an alternate qualified person in charge
  if, in the past 10 years,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a designated offence, a controlled
    substance offence, or any other offence referred to in subsection
    (2), the individual

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} was convicted as an adult, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} was a [[young person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} who
      received an [[adult sentence]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those
      terms are defined in subsection 2(1) of the [Youth Criminal
      Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an offence committed outside Canada
    that, if committed in Canada, would have constituted a designated
    offence, a controlled substance offence or any other offence
    referred to in subsection (2),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the individual was convicted as an adult, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they committed the offence when they were at
      least 14 years old but less than 18 years old, the individual
      received a sentence that was longer than the maximum youth
      sentence that could have been imposed under the [Youth Criminal
      Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5) for such an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other offences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The other offences for the purpose of subsection (1)
  are the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an offence involving the financing of terrorism
    referred to in any of sections 83.02 to 83.04 of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an offence involving fraud referred to in any of
    sections 380 to 382 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the offence of laundering proceeds of crime
    referred to in section 462.31 of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an offence involving a criminal organization
    referred to in any of sections 467.11 to 467.13 of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a conspiracy or an attempt to commit, being an
    accessory after the fact in relation to, or any counselling in
    relation to, an offence referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to
    (d). 

#### [Changes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848906 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's approval

- **[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a cannabis drug licence must obtain the Minister's approval
  before making the following changes:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} replacing the senior person in charge;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} replacing the qualified person in charge; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} designating or replacing an alternate qualified
    person in charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must, for the purpose of obtaining the
  Minister's approval, submit an application that includes the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the change;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the replacement of the senior person
    in charge,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a declaration signed and dated by the proposed
      individual attesting that they are not ineligible for a reason
      specified in section 151,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document issued by a Canadian police force
      indicating whether, during the 10 years before the day on which
      the application is submitted, the individual was convicted as
      specified in subparagraph 151(1)(a)(i) or received a sentence as
      specified in subparagraph 151(1)(a)(ii), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the individual has ordinarily resided in a
      country other than Canada during the 10 years before the day on
      which the application is submitted, a document issued by a police
      force of that country indicating whether in that period that they
      were convicted as specified in subparagraph 151(1)(b)(i) or
      received a sentence as specified in subparagraph 151(1)(b)(ii);
      and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the designation of an alternate
    qualified person in charge or in the case of the replacement of the
    qualified person in charge or an alternate qualified person in
    charge

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the declaration and the documents referred to in
      subparagraphs (b)(i) to (iii),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the senior
      person in charge, attesting that the proposed individual has the
      knowledge and experience required under paragraphs 150(3)(c) and
      (d), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the proposed individual does not meet the
      requirement of subparagraph 150(3)(b)(i), either

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the individual's diploma, certificate
        or credential referred to in subparagraph 150(3)(b)(ii) or (iii)
        and a copy of the course transcript for it, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the individual's
        education, training or work experience required under paragraph
        150(4)(c), together with supporting evidence, such as a copy of
        a course transcript or an attestation by the person who provided
        the training.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on receiving an application for
  approval, require the submission of any additional information that
  pertains to the information contained in the application and that is
  necessary for the Minister to consider the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval issued

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must approve the change on completion of
  the review of the application if the applicable requirements referred
  to in subsections 150(3) and (4) and section 151 are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification --- various changes

- **[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a cannabis drug licence must notify the Minister of any of
  the following changes within five days after the change occurs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change to the mailing address, telephone number,
    email address and facsimile number of the holder; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change to the plan of the licensed site that does
    not require different security measures to be implemented in order
    to comply with Part 4.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification --- content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the change; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the senior
    person in charge, indicating that all information provided in
    support of the notification is correct and complete to the best of
    their knowledge.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of activities

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a cannabis drug licence that intends to cease conducting all
  the activities authorized by the licence --- whether before or on the
  date of expiry of the licence --- must provide to the Minister a
  written notice to that effect at least 30 days before the day on which
  those activities cease.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be signed and dated by the senior
  person in charge and contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which activities are expected to cease;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the manner in which any cannabis
    remaining at the site as of the date referred to in paragraph (a)
    will be disposed of by the holder, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis will be sold or distributed, in
      whole or in part, the name and address of the person to which it
      will be sold or distributed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it will be destroyed, in whole or in part,
      the day on which and the location at which the destruction is to
      take place;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the holder's
    records, reports, electronic data and other documents that are
    required to be retained under the Act by the holder, will be
    retained after activities have ceased; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, telephone number and, if
    applicable, facsimile number and email address of a person from
    which the Minister may obtain further information after activities
    have ceased.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Update

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} After having ceased the activities, the holder must
  submit to the Minister an update of the information referred to in
  paragraphs (2)(a) to (d), if it differs from what was set out in the
  notice submitted under subsection (1). The update must be signed and
  dated by the senior person in charge.

#### [Refusal, suspension and revocation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848952 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other grounds for refusal

**[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 62(7)(h) of the Act, the other grounds for refusing to issue,
renew or amend a cannabis drug licence are as follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not hold an establishment licence
  that is necessary to authorize them to conduct, at the site proposed
  in the application or at a building within the site, the activities in
  relation to drugs containing cannabis that they intend to conduct
  there;

- [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the Minister of Health suspends, in respect of an
  activity that the applicant intends to conduct in relation to drugs
  containing cannabis, an establishment licence that is necessary to
  authorize the applicant to conduct that activity at the site proposed
  in the application or at a building within the site;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the cannabis drug licence applies for
  an amendment to change the location of the site where an activity that
  is authorized by the licence may be conducted; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the renewal or amendment of the
  licence, the holder of the licence does not hold the cannabis licence
  issued under subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), if it is required;

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 51](#1143415-1204705){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143415-1204705.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 155"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension --- other circumstance

**[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 64(1)(b) of the Act, other circumstances for the suspension of
a cannabis drug licence are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister of Health suspends, in respect of an
  activity that the holder of the cannabis drug licence is authorized to
  conduct under the licence, an establishment licence that is necessary
  to authorize the holder to conduct that activity at the site or at a
  building within the site;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis licence issued to the holder of the
  cannabis drug licence under subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) is suspended under subsection 23(2) of that
  Act.

- [SOR/2019-206, s. 52](#1143417-1204708){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143417-1204708.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-156-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 156"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- other circumstances

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 65(h) of the Act, other circumstances for the revocation of a
cannabis drug licence are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence that has been suspended is not reinstated
  because the reasons for the suspension still exist or the holder of
  the licence has not demonstrated to the Minister that the suspension
  is unfounded;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence has requested its
  revocation in writing;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence no longer holds an
  establishment licence that is necessary to authorize them to conduct,
  at the site or at a building within the site, an activity that is
  authorized under the cannabis drug licence; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence no longer holds the
  cannabis licence referred to in subsection 14(1.1) of the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), if it is required.
