#### [Security]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848968 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security obligations

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a cannabis
drug licence must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect the cannabis they
  possess against theft or loss;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they experience a theft of cannabis or a loss of
  cannabis that cannot be explained on the basis of normally accepted
  operations,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} notify a police force within 24 hours after
    becoming aware of its theft or loss, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, within 10 days
    after becoming aware of its theft or loss; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} take any steps that are necessary to ensure the
  safekeeping of cannabis when distributing it.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Possession, Pharmacists, Practitioners and Hospitals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-848976 .Subheading}

#### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-848977 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession --- Person

**[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** The following persons,
if they have obtained a drug containing cannabis either in accordance
with these Regulations, or from a person that is exempt under section
140 of the Act from the application of subsections 9(1) and (2) and
10(1) and (2) of the Act with respect to that drug, are authorized to
possess the drug:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a pharmacist, a practitioner who is entitled to
  practise in the province in which they possess the drug or a holder of
  a cannabis drug licence, if they require the drug for their business
  or profession;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a practitioner who is entitled to practise in a
  province other than the province in which they possess the drug and
  their possession is for emergency medical purposes only;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a hospital employee or a practitioner in a hospital;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an individual who possesses a quantity of the drug
  that exceeds the applicable limit under the Act and has obtained the
  drug for their own use or for their animal from a practitioner or in
  accordance with a prescription that was not issued or obtained in
  contravention of these Regulations;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} an individual who possesses a quantity of the drug
  that exceeds the applicable limit under the Act and has reasonable
  grounds to believe that the drug was obtained by another individual in
  accordance with paragraph (d) if the possession is for the purpose of
  its return or destruction;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a licensed dealer, if the possession is for the
  purpose of its destruction;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person to which an exemption has been granted under
  section 140 of the Act with respect to the possession of that drug, if
  the possession is for a purpose set out in the exemption; or

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession --- quantity allowed

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** Any quantity of drug
containing cannabis that an individual is authorized to possess under
paragraph 159(d) or (e) is in addition to any other quantity of cannabis
that the individual is authorized to possess under the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

**[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, an individual referred to in paragraph
159(d) or (e) is authorized to distribute a drug containing cannabis to
a pharmacist for its destruction.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Promotion --- communication of brand
name, etc.

**[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person is authorized to promote a
prescription drug by communicating the brand name, the proper name, the
common name, the price or the quantity of the drug.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- section 21 of Act

**[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** A person is exempt from
the application of section 21 of the Act if they display, refer to or
otherwise use the brand name of a prescription drug, or the name of the
holder of the drug identification number assigned in accordance with
subsection C.01.014.2(1) of the [Food and Drug
Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for a prescription drug,
directly or indirectly in a promotion that is used in the sponsorship of
a person, entity, event, activity or facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- section 22 of Act

**[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** A person is exempt from
the application of section 22 of the Act if they display on a facility,
as part of the name of the facility or otherwise, if the facility is
used for a sports or cultural event or activity, the brand name of a
prescription drug or the name of the holder of the drug identification
number assigned in accordance with subsection C.01.014.2(1) of the [Food
and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for a
prescription drug.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- subsection 23(1) of Act

**[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** A person is exempt from
the application of subsection 23(1) of the Act if they publish,
broadcast or otherwise disseminate, on behalf of another person, with or
without consideration, any promotion that is authorized under sections
162 to 164.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization -- drug for veterinary use

**[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person that is authorized to sell a
prescription drug for veterinary use may sell it in a package or with a
label that sets out a depiction of a real or fictional animal that
corresponds to the species of the animal for which the drug is intended.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- drug appealing to young
person

**[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person is authorized to sell a drug
containing cannabis that has an appearance, shape or other sensory
attribute or a function that there are reasonable grounds to believe
could be appealing to young persons.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- drug not in Schedule 4

**[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person authorized to sell a drug
containing cannabis may sell a drug that is of a class that is not
referred to in Schedule 4 to the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- drug containing a
substance in Schedule 5

**[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person is authorized to sell a drug
containing cannabis that contains any substance that is referred to in
column 1 of Schedule 5 to the Act.

#### [Pharmacists]{.HTitleText3} {#h-849008 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

**[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** A pharmacist who
receives a prescription drug from a holder of a cannabis drug licence
must enter the following in a record retained for such purposes:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and quantity of the drug received;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date the drug was received; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the holder.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, distribution and administration

**[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a pharmacist is authorized to sell,
distribute or administer a prescription drug to a person:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is exempted under section 140 of the
  Act with respect to the possession of that drug; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the pharmacist sells, distributes or administers
  the drug

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a written order or prescription
    signed and dated by a practitioner, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} after verifying the signature, if it is unknown to
    the pharmacist.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return and destruction

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a pharmacist is authorized to sell or distribute a
  prescription drug, other than a drug referred to in section 173, in
  accordance with a written order, to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a cannabis drug licence from which
    the pharmacist received the drug, if the drug is sold or distributed
    for the purpose of its return; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence or a licensed
    dealer, if the drug is sold or distributed for the purpose of its
    destruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The written order must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, quantity and strength per unit of the
    drug; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sale or distribution is for the purpose of
    its destruction, a written statement that affirms that the sole
    purpose of the order is the destruction of the drug and that is
    signed and dated by, or on behalf of, the holder of the cannabis
    drug licence or the licensed dealer, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The pharmacist must prepare a record with respect to
  the drug that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its brand name, form, quantity and strength per
    unit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the person to which it was sold or
    distributed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it was sold or distributed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Drug given by individual

- **[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pharmacist is authorized to sell or distribute to a licensed dealer a
  prescription drug that the pharmacist received from an individual
  referred to in paragraph 159(d) or (e) for the purpose of its
  destruction, if the drug is in a container that prevents the removal
  of the drug from the container and is marked in a manner that is
  sufficient to identify the container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping --- pharmacist

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The pharmacist must prepare a record, with respect to
  the drug, that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers distributed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which each container is distributed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the identifying mark of each container; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the licensed dealer to
    which each container is distributed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping --- licensed dealer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The licensed dealer must prepare a record, with
  respect to the drug, that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers received;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the container is received;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the identifying mark of the container; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the pharmacist from whom
    the container is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The pharmacist and the licensed dealer must retain
  the record for at least two years after the day on which the
  pharmacist distributes the drug or the licensed dealer receives the
  drug, as the case may be.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited activities

- **[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 171, subsection
  172(1) and section 175, it is prohibited for a pharmacist to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sell or distribute a prescription drug to a
    pharmacist who is named in a notice issued under subsection 181(2)
    or (4);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sell or distribute a prescription drug to a
    practitioner who is named in a notice issued under subsection 189(2)
    or (4); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fill a prescription or order for a prescription
    drug from such a practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a pharmacist to whom
  the Minister has issued a notice of retraction under

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 182(1), in respect of a pharmacist who
    is named in a notice issued under subsection 181(2) or (4); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 190(1), in respect of a practitioner who
    is named in a notice issued under subsection 189(2) or (4).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution --- hospital

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a
  pharmacist may distribute a prescription drug to a hospital employee
  or a practitioner in a hospital in accordance with a written order
  signed and dated by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist in charge of the hospital's
    pharmacy; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a practitioner who is authorized by the individual
    in charge of the hospital to sign the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before distributing the prescription drug, the
  pharmacist receiving the order must verify the signature on the order,
  if it is unknown to them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security obligations

**[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** A pharmacist must, with
respect to a drug containing cannabis on their premises or for which the
pharmacist is responsible,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect it against theft or
  loss; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, within 10 days after
  becoming aware of its theft or loss.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

**[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177 .sectionLabel}** If, in accordance with
a written order or prescription, a pharmacist dispenses a prescription
drug, the pharmacist must retain a record of the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name or initials;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, initials and address of the practitioner
  who issued the order or prescription;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person for whom the drug
  was dispensed;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, quantity and form of the drug;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the pharmacist dispensed the drug;
  and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number assigned to the order or prescription.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale and distribution for emergency
purposes

- **[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pharmacist may sell or distribute a prescription drug for emergency
  purposes to another pharmacist in accordance with a written order that
  is signed and dated by the other pharmacist.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping --- selling and
  distributing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist must, after selling or distributing a
  prescription drug under subsection (1) or after receiving such a drug
  under subsection (1) or subsection 193(3), enter the details of the
  transaction in a record retained for such purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping --- removing,
  transporting and transferring

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist must, after removing, transporting or
  transferring a prescription drug from one place of business operated
  by the pharmacist to another, enter the details of the transaction in
  a record retained for such purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

**[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** A pharmacist must
retain the records which the pharmacist is required to retain under
these Regulations for at least two years after the day on which they are
prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factual information

**[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
provide in writing any factual information about a pharmacist with
respect to cannabis that has been obtained under the Act or the
[Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) to the
provincial professional licensing authority that is responsible for the
authorization of individuals to practise their profession

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the province in which the pharmacist is or was
  entitled to practise if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the authority submits to the Minister a written
    request that sets out the pharmacist's name and address, a
    description of the information being requested and a statement that
    the information is required for the purpose of assisting an
    investigation by the authority, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
    that the pharmacist has

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} contravened, in relation to cannabis, a rule of
      conduct established by the authority,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} been convicted of a designated offence or a
      controlled substance offence in relation to cannabis,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} contravened these Regulations, or

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} has contravened the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a province in which the pharmacist is not entitled
  to practise, if the authority submits to the Minister

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a written request that sets out the pharmacist's
    name and address and a description of the information requested, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that shows that

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist has applied to that authority to
      practise in that province, or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the authority has reasonable grounds to believe
      that the pharmacist is practising in that province without being
      authorized to do so.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- prohibition to sell or
distribute

- **[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pharmacist may make a written request to the Minister to send a notice
  advising that the following persons must not sell or distribute any
  prescription drug to the pharmacist:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all holders of a cannabis drug licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacies in the province in which the
    pharmacist who is named in the notice is entitled to practise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recipients

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, in the circumstances set out in
  subsection (3), issue the notice referred to in subsection (1) to the
  following persons and authorities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all holders of a cannabis drug licence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacies in the province in which the
    pharmacist is entitled to practise;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicable provincial professional licensing
    authority for the province in which the pharmacist is entitled to
    practise; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} on request, the applicable provincial professional
    licensing authority for any province other than the province
    referred to in paragraph (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be issued if the pharmacist

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made a request to the Minister in accordance with
    subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contravened a rule of conduct, in relation to
    cannabis, established by the provincial professional licensing
    authority for the province in which the pharmacist is practising and
    that provincial professional licensing authority has requested the
    Minister in writing to issue the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has been convicted of any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a designated offence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a controlled substance offence in relation to
      cannabis, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an offence under the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
    the pharmacist has contravened this Part or Part 9.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue the notice to the persons and
  authorities referred to in subsection (2) if the Minister, on
  reasonable grounds, believes that the pharmacist named in the notice

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has contravened section 171, subsection 172(1)
    or (2) or 173(1), section 174, 175 or 176 or subsection 178(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, self-administered a
    prescription drug contrary to accepted pharmaceutical practice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, distributed or
    administered a prescription drug to their spouse, common-law
    partner, parent or child, including a child adopted in fact,
    contrary to accepted pharmaceutical practice; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is unable to account for the quantity of
    prescription drugs for which the pharmacist was responsible under
    this Part or Part 9.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prerequisite

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing a notice under subsection (4), the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consult with the provincial professional licensing
    authority for the province in which the pharmacist to whom the
    notice relates is entitled to practise;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} send to the pharmacist a written notice that
    indicates the reasons why a notice is being considered and give them
    an opportunity to present reasons why the notice should not be
    issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consider

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any reasons that have been presented by the
      pharmacist under paragraph (b),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the compliance history of the pharmacist in
      respect of the Act and its regulations, the [Controlled Drugs and
      Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) and the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) with respect to
      cannabis, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} whether the actions of the pharmacist pose a
      significant risk to public health or public safety, including the
      risk of any prescription drug being diverted to an illicit market
      or activity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retraction of notice

- **[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must retract a notice that was issued under subsection 181(2)
  or (4) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice has
    requested in writing that it be retracted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist has provided a letter from the
    applicable provincial professional licensing authority for the
    province in which the pharmacist is entitled to practise that states
    that the authority consents to the retraction of the notice; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the notice was issued at the
    request of the pharmacist, one year has elapsed since the notice was
    issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to notify

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister retracts a notice, he or she must
  notify, in writing, the persons and authorities to which the notice
  was issued under subsection 181(2) or (4).

#### [Practitioners]{.HTitleText3} {#h-849146 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, distribution and administration

**[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a practitioner is authorized to
administer a drug containing cannabis to an individual or animal, or
sell or distribute it for an individual or animal if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the individual or the animal is under their
  professional treatment; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the drug is required for the condition for which the
  individual or animal is receiving treatment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return and destruction

- **[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  practitioner is authorized to sell or distribute a drug containing
  cannabis, in accordance with a written order, to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a cannabis drug licence from which
    the practitioner received the drug, if the drug is sold or
    distributed for the purpose of its return; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence or a licensed
    dealer, if the drug is sold or distributed for the purpose of its
    destruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written Order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The written order must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, quantity and strength per unit of the
    drug; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sale or distribution is for the purpose of
    its destruction, a written statement that affirms that the sole
    purpose of the order is the destruction of the drug and that is
    signed and dated by, or on behalf of, the holder of the cannabis
    drug licence or the licensed dealer, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The practitioner must prepare a record with respect
  to the drug that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its brand name, form, quantity and strength per
    unit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the person to which it was sold or
    distributed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it was sold or distributed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security obligations

**[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185 .sectionLabel}** A practitioner must,
with respect to a drug containing cannabis that they possess,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect it against theft or
  loss; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, within 10 days after
  becoming aware of its theft or loss.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

**[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186 .sectionLabel}** A practitioner who
sells or distributes a prescription drug to an individual for
self-administration or for administration to an animal must, whether or
not the practitioner charges for the drug, retain a record showing the
name and quantity of the drug sold or distributed, the name and address
of the individual to whom it was sold or distributed and the date on
which it was sold or distributed, if the quantity of the drug exceeds

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} three times the maximum daily dosage set out in the
  notice of compliance issued under section C.08.004 or C.08.004.01 of
  the [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for
  the drug; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the notice of compliance does not set out any
  maximum daily dosage, three times the generally recognized maximum
  daily dosage for the drug.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** A practitioner must
retain the records which the practitioner is required to retain under
these Regulations for at least two years after the day on which they are
prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factual information

**[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
provide in writing any factual information about a practitioner with
respect to cannabis that has been obtained under the Act or the
[Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) to the
provincial professional licensing authority that is responsible for the
authorization of individuals to practise their profession

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a province in which the practitioner is, or was,
  entitled to practise if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the authority submits to the Minister a written
    request that sets out the practitioner's name and address, a
    description of the information requested and a statement that the
    information is required for the purpose of assisting a investigation
    by the authority, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
    that the practitioner has

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} contravened, in relation to cannabis, a rule of
      conduct established by the authority,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} been convicted of a designated offence or a
      controlled substance offence in relation to cannabis,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} contravened these Regulations, or

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} contravened the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a province in which the practitioner is not
  entitled to practise, if the authority submits to the Minister

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a written request that sets out the practitioner's
    name and address and a description of the information requested, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that shows that

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the practitioner has applied to that authority to
      practise in that province, or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the authority has reasonable grounds to believe
      that the practitioner is practising in that province without being
      authorized to do so.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- prohibition

- **[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  practitioner may make a written request to the Minister to send a
  notice advising that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following persons must not sell or distribute
    any prescription drug to the practitioner:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all holders of a cannabis drug licence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacies in the province in which the
      practitioner who is named in the notice is entitled to practise,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacies in an adjacent province in which
      a prescription or order from the practitioner who is named in the
      notice may be filled; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following individual must not fill a
    prescription or order written by the practitioner for a prescription
    drug:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacists in the province in which the
      practitioner who is named in the notice is entitled to practise,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacists in an adjacent province in which
      a prescription or order from the practitioner who is named in the
      notice may be filled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recipients

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, in the circumstances set out in
  subsection (3), issue the notice referred to in subsection (1) to the
  following persons and authorities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the practitioner who is named in the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all holders of a cannabis drug licence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacies in the province in which the
    practitioner is entitled to practise;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all pharmacies in an adjacent province in which a
    prescription or order from the practitioner who is named in the
    notice may be filled;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the applicable professional licensing authority for
    the province in which the practitioner is entitled to practise; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} on request, the applicable provincial professional
    licensing authority for any province other than the province
    referred to in paragraph (e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be issued if the practitioner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made a request to the Minister in accordance with
    subsection (1) to issue the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contravened a rule of conduct, in relation to
    cannabis, established by the provincial professional licensing
    authority for the province in which the practitioner is practising
    and that provincial professional licensing authority has requested
    the Minister in writing to issue the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} been convicted of any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a designated offence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a controlled substance offence in relation to
      cannabis,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an offence under the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
    the practitioner has contravened this Part or Part 9.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue the notice to the persons and
  authorities referred to in subsection (2) if the Minister, on
  reasonable grounds, believes that the practitioner named in the notice

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has contravened section 183, subsection 184(1)
    or (2) or section 185;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, self-administered a
    prescription drug under a self-directed prescription or order or, in
    the absence of a prescription or order, contrary to accepted
    professional practice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, distributed or
    administered a prescription drug to their spouse, common-law
    partner, parent or child, including a child adopted in fact,
    contrary to accepted professional practice; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is unable to account for the quantity of
    prescription drugs for which the practitioner was responsible under
    this Part or Part 9.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prerequisite

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing a notice under subsection (4), the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consult with the applicable provincial professional
    licensing authority for the province in which the practitioner to
    whom the notice relates is entitled to practise;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} send to the practitioner a written notice that
    indicates the reasons why a notice is being considered and give them
    an opportunity to present reasons why the notice should not be
    issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consider

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any reasons that have been presented by the
      practitioner under paragraph (b),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the compliance history of the practitioner in
      respect of the Act and its regulations, the [Controlled Drugs and
      Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) and the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) with respect to
      cannabis, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} whether the actions of the practitioner pose a
      significant risk to public health or public safety, including the
      risk of any prescription drug being diverted to an illicit market
      or activity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retraction of notice

- **[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must retract a notice that was issued under subsection 189(2)
  or (4) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the practitioner who is named in the notice has
    requested in writing that it be retracted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the practitioner has provided a letter from the
    applicable provincial professional licensing authority for the
    province in which the practitioner is entitled to practise that
    states that the authority consents to the retraction of the notice;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the notice was issued at the
    request of the practitioner, one year has elapsed since the notice
    was issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to notify

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister retracts a notice, the Minister must
  notify, in writing, the persons and authorities to which the notice
  was issued under subsection 189(2) or (4).

#### [Hospitals]{.HTitleText3} {#h-849238 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

**[[191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-191 .sectionLabel}** An individual who is in
charge of a hospital must retain a record of the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any prescription drug received by the
  hospital,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name and quantity of the drug,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person from whom the
    drug was received, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date the drug was received; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a prescription drug that is dispensed
  by the hospital,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the patient for whom or animal for
    which the drug was dispensed,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the practitioner who ordered or
    prescribed the drug, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the drug was ordered or
    prescribed and the form and quantity of the drug.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security obligations

**[[192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-192 .sectionLabel}** An individual in charge
of a hospital must, with respect to a drug containing cannabis for which
the hospital is responsible,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect it against theft or
  loss; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, within 10 days after
  becoming aware of its theft or loss.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, distribution and administration

- **[[193]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-193
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, it is prohibited for a person to sell, distribute
  or administer a prescription drug that a hospital possesses without
  the authorization of the individual in charge of the hospital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}In--patients and out-patients

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The individual in charge of a hospital may authorize
  the sale, distribution or administration of a prescription drug only
  in accordance with a prescription or a written order, signed and dated
  by a practitioner and only

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of administration, to an individual or
    an animal under treatment as an in-patient or out-patient of the
    hospital; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of sale or distribution, for the
    individual referred to in paragraph (a) or to the person in charge
    of the animal referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution or sale in an emergency

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), for emergency purposes, the
  individual in charge of a hospital may authorize the sale or
  distribution of a prescription drug without a prescription to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an employee of or a practitioner in another
    hospital, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the drug is sold or distributed in accordance
      with a written order signed and dated by a pharmacist in charge of
      the pharmacy in the other hospital or by a practitioner who is
      authorized by the individual in charge of the other hospital to
      sign the order, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the signature of the pharmacist or the
      practitioner is known to the person who sells or distributes the
      drug or has been verified by that person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pharmacist of another hospital, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the drug is sold or distributed in accordance
      with a written order signed and dated by the pharmacist, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the signature of the pharmacist or the
      practitioner who is authorized by the individual in charge of the
      other hospital to sign the order is known to the person who sells
      or distributes the drug or has been verified by that person; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is exempted under section 140 of
    the Act with respect to the drug and who is employed in a research
    laboratory in the hospital for the purpose of research.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return and destruction

- **[[194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-194
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 193(2), the
  individual in charge of a hospital may authorize the sale or
  distribution of a prescription drug in accordance with a written order
  to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a cannabis drug licence from which
    the drug was received, if the drug is sold or distributed for the
    purpose of its return; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a holder of a cannabis drug licence or a licensed
    dealer, if the drug is sold or distributed for the purpose of its
    destruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written Order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The written order must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, the quantity and the strength per unit of
    the drug; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sale or distribution is for the purpose of
    its destruction, a written statement that affirms that the sole
    purpose of the order is the destruction of the drug and that is
    signed and dated by, or on behalf of, the holder of the cannabis
    drug licence or the licensed dealer, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The individual in charge of the hospital must prepare
  a record, with respect to the drug for the purpose of its return or
  destruction, that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its brand name, form, quantity and strength per
    unit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and the address of the person to whom it
    was sold or distributed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it was sold or distributed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

**[[195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-195 .sectionLabel}** An individual in charge
of a hospital must retain the records that the individual is required to
retain under these Regulations for at least two years after the day on
which they are prepared.

## [PART 9]{.HLabel1}[Combination Products and Devices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-849285 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[brand name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brand name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
    commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[combination product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[combination product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2019-206, s. 54\]]{.Repealed}

[[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection C.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
    usuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 54](#1143421-1204715){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143421-1204715.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-196-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 196"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

**[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person may promote a combination
product by communicating the brand name, proper name, common name, price
or quantity of the product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- section 21 of Act

**[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** A person is exempt from
the application of section 21 of the Act if they display, refer to or
otherwise use the brand name of a combination product, or the name of
the holder of the drug identification number assigned in accordance with
subsection C.01.014.2(1) of the [Food and Drug
Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for the product, directly
or indirectly in a promotion that is used in the sponsorship of a
person, entity, event, activity or facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- section 22 of Act

**[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** A person is exempt from
the application of section 22 of the Act if they display on a facility,
as part of the name of the facility or otherwise, if the facility is
used for a sports or cultural event or activity, the brand name of a
combination product or the name of the holder of the drug identification
number assigned in accordance with subsection C.01.014.2(1) of the [Food
and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for the product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- subsection 23(1) of Act

**[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** A person is exempt from
the application of subsection 23(1) of the Act if they publish,
broadcast or otherwise disseminate, on behalf of another person, with or
without consideration, any promotion that is authorized under sections
197 to 199.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- combination product for
veterinary use

**[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the other
provisions of these Regulations, a person that is authorized to sell a
combination product for veterinary use may sell it in a package or with
a label that sets out a depiction of a real or fictional animal that
corresponds to the species of the animal for which the product is
intended.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- combination product

- **[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a person is authorized to sell a combination
  product to a young person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- device

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of these Regulations,
  a person is authorized to sell a device licensed for use with a
  prescription drug to a young person if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has ascertained that there is a written
    prescription for the young person with respect to the drug; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is entitled under the laws of a province to
    dispense a prescription drug.

## [PART 10]{.HLabel1}[Importation and Exportation for Medical or Scientific Purposes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-849307 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849308 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[203]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[customs office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customs office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 2(1) of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6). ([bureau de
    douane]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[customs officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customs officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
    the definition [[officer ]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1)
    of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6). ([agent des
    douanes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Importation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849313 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import permit

- **[[204]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-204 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence is authorized to import cannabis for medical or
  scientific purposes if they also hold an import permit for each
  shipment of cannabis that is imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of an import permit is also authorized to
  possess, transfer, transport, send or deliver the shipment of cannabis
  to the extent necessary to import the cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import permit --- content

**[[205]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205 .sectionLabel}** The import permit must
set out the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and mailing address of the holder;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the permit number and the licence number;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the shipment of cannabis to be
  imported,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the brand name of the cannabis,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} quantity of the cannabis, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of THC w/w and CBD w/w of the
    cannabis, except in the case of cannabis plants and cannabis plant
    seeds;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the exporter in the country
  of export;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 59\]]{.Repealed}

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 59\]]{.Repealed}

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} each mode of transportation used, the country of
  export and, if applicable, any country of transit or transhipment;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} any conditions that the Minister considers
  appropriate;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the permit; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 55(E)](#1143425-1204718){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143425-1204718.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 59](#1514992-1524165){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514992-1524165.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 205"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue --- other grounds

**[[206]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-206 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 62(7)(h) of the Act, other grounds for refusing to issue an
import permit are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not hold a licence; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipment to which the permit application
    pertains would contravene these Regulations or the laws of the
    country of export or any country of transit or transhipment, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the importation of the cannabis is for the purpose
    of exporting it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

**[[207]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-207 .sectionLabel}** An import permit is
valid until the earliest of the following dates:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is imported,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the permit or the date of its
  revocation,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the licence referred to in
  paragraph 205(b) or the date of its revocation, or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of revocation of the permit for exportation
  issued by the competent authority of the country of export that
  pertains to the shipment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of copy of import permit

**[[208]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-208 .sectionLabel}** A holder of an import
permit must provide a copy of the import permit to the customs office at
the time of importation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[209]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209 .sectionLabel}** The holder of an import
permit must, within 15 days after the date of release of a shipment of
cannabis in Canada, provide the Minister with the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name, the number of the licence referred to in
  paragraph 205(b) and the import permit number issued in respect of the
  shipment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of release of the shipment; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the shipment of cannabis that is
  imported,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the brand name of the cannabis,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the cannabis, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of THC w/w and CBD w/w of the
    cannabis, except in the case of cannabis plants and cannabis plant
    seeds.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 56(E)](#1143427-1204721){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143427-1204721.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 209"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation of imported cannabis

**[[210]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-210 .sectionLabel}** The holder of an import
permit must ensure that, after the imported cannabis is released, it is
transported directly to the site set out in the licence referred to in
paragraph 205(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- other circumstances

**[[211]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-211 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 65(h) of the Act, other circumstances for the revocation of an
import permit are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder has requested, in writing, the
  revocation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence referred to in paragraph 205(b) has been
  revoked;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the importation of the cannabis is for the purpose of
  exporting it; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a permit that has been suspended is not reinstated
  because the reasons for the suspension still exist or the permit
  holder has not demonstrated to the Minister that the suspension is
  unfounded.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

**[[212]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-212 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, for
the purpose of verifying whether an importation of cannabis complies
with these Regulations, provide to a customs officer any information
provided in the import permit application or referred to in sections 205
and 209 and inform that customs officer whether the import permit has
been suspended or revoked.

### [Exportation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849370 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Export permit

- **[[213]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-213 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence is authorized to export cannabis for medical or
  scientific purposes if they also hold an export permit for each
  shipment of cannabis that is exported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of an export permit is also authorized to
  possess, transfer, transport, send, deliver or sell the shipment of
  cannabis to the extent necessary to export the cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Export permit --- content

**[[214]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-214 .sectionLabel}** The export permit must
set out the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and mailing address of the holder;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the permit number and the licence number;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the shipment of cannabis to be
  exported,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the intended use of the cannabis,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the brand name of the cannabis,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the cannabis, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of THC w/w and CBD w/w of the
    cannabis, except in the case of cannabis plants and cannabis plant
    seeds;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the importer in the country
  of import;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the country of import and, if applicable, any country
  of transit or transhipment;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the address of the customs office, sufferance
  warehouse or bonded warehouse to which the shipment is to be
  delivered, if applicable;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} each mode of transportation used;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} any conditions that the Minister considers
  appropriate;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the permit; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 57(E)](#1143429-1204724){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143429-1204724.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 60](#1514993-1524167){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514993-1524167.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-214-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 214"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue --- other grounds

**[[215]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-215 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 62(7)(h) of the Act, other grounds for refusing to issue an
export permit are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not hold a licence; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  the shipment to which the permit application pertains

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} would contravene these Regulations or the laws of
    the country of import or any country of transit or transhipment, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} would not comply with the permit for importation
    issued by a competent authority of the country of import.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

**[[216]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-216 .sectionLabel}** An export permit is
valid until the earliest of the following dates:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is exported,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the permit or the date of its
  revocation,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the licence referred to in
  paragraph 214(b) or the date of its revocation, or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the permit for importation
  issued by the competent authority of the country of import that
  pertains to the shipment or the date of its revocation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of copy of export permit

**[[217]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-217 .sectionLabel}** A holder of an export
permit must provide a copy of the export permit to the customs office at
the time of exportation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[218]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-218 .sectionLabel}** The holder of an export
permit must, within 15 days after the date of exportation of a shipment
of cannabis, provide the Minister with the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name and the number of the licence referred to
  in paragraph 214(b) and the export permit number issued in respect of
  the shipment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of exportation of the shipment; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the shipment of cannabis that is
  exported,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} intended use of the cannabis,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the brand name of the cannabis,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the cannabis, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of THC w/w and CBD w/w of the
    cannabis, except in the case of cannabis plants and cannabis plant
    seeds.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 58(E)](#1143431-1204727){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143431-1204727.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-218-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 218"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- other circumstances

**[[219]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-219 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 65(h) of the Act, other circumstances for the revocation of an
export permit are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder has requested, in writing, the
  revocation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence referred to in paragraph 214(b) has been
  revoked; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a permit that has been suspended is not reinstated
  because the reasons for the suspension still exist or the permit
  holder has not demonstrated to the Minister that the suspension is
  unfounded.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

**[[220]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-220 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, for
the purpose of verifying whether an exportation of cannabis complies
with these Regulations, provide to a customs officer any information
provided in the export permit application or referred to in sections 214
and 218 and inform that customs officer whether the export permit has
been suspended or revoked.

## [PART 11]{.HLabel1}[Retention of Documents and Information]{.HTitleText1} {#h-849424 .Part}

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849425 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of retention

**[[221]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-221 .sectionLabel}** A person that is
required to retain a document or information --- or that must ensure
that a document or information is retained --- under these Regulations
must ensure that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the document or information is retained in a manner
  that will enable an audit of it to be made in a timely manner;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person holds a licence, the document or
  information is available at the site specified in the licence; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person does not hold a licence, the document
  or information is available at their place of business in Canada or,
  if they do not have such a place of business, at a place of business
  in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to continue to retain

**[[222]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-222 .sectionLabel}** If a person ceases to
hold a licence, they must, in respect of any document or information
that they were required to retain under these Regulations and for which
the retention period has not yet ended,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the document or information continues to
  be retained until the end of the retention period; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the Minister with notice in writing of the
  address of the location at which the document or information is
  retained and of any subsequent change to the address.

### [Notices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849435 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of notices

**[[223]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-223 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence,
if they send or provide a notice under these Regulations and are not
otherwise required to retain a copy, must retain a copy for at least two
years after the day on which the notice is sent or provided.

### [Inventory and Distribution]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849438 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inventory

- **[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must retain, for each lot or batch of cannabis ---
  other than a cannabis extract, a cannabis topical or edible cannabis
  --- that they produce, a document that contains the following
  information, as applicable:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which cannabis plants are propagated by
    means other than sowing seeds and the number of new plants
    propagated in this manner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which cannabis plant seeds are sown and
    the number of seeds sown on that date;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which cannabis is harvested and its net
    weight on that date;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which drying processes are completed
    for the cannabis and its net weight on that date;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date on which dried or fresh cannabis is put
    into a discrete unit form, the net weight of cannabis in each unit
    and the number of units;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date on which cannabis that is not of a class
    of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to the Act is produced and its net
    weight or volume on that date; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of cannabis plants or cannabis
    plant seeds, any information that is obtained through testing and
    that relates to the phytocannabinoid and terpene content of the
    cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence must retain, for each lot or
  batch of cannabis --- other than a cannabis extract, a cannabis
  topical or edible cannabis --- that they package, a document that
  contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
    applicable, the brand name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis is packaged and its
    net weight on that date; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drug containing cannabis, the
    strength per unit of the drug.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The documents must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which they are prepared.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 59](#1143433-1204735){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143433-1204735.html"}
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 61](#1514995-1524169){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1514995-1524169.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-224-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 224"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inventory --- cannabis extract, etc.

- **[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must retain, for each lot or batch of cannabis
  extract, cannabis topical or edible cannabis that they produce, a
  document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of production and the net weight or volume
    of the cannabis extract, cannabis topical or edible cannabis on that
    date;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the date on which the cannabis
    extract, cannabis topical or edible cannabis is put into a discrete
    unit form, the net weight or volume of each unit and the number of
    units;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the cannabis that is used to produce
    the cannabis extract, cannabis topical or edible cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its description,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its net weight or volume,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} its lot or batch number, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it was produced;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis extract, cannabis topical or edible
    cannabis is or will become a cannabis product or is or will be
    contained in a cannabis accessory that is or will become a cannabis
    product,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the list of ingredients that is required to
      appear on the label of the cannabis product, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, net volume or concentration by
      weight or volume of each of those ingredients;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis extract, cannabis topical or
    edible cannabis is not referred to in paragraph (d) but is or will
    be distributed in the conduct of non-therapeutic research on
    cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the list of ingredients that would be required to
      appear on the label if the cannabis extract, cannabis topical or
      edible cannabis were a cannabis product or were contained in a
      cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the net weight, net volume or concentration by
      weight or volume of each of those ingredients;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis extract is or will become a
    cannabis product or is or will be contained in a cannabis accessory
    that is or will become a cannabis product,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether each ingredient that is
      required to appear on the label of the cannabis product is a
      carrier substance, flavouring agent or substance that is necessary
      to maintain the quality or stability of the cannabis product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any additional information in the possession of
      the holder that relates to the purpose of each ingredient, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the flavour, if any, of the
      cannabis product;

  - [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis extract is not referred to in
    paragraph (e) but is or will be distributed in the conduct of
    non-therapeutic research on cannabis,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether each ingredient that
      would be required to appear on the label if the cannabis extract
      were a cannabis product or were contained in a cannabis accessory
      that is a cannabis product is a carrier substance, flavouring
      agent or substance that is necessary to maintain the quality or
      stability of the cannabis extract,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any additional information in the possession of
      the holder that relates to the purpose of each ingredient, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the flavour, if any, of the
      cannabis extract; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any information that is obtained through testing
    and that relates to the phytocannabinoid and terpene content of the
    cannabis extract, cannabis topical or edible cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subparagraph (1)(d)(ii) or
  (d.1)(ii)

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The document is not required to contain the
  information referred to in subparagraph (1)(d)(ii) or (d.1)(ii), as
  the case may be, in respect of an ingredient if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ingredient is part of a mixture of substances
    that was used in the production of cannabis referred to in paragraph
    (1)(d) or (d.1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder obtained the mixture from another
    person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information has not been disclosed to the
    holder;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the holder has made the necessary arrangements to
    ensure that the information will be provided to the Minister if,
    within the retention period referred to in subsection (3), the
    Minister requires the holder to provide it; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the document contains the net weight or volume of
    the mixture at the time it was used to produce the cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subparagraph (1)(e)(i) or
  (e.1)(i)

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The document is not required to contain the
  information referred to in subparagraph (1)(e)(i) or (e.1)(i), as the
  case may be, in respect of an ingredient if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirements in paragraphs (1.1)(a) to (d) are
    met; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder includes in the document an indication
    of whether the mixture referred to in paragraph (1.1)(a) contains
    carrier substances, flavouring agents, substances that are necessary
    to maintain the quality or stability of the cannabis, or a
    combination of any of these.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence must retain, for each lot or
  batch of cannabis extract, cannabis topical or edible cannabis that
  they package, a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis extract, cannabis
    topical or edible cannabis, including the brand name, if applicable;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis extract, cannabis
    topical or edible cannabis is packaged and its net weight or volume
    on that date; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drug containing cannabis, the
    strength per unit of the drug.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The documents must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which they are prepared.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), in the case of cannabis that
  was distributed to human participants in the conduct of
  non-therapeutic research on cannabis, the holder of the licence for
  research that distributed the cannabis must retain the documents for
  at least two years after the day on which the research ends.

::: HistoricalNote

  ajax-after="1143440-1204770.html"}

  ajax-after="1381784-1382999.html"}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-225-20191017.html "Link to previous version of section 225"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis obtained from another person

- **[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must, if they obtain cannabis from another person,
  retain a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person from which the cannabis is
    obtained;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the cannabis
    is obtained and, if that location is different from the site or
    sites at which the cannabis was produced, the address of the site or
    sites, if known;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis is obtained;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is obtained;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
    applicable, the brand name;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the lot or batch number of the
    cannabis;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drug containing cannabis, the form
    of the drug and its strength per unit; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cannabis plants, cannabis plant
    seeds or cannabis that is not of a class of cannabis set out in
    Schedule 4 to the Act, the intended use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which it is prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Things to be used as ingredients

- **[[226.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing
  must, if they obtain or produce anything that will be used as an
  ingredient to produce a cannabis extract, a cannabis topical or edible
  cannabis, retain a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and business address of the person, if
    any, that supplies the thing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the holder takes possession of
    the thing or, if the thing is produced by the holder, the date on
    which production is completed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the thing, including the name by
    which it is generally known and, if applicable,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its chemical name,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its common name, if that name is not the name by
      which it is generally known,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} its INCI name, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} its CAS registry number; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any lot code or other unique identifier that
    enables the thing to be traced.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which it is prepared.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in paragraph (1)(c).

  [[CAS registry number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CAS registry number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      identification number assigned to a chemical by the Chemical
      Abstracts Service, a division of the American Chemical
      Society. ([numéro d'enregistrement CAS]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([nom
      usuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[INCI name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[INCI name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Cosmetic
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._869). ([appellation
      INCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 62](#1143444-1204802){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143444-1204802.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, distribution and export of cannabis

- **[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, if they sell, distribute or export cannabis, must
  retain a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to which it is sold,
    distributed or exported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location from which it is sold,
    distributed or exported and the place to which it is sent or
    delivered;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it is sold, distributed or
    exported;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity that is sold, distributed or exported;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
    applicable, the brand name;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} its lot or batch number;

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 62\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drug containing cannabis, the form
    of the drug and its strength per unit;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cannabis plants, cannabis plant
    seeds or cannabis that is not of a class of cannabis set out in
    Schedule 4 to the Act, the intended use, if known; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis accessory that is a
    cannabis product, a description of the cannabis accessory.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The obligation set out in subsection (1) does not
  apply if the cannabis is sold or distributed to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has placed a purchase order for
    it under subsection 289(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual, other than an individual referred to
    in paragraph (a), who does not hold a licence and who is obtaining
    the cannabis for their personal use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which it is prepared.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 63](#1143446-1204805){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143446-1204805.html"}

  ajax-after="1514997-1524171.html"}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Antimicrobial treatment

- **[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, if they conduct antimicrobial treatment of
  cannabis at a location other than the site specified in the licence,
  must retain a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
    applicable, the brand name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis leaves the site
    specified in the licence and the quantity that leaves the site;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person that receives the cannabis
    at the location where the treatment is to be conducted;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location referred to in
    paragraph (c);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person from which the cannabis is
    received after the treatment;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the address of the site to which the cannabis is
    returned, or of the location to which it is distributed, after the
    treatment; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis is received at the
    site or location referred to in paragraph (f) and the quantity that
    is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which it is prepared.

### [Destruction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849511 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of cannabis

- **[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence other than a cannabis drug licence, if they
  destroy cannabis or cause it to be destroyed, must retain a document
  that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
    applicable, the brand name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis is destroyed and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if whole cannabis plants are destroyed, the
      number of plants destroyed on that date, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the pre-destruction net
      weight or volume of the cannabis on that date; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the employee referred to in paragraph
    43(1)(b) who witnesses the destruction.

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 63\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 63\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement by witness

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must obtain, for each instance in which
  cannabis is destroyed, a statement signed and dated by the employee
  referred to in paragraph 43(1)(b) stating that they witnessed the
  destruction and that the cannabis was destroyed in accordance with a
  method referred to in paragraph 43(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The document referred to in subsection (1) and the
  statement referred to in subsection (2) must be retained for at least
  two years after the day on which the cannabis is destroyed, except if
  they relate to cannabis destroyed under section 28.5, in which case
  they must be retained for at least two years after the day on which
  the non-therapeutic research on cannabis ends.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in cases where a
  holder of a licence destroys cultivation waste.
