### [Security]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849524 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organizational security plan

**[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must retain a copy of any organizational security plan that they submit
to the Minister --- including any updated plan referred to in section 45
--- for at least two years after the day on which the plan is replaced
by an updated plan or, if the plan has not been replaced, at least two
years after the day on which the licence expires or is revoked.

### [Production]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849527 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production

- **[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence other than a cannabis drug licence must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each lot or batch of cannabis any portion of
    which has been sold or exported, retain a document demonstrating
    that the cannabis and anything that was used as an ingredient was
    produced, packaged, labelled, distributed, stored, sampled and
    tested in accordance with the applicable provisions of Parts 5 and
    6;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, maintain a list of the brand names
    of cannabis --- of any class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to
    the Act --- that the holder has produced, packaged, labelled,
    distributed, stored, sampled or tested;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of each instance in which a substance
    --- including a pest control product and a fertilizer but excluding
    water --- is applied directly or indirectly to cannabis, retain a
    document that contains the name of that substance, as well as the
    date on which it was applied;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the testing conducted under Part 5 or
    to meet the requirements set out in Part 6,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} maintain a document that describes the validated
      methods used, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for each lot or batch of cannabis that is
      tested, retain a document that contains the test results;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for processing, retain

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a document that describes the qualifications of
      the quality assurance person --- and of any alternate quality
      assurance person --- in respect of the matters referred to in
      subsection 19(1), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that describes every investigation
      conducted under paragraph 19(2)(b) or (c) and any measures taken
      under that paragraph; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for analytical testing,
    retain a document that describes the qualifications of the head of
    laboratory in respect of the matters referred to in subsection
    23(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-therapeutic research on cannabis

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for research must also
  retain a document demonstrating that each lot or batch of cannabis
  that the holder produced or received from another holder of a licence
  for research and that has been distributed to human participants in
  the conduct of non-therapeutic research on cannabis, and anything that
  was used as an ingredient, was produced, packaged, labelled,
  distributed, stored, sampled or tested in accordance with sections
  28.1 and 28.2 and the provisions of Parts 5 and 6, if applicable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention periods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following documents must be retained for the
  following periods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a document referred to in paragraph (1)(a), for at
    least two years after the day on which the last sale or export of
    any portion of the lot or batch takes place;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document referred to in paragraph (1)(c), for at
    least two years after the day on which it is prepared;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document referred to in subparagraph (1)(d)(ii),
    for at least two years after the day on which the last sale or
    export of any portion of the lot or batch takes place;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a document referred to in subparagraph (1)(e)(i) or
    paragraph (f), for the period during which the quality assurance
    person, the alternate quality assurance person or the head of
    laboratory acts in that capacity and at least two years after the
    day on which they cease to do so;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a document referred to in subparagraph (1)(e)(ii),
    for at least two years after the day on which it is prepared; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a document referred to in subsection (1.1), for at
    least two years after the day on which the non-therapeutic research
    on cannabis ends.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention periods --- previous versions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each version of the list referred to in paragraph
    (1)(b), for at least two years after the day on which it is replaced
    by a new version or, if it has not been replaced, at least two years
    after the day on which the licence expires or is revoked; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each version of the document referred to in
    subparagraph (1)(d)(i), for at least two years after the day on
    which the validated methods are replaced or, if the methods have not
    been replaced, two years after the day on which the licence expires
    or is revoked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standard operating procedures and
sanitation program

- **[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence other than a cannabis drug licence must maintain
  documentation describing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the standard operating procedures referred to in
    section 80 that are in use at the site set out in the licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sanitation program referred to in section 87
    that is in use at the site set out in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain each version of the
  documentation for at least two years after the day on which it is
  replaced by a new version or, if it has not been replaced, at least
  two years after the day on which the licence expires or is revoked.

### [Packaging and Labelling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849562 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packages and labels

**[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
other than a cannabis drug licence must retain the following samples and
copies for at least two years after the day on which they are made:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a sample or copy of each distinct package for a
  cannabis product that the holder makes available for sale; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of each distinct label that relates to a
  cannabis product that the holder makes available for sale.

### [Cannabis Accessories]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849567 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis accessories

**[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must maintain a list of the names and types of the cannabis accessories
that they sell and must retain each version of the list for at least two
years after the day on which it is replaced by a new version or, if it
has not been replaced, at least two years after the day on which the
licence expires or is revoked.

### [System of Control for Recalls]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849570 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}System of control

- **[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, other than a licence for analytical testing or a
  cannabis drug licence must retain, for each lot or batch of cannabis
  that they sell or distribute, a document that contains the information
  that is necessary for the system of control referred to in subsection
  46(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which the last sale or distribution of any portion of
  the lot or batch takes place, other than for destruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documentation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder must maintain documentation concerning the
  system of control and retain each version of the documentation for at
  least two years after the day on which it is replaced by a new version
  or, if it has not been replaced, at least two years after the day on
  which the licence expires or is revoked.

- [SOR/2019-206, s. 66](#1143459-1204817){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143459-1204817.html"}
:::

### [Promotion]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849578 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Promotion

**[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
must retain

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 65\]]{.Repealed}

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a document that contains the
  information provided to the Minister under subsection 43(4) of the Act
  for at least two years after the day on which the information was
  provided; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sample or copy of any promotional materials for at
  least two years after the day on which the promotion ended.

- [SOR/2025-43, s. 65](#1515007-1524189){.wb-lbx

### [Research and Development]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849584 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Research and development

- **[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence, if they undertake research and development
  activities, must retain a document that contains the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any cannabis that is used in the
    activities,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its description, including, if applicable, its
      brand name,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity used and, if applicable, the lot or
      batch number,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it is used, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the purpose and a brief description of the
      activity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any cannabis that is produced in the
    course of the activities,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its description,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity produced,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it is produced,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the date on which it is used for
      testing and the quantity used, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the date on which it is placed in
      inventory intended for sale and the quantity placed in inventory;
      and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other information that can be used to reconcile
    the quantities of cannabis referred to in paragraphs (a) and (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-therapeutic research on cannabis

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for research must also
  retain, in relation to any non-therapeutic research on cannabis that
  the holder conducts, the following documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the documents submitted to the Minister
    in relation to that research;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the documents produced in relation to
    that research; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of any document required by licence
    conditions that the Minister has imposed under subsection 62(10) of
    the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period --- general rule

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document referred to in subsection (1) that does
  not relate to non-therapeutic research on cannabis must be retained
  for at least two years after the day on which it is prepared.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period --- non-therapeutic
  research on cannabis

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of these Regulations that
  specifies a retention period and except in the case of reports and
  records referred to in section 248.2, every document that the holder
  of a licence for research must retain under these Regulations in
  relation to non-therapeutic research on cannabis that the holder
  conducts or in relation to cannabis that is distributed in the conduct
  of such research must be retained for at least two years after the day
  on which the non-therapeutic research on cannabis ends.

  ajax-after="1381806-1383021.html"}

### [Limit for Micro-processing Licence]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849602 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit --- micro-processing licence

**[[238]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-238 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for micro-processing must maintain documentation that demonstrates that
they comply with the limit set out in subsection 21(1) and retain each
version of the documentation for at least two years after the day on
which it is replaced by a new version or, if it has not been replaced,
for at least two years after the day on which the licence expires or is
revoked.

### [Import and Export]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849605 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import of cannabis

**[[239]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239 .sectionLabel}** A holder or former
holder of an import permit must retain a document that contains the
information that they provided to the Minister under section 209 ---
together with a copy of the relevant export permit that was issued by a
competent authority in the country of export --- for at least two years
after the day on which the information is provided.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Export of cannabis

**[[240]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-240 .sectionLabel}** A holder or former
holder of an export permit must retain a document that contains the
information that they provided to the Minister under section 218 ---
together with a copy of the relevant import permit issued by a competent
authority in the country of final destination --- for at least two years
after the day on which the information is provided.

### [Key Investors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849610 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of key investors

- **[[241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for cultivation, processing or sale must maintain
  a record that contains the following information in respect of each
  key investor:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the key investor's name and mailing address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the means by which the
    key investor exercises, or is in a position to exercise, control
    over the holder;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} details regarding the transaction by virtue of
    which the key investor became such an investor, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if they provided money directly or indirectly to
      the holder, the amount provided, the date on which it was provided
      and the terms and conditions under which it was provided, and if
      the money was provided in the form of a loan, the interest rate
      and term, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they provided goods or services directly or
      indirectly to the holder, a description of the goods or services,
      their fair market value at the time they were provided, the date
      on which they were provided and the terms and conditions under
      which they were provided;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} details regarding each instance in which the key
    investor provides money, goods or services directly or indirectly to
    the holder, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of money, the amount provided, the
      date on which it is provided and the terms and conditions under
      which it is provided, and if the money is provided in the form of
      a loan, the interest rate and term, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods or services, a description
      of the goods or services, their fair market value at the time at
      which they are provided, the date on which they are provided and
      the terms and conditions under which they are provided;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} details regarding any benefit that the key investor
    receives from the holder as a result of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} having provided money, goods or services to the
      holder, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} holding an ownership interest or other right or
      interest in, or in respect of, a business operated by the holder
      or, if the holder is an organization, in or in respect of the
      organization; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} details regarding each instance in which money is
    repaid or goods are returned to the key investor, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of money, the amount repaid and the
      date on which it is repaid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods, a description of the
      goods, their fair market value at the time they are returned and
      the date on which they are returned.

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 66\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 66\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-43, s. 66\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- published market

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a holder that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is an organization whose equity securities are, or
    a class of those securities is, listed on a published market; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is wholly owned by an organization whose equity
    securities are, or a class of those securities is, listed on a
    published market.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- key investors before
  issuance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder is not required to include the information
  referred to in paragraph (1)(c) in respect of a key investor that
  became such an investor before the day on which the licence was
  issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- continued licences

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the licence has been continued in force by virtue
  of subsection 158(1) of the Act, the holder is not required to include
  the information referred to in paragraph (1)(c) in respect of a key
  investor that became such an investor before the day on which these
  Regulations come into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements regarding content

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The holder must not delete any information from the
  record and must indicate the effective date of the event in respect of
  which new information is added to the record.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former key investors

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the information that has been
  included in the record in respect of a key investor must continue to
  be retained in the record even if the person ceases to be such an
  investor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual reporting

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The holder must, no later than January 31 in any
  given year, provide the Minister with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the record; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if any person ceased to be a key investor during
    the previous calendar year, a document containing details regarding
    how and when they ceased to be such an investor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance and retention of record

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The holder must ensure that the record is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} maintained in a manner that will enable an audit of
    it to be made in a timely manner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} available at the site specified in the licence; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} retained for at least two years after the day on
    which the holder ceases to be required to maintain it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[key investor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[key investor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of the
      holder of a licence, a person that exercises, or is in a position
      to exercise, direct or indirect control over the holder by virtue
      of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} having provided money, goods or services
        directly or indirectly to the holder; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} holding an ownership interest or other right or
        interest in, or in respect of, a business operated by the holder
        or, if the holder is an organization, in or in respect of the
        organization. ([investisseur-clé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[published market]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[published market]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a market inside or
      outside Canada on which equity securities are traded, if the
      prices at which the securities are traded are regularly published
      either electronically or in a newspaper or financial or business
      publication of general circulation. ([marché
      publié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [SOR/2025-43, s. 66](#1515011-1524196){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1515011-1524196.html"}

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Reporting and Disclosure]{.HTitleText1} {#h-849656 .Part}

### [Documents and Information Provided to Minister]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849657 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and manner

**[[242]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-242 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided in these Regulations, documents that are required to be
provided to the Minister under this Part, Part 8, subsection 241(7) or
section 297 must be provided in the form and manner specified in the
document entitled Form and Manner Requirements -- Documents Provided to
the Minister for the Purposes of the Cannabis Act, as amended from time
to time and published by the Government of Canada on its website.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 67](#1143461-1204821){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143461-1204821.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-242-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 242"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request by Minister

- **[[243]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-243 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person that is required to ensure the retention of documents or
  information under these Regulations and that is not authorized under
  the Act to conduct activities in relation to cannabis may be required
  by the Minister to provide the documents or information to the
  Minister if the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that the
  documents are, or the information is, necessary to address an issue of
  public health or public safety or to verify compliance or prevent
  non-compliance with the provisions of the Act or of these Regulations.
  If the person is required to provide the documents or information to
  the Minister, they must do so as soon as feasible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import and export permits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that has ceased to hold a licence and that
  is required to retain documents --- including copies of permits ---
  under section 239 or 240 may be required by the Minister to provide
  the documents to the Minister if the Minister has reasonable grounds
  to believe that they are necessary for a purpose specified in
  subsection (1). If the person is required to provide the documents to
  the Minister, they must do so as soon as feasible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- urgent issues

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A document or information that must be provided under
  subsection (1) or (2) to address an issue of public health or public
  safety must be provided without delay if the Minister has reasonable
  grounds to believe that the issue must be addressed urgently and so
  indicates in the request.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- new cannabis product

- **[[244]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for processing must, at least 60 days before
  making available for sale a cannabis product --- except dried
  cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds ---
  that they have not previously sold in Canada, provide the Minister
  with a written notice that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to the
    Act to which the cannabis product belongs;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis product, including
    the brand name; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis product is expected
    to be made available for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain a copy of the notice for at
  least two years after the date referred to in paragraph (1)(c).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-43, s. 67](#1515015-1524203){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1515015-1524203.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-244-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 244"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[245]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-245 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2025-43, s. 68\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-245-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 245"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Theft or loss of cannabis

- **[[246]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence other than a cannabis drug licence must, if they
  experience a theft of cannabis or a loss of cannabis that cannot be
  explained on the basis of normally accepted business activities,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify a police force within 24 hours after
    becoming aware of its theft or loss; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the Minister with a written notice within
    10 days after becoming aware of its theft or loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain a copy of the notice provided
  to the Minister for at least two years after the day by which the
  Minister must be notified.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voluntary recall

- **[[247]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence must, before commencing a voluntary recall of a
  cannabis product that has been sold or distributed in Canada, provide
  the Minister with a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis product, including
    the brand name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of each lot or batch of the cannabis
    product to be recalled, together with, if known, the number of any
    lot or batch of cannabis that was used to make the cannabis product;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if known, the name and address of each person that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} produced or imported into Canada the cannabis
      that is, or is contained in, the cannabis product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} packaged or labelled the cannabis referred to in
      subparagraph (i) before it became, or became part of, the cannabis
      product,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis accessory that is a
      cannabis product, produced or imported into Canada the cannabis
      accessory or any component of it, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} packaged or labelled the cannabis product;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for commencing the recall;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis that is, or is contained in, the
    cannabis product was produced or imported into Canada by the holder,
    the quantity of cannabis that was produced or imported;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the cannabis product that was sold
    or distributed by the holder in Canada;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the quantity of the cannabis product
    that is affected by the problem or potential problem underlying the
    recall and that remains in the possession of the holder;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the number of persons to which the holder sold or
    distributed the cannabis product in Canada;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the period during which the holder sold or
    distributed the cannabis product in Canada;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the time and manner in which the recall is to be
    carried out, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the expected date for the commencement of the
      recall,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} how and when the Minister will be informed of
      the progress of the recall, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date by which the recall is expected to be
      completed;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a description of any other measure that the holder
    is taking, or intends to take, in respect of the recall; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} contact information for a representative who will
    be responsible for the recall.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall --- exported cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence must, before commencing a
  voluntary recall of cannabis that has been exported from Canada,
  provide the Minister with a document that contains the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis, including, if
    applicable, the brand name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of each lot or batch of the cannabis;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if known, the name and address of each person that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} produced or imported into Canada the cannabis,
      and, if applicable, packaged or labelled it, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the cannabis is contained in a
      cannabis accessory, produced or imported into Canada the cannabis
      accessory or any component of it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for commencing the recall;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the quantity of the cannabis that
    was produced or imported into Canada by the holder;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the cannabis that was sold or
    distributed by the holder in foreign countries;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the quantity of the cannabis that is
    affected by the problem or potential problem underlying the recall
    and that remains in the possession of the holder;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the number of persons to which the holder sold or
    distributed the cannabis in foreign countries;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the period during which the holder sold or
    distributed the cannabis in foreign countries;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the time and manner in which the recall is to be
    carried out, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the expected date for the commencement of the
      recall,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} how and when the Minister will be informed of
      the progress of the recall, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date by which the recall is expected to be
      completed;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a description of any other measure that the holder
    is taking, or intends to take, in respect of the recall; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} contact information for a representative who will
    be responsible for the recall.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk evaluation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder must, within 72 hours after providing the
  Minister with the document referred to in subsection (1) or (2),
  provide the Minister with a document that contains an evaluation of
  the risk associated with the problem or potential problem that
  underlies the recall.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder must, within 30 days after the day on
  which the recall is completed, provide the Minister with a written
  report that sets out the results of the recall and the measures taken
  to prevent a recurrence of the problem.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (4), the Minister may extend the
  period for providing the report --- to a maximum of 90 days after the
  day on which the recall is completed --- if, for reasons beyond the
  holder's control, it is not feasible to provide it within the 30-day
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention periods

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the documents that they provide to the
    Minister under subsections (1) and (3) for at least two years after
    the day on which their last sale or distribution of any cannabis
    product that is the subject of the recall takes place;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the documents that they provide to the
    Minister under subsections (2) and (3) for at least two years after
    the day on which their last export of any of the cannabis that is
    the subject of the recall takes place; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the report that they provide to the
    Minister under subsection (4) for at least two years after the day
    on which the recall is completed.

Definitions of [[adverse reaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[serious
adverse reaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this section and sections 248.1 and 248.2.

[[adverse reaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adverse reaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a noxious and
    unintended response to cannabis or a cannabis accessory that
    contains cannabis. ([réaction indésirable]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[serious adverse reaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[serious adverse reaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an adverse
    reaction that requires inpatient hospitalization or a prolongation
    of existing hospitalization, causes congenital malformation, results
    in persistent or significant disability or incapacity, is
    life-threatening or results in death. ([réaction indésirable
    grave]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 24](#1381808-1383027){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381808-1383027.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-248-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 248"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis products

- **[[248.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence that sells or
  distributes a cannabis product must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 15 days after becoming aware of a serious
    adverse reaction to the cannabis product, provide the Minister with
    a detailed report containing all information in their possession
    that is associated with the use of the product by the individual who
    experienced the serious adverse reaction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prepare an annual summary report that contains a
    concise and critical analysis of all adverse reactions to the
    cannabis product that the holder became aware of during the previous
    12 months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain the reports for at least 25
  years after the day on which they are prepared.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply in respect of an adverse
  reaction to cannabis, or a cannabis accessory that contains cannabis,
  that is distributed to human participants in the conduct of
  non-therapeutic research on cannabis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 24](#1381808-1383038){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381808-1383038.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-therapeutic research on cannabis

- **[[248.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} During the conduct of
  non-therapeutic research on cannabis, the holder of a licence for
  research that distributes cannabis, or a cannabis accessory that
  contains cannabis, to human participants must,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within seven days after becoming aware of a serious
    adverse reaction to the cannabis or cannabis accessory that is
    life-threatening or that results in death, notify the Minister of
    the serious adverse reaction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within eight days after notifying the Minister
    under paragraph (a), provide the Minister with a detailed report
    containing all information in their possession that is associated
    with the use of the cannabis or cannabis accessory by the individual
    who experienced the serious adverse reaction, including the intended
    use of the cannabis or cannabis accessory, an assessment of the
    cause of the serious adverse reaction and a statement of what
    further action, if any, needs to be taken by the holder of the
    licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Serious adverse reaction not life
  threatening

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence must, within 15 days after
  becoming aware of a serious adverse reaction to the cannabis or
  cannabis accessory that is not life-threatening and does not result in
  death, provide the Minister with a detailed report containing all
  information in their possession that is associated with the use of the
  cannabis or cannabis accessory by the individual who experienced the
  serious adverse reaction, including the intended use of the cannabis
  or cannabis accessory, an assessment of the cause of the serious
  adverse reaction and a statement of what further action, if any, needs
  to be taken by the holder of the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain a record of each adverse
  reaction and serious adverse reaction that occurs during the holder's
  conduct of non-therapeutic research on cannabis and that the holder
  becomes aware of.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain the reports and record for at
  least 15 years after the day on which they are prepared.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-251, s. 24](#1381808-1383051){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381808-1383051.html"}
:::

### [Disclosure of Information to Third Parties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849762 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notices to local authorities

- **[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, at the request of the government of a province or any
  police force or other law enforcement agency in a province, disclose
  to the government, police force or agency any information set out in a
  notice that is referred to in section 7 or 35 and that relates to a
  site located in that province if the disclosure is for a purpose
  related to verifying compliance or preventing non-compliance with the
  provisions of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} these Regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a provincial Act; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} regulations made under a provincial Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Police investigation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose any information that is set
  out in a notice referred to in section 7 or 35 to a Canadian police
  force, or a member of a Canadian police force, that requests the
  information in the course of an investigation under the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Information that is disclosed under subsection (2)
  must be used only for the purposes of the investigation or the
  administration or enforcement of the Act or these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to province

- **[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of paragraph 83(f) of the Act, information contained in
  the national cannabis tracking system may be disclosed to the
  government of a province, at its request, if the disclosure is for a
  purpose related to the implementation of public health programs or
  activities that are related to cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public health programs and activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, at the request of the government of
  a province, disclose to the government any information obtained under
  section 297 if the disclosure is for a purpose related to the
  implementation of public health programs or activities that are
  related to cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}International Narcotics Control Board

- **[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to the information that may be disclosed under sections 83,
  128 and 129 of the Act, the Minister may disclose to the International
  Narcotics Control Board other information that is obtained under the
  Act if the disclosure is necessary to enable Canada to fulfill its
  international obligations in relation to cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former statute

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may also disclose to the International
  Narcotics Control Board any information relating to cannabis that was
  obtained under the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
  Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) before the day on which these Regulations come
  into force if the disclosure is necessary to enable Canada to fulfill
  its international obligations in relation to cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Competent authorities

**[[252]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, for
the purposes of the administration or enforcement of the Act or these
Regulations or if it is necessary to enable Canada to fulfill its
international obligations in relation to cannabis, disclose to a
competent authority

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information obtained from a person that has applied
  for or that holds an import or export permit;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} information relating to an activity authorized by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an import or export permit, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a licence held by a person that has applied for or
    that holds an import or export permit;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any document that the holder or former holder of an
  import or export permit is required to retain, including any document
  that relates to a licence that they hold or held; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of any import or export permit.

## [PART 13]{.HLabel1}[Test Kits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-849792 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- Division 2 of Part 1 of Act

**[[253]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-253 .sectionLabel}** The cannabis that is
contained in a test kit that meets the requirements of paragraphs 255(a)
and (b) is exempt from Subdivisions A to D of Division 2 of Part 1 of
the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

**[[254]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-254 .sectionLabel}** Part 5 and section 137
do not apply to the cannabis that is contained in a test kit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, importation and exportation

**[[255]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-255 .sectionLabel}** A person is authorized
to sell, import or export a test kit if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registration number has been issued for the test
  kit and the number has not been cancelled;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following information is included on the label
  that is applied to the test kit:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the registration number, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a test kit that is not subject to
    the labelling requirements of the [Medical Devices
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-282),

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the manufacturer or
      assembler or, if applicable, the name and address of the person
      for which the test kit was manufactured or assembled further to a
      custom order, and

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} its brand name; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the test kit is sold, imported or exported for a
  medical, laboratory, industrial, educational, law administration or
  enforcement or research purpose.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individual --- possession and
distribution

**[[256]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-256 .sectionLabel}** An individual is
authorized to conduct the following activities, in relation to one or
more test kits that contain a total amount of cannabis that, as
determined in accordance with subsection 2(4) of the Act, is equivalent
to more than the amount the individual may possess under the Act, if the
registration number is included on the label of each test kit

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} possess it in a public place; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} distribute it for a medical, laboratory, industrial,
  educational, law administration or enforcement or research purpose.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organization --- possession and
distribution

**[[257]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-257 .sectionLabel}** An organization is
authorized to conduct the following activities if the label of a test
kit includes its registration number:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} possess the test kit; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} distribute the test kit for a medical, laboratory,
  industrial, educational, law administration or enforcement, or
  research purpose.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for registration number

- **[[258]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  manufacturer or assembler of a test kit or, if the test kit is
  manufactured or assembled further to a custom order, the person for
  which the test kit was manufactured or assembled, may apply for a
  registration number for the test kit by submitting an application to
  the Minister containing the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the brand name of the test kit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the design and construction of the
    test kit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the proposed use of the test kit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the cannabis contained in
    the test kit, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 3 to the Act, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a detailed description of every
    other substance contained in the test kit, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of each substance,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the substance is a salt, the name of the
      salt, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of each substance; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the directions for use of the test kit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature and declaration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application for the registration of a test kit
  must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be signed and dated by an individual authorized by
    the applicant for that purpose; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include a declaration, signed and dated by that
    individual, indicating that all the information provided in support
    of the application is correct and complete to the best of their
    knowledge.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

**[[259]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-259 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on
receiving an application referred to in section 258, require the
submission of any additional information that pertains to the
information contained in the application and that is necessary for the
Minister to consider the application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of a registration number

- **[[260]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-260
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  Minister must, after examining the information and documents required
  under sections 258 and 259, issue a registration number for the test
  kit to the applicant if the applicant demonstrates that the test kit
  is designed to be used solely for a medical, laboratory, industrial,
  educational, law administration or enforcement or research purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must refuse to issue a registration
  number if he or she has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the test kit is likely to create a risk to public
    health or safety, including the risk of cannabis being diverted to
    an illicit market or activity, because

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis in the test kit is too
      high, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the adulterating or denaturing agent in the test
      kit is not likely to prevent or deter consumption or
      administration of the cannabis; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the test kit is likely to be used for a purpose
    other than any of those set out in subsection (1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

- **[[261]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must cancel the registration number of a test kit if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister receives a notice from the holder of
    the registration number stating that it has ceased all authorized
    activities referred to in section 255, 256 or 257 with respect to
    the test kit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the test kit is likely to create a risk to public
      health or safety, including the risk of cannabis being diverted to
      an illicit market or activity, because

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis in the test kit is too
        high, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the adulterating or denaturing agent in the
        test kit is not likely to prevent or deter consumption or
        administration of the cannabis, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the test kit is likely to be used for a purpose
      other than any of those set out in subsection 260(1); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the test kit is a medical device and is no longer
    authorized for sale in Canada under the [Medical Devices
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-282).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a registration number issued for a test kit is
  cancelled, the cancelled registration number

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must not be displayed on the label of any test kit
    manufactured or assembled further to a custom order after the
    cancellation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cancellation under paragraph
    (1)(a), must remain on the label of any existing test kit until all
    of the test kits with that registration number are disposed of.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal or cancellation

**[[262]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister
proposes to refuse to issue or to cancel a registration number, the
Minister must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} send a notice to the applicant or to the holder of
  the registration number that sets out the reasons for the refusal or
  cancellation; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} give the applicant or holder an opportunity to be
  heard in respect of the refusal or cancellation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for new number

- **[[263]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  registration number issued for a test kit has been cancelled under
  section 261, the manufacturer or assembler of the test kit or, if the
  test kit is manufactured or assembled further to a custom order, the
  person for which the test kit was manufactured or assembled,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may apply to the Minister for the issuance of a new
    registration number in accordance with section 258; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, in support of the application, provide the
    Minister with proof that the circumstance that gave rise to the
    cancellation has been corrected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New registration number

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister, after examining the application and
  supporting proof referred to in subsection (1), must issue a new
  registration number for the test kit unless there exists a
  circumstance set out in paragraph 261(1)(b) or (c).

## [PART 14]{.HLabel1}[Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-849868 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849869 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[264]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-264 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[adult]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adult]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who is 18 years
      of age or older. ([adulte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[designated person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who
      is designated by a registered person, in accordance with Division
      2 of this Part, to produce cannabis for the medical purposes of
      the registered person. ([personne désignée]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[former Marihuana for Medical Purposes Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Marihuana for Medical Purposes
      Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the regulations made by
      Order in Council P.C. 2013-645 of June 6, 2013 and registered as
      SOR/2013-119. ([ancien Règlement sur la marihuana à des fins
      médicales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[health care practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[health care practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except as
      otherwise provided, a medical practitioner or a nurse
      practitioner. ([praticien de la santé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[licence for sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence for sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence for sale
      for medical purposes. ([licence de vente]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[medical document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[medical document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a document provided
      by a health care practitioner to support the use of cannabis for
      medical purposes. ([document médical]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[medical practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[medical practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual
      who

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is entitled under the laws of a province to
        practise medicine in that province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not restricted, under the laws of the
        province in which they practise, from authorizing the use of
        cannabis; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not named in a notice issued under section
        335 that has not been retracted. ([médecin]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[named responsible adult]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[named responsible adult]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect
      of a client, an adult who is named in the client's registration
      document under subparagraph 282(2)(a)(iv) or subsection
      285(5). ([responsable nommé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[nurse practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[nurse practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is entitled under the laws of a province to
        practise as a nurse practitioner or an equivalent designation
        and is practising as a nurse practitioner or an equivalent
        designation in that province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not restricted, under the laws of the
        province in which they practise, from authorizing the use of
        cannabis; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not named in a notice issued under section
        335 that has not been retracted. ([infirmier
        praticien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial professional licensing authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial professional licensing
      authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} except in sections 343 to 345, an authority
        that is responsible for

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the practise of medicine in a
          province, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} authorizing individuals to practise as nurse
          practitioners in a province; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in sections 343 to 345, an authority that is
        responsible for authorizing individuals to practise pharmacy in
        a province. ([autorité provinciale attributive de licences en
        matière d'activités professionelles]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[registered person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who
      is registered with the Minister under subsection
      313(1). ([personne inscrite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registration certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registration certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      certificate issued by the Minister under subsection
      313(1). ([certificat d'inscription]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registration document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registration document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a document
      provided under paragraph 282(2)(a) to a client by a holder of a
      licence for sale. ([document d'inscription]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[written order]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[written order]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a written
      authorization given by a health care practitioner that a stated
      amount of cannabis be dispensed for the individual named in the
      authorization. ([commande écrite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalent designation --- nurse
  practitioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (a) of the definition of
  [[nurse practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, a designation is
  equivalent when it designates an individual who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a registered nurse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} possesses additional educational preparation and
    experience related to health care;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} can autonomously make diagnoses, order and
    interpret diagnostic tests, prescribe drugs and perform other
    specific procedures under the laws of a province; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is practising their profession in accordance with
    one of the following provincial laws, as amended from time to time,
    or a similar law in another province:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Extended Practice Regulation, Man. Reg.
      43/2005, made under The Registered Nurses Act, C.C.S.M., c. R40,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Ontario Regulation 275/94, made under the
      Nursing Act, 1991, S.O. 1991, c. 32, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Regulation respecting the classes of
      specialization of the Ordre des infirmières et infirmiers du
      Québec for the activities referred to in section 36.1 of the
      Nurses Act to be engaged in, CQLR, c. I-8, r. 8, made under the
      Nurses Act of Quebec, CQLR, c. I-8.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalent quantity --- cannabis

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the following provisions, the equivalency of a
  quantity of cannabis is to be determined in accordance with subsection
  2(4) of the Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 266(2), (3), (4), (6) and (7);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsections 267(2) to (5);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 290(1)(e);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 292(4);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 293(1);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 297(1)(e)(iii);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 322(1)(c);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 348(3)(a); and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 350(2).

### [Non-application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849917 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Drugs containing cannabis

**[[265]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply in respect of a drug containing cannabis.

### [Possession]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849920 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession in public place --- adults

- **[[266]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following individuals are authorized to possess, in a public place,
  cannabis --- other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds ---
  that has been obtained under the Act, subject to the applicable
  maximum amounts and purposes set out in this section:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an adult who is registered with a holder of a
    licence for sale on the basis of a medical document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an adult who is a registered person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an adult who, directly or indirectly, obtained the
    cannabis as an inpatient or outpatient of a hospital;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an adult who is named in a registration document or
    a registration certificate as being responsible for an individual
    referred to in paragraph (a) or (b) or 267(1)(a) or (b) and who
    possesses the cannabis for the medical purposes of that individual;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an adult who is responsible for an individual
    referred to in paragraph (c) or 267(1)(c) and who possesses the
    cannabis for the medical purposes of that individual; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an adult who possesses the cannabis in the presence
    of an individual referred to in paragraph (a), (b) or (c) or
    267(1)(a), (b) or (c) for the purpose of providing assistance in
    administering it to the individual.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Client registered on basis of medical
  document

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that an adult referred
  to in paragraph (1)(a) is authorized to possess in a public place, for
  their own medical purposes, is an amount that is equivalent to the
  lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
    indicated in their registration document or, if they have more than
    one registration document, 30 times the total of the daily
    quantities indicated in the registration documents, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered person

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that an adult referred
  to in paragraph (1)(b) is authorized to possess in a public place, for
  their own medical purposes, is an amount that is equivalent to the
  lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
    indicated in their registration certificate, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hospital patient

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that an adult referred
  to in paragraph (1)(c) is authorized to possess in a public place, for
  their own medical purposes, is an amount that is equivalent to the
  lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
    indicated on the hospital label that is applied to the container in
    which the cannabis was provided to or for them, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsible adult

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that an adult referred
  to in paragraph (1)(d) or (e) is authorized to possess in a public
  place, for the medical purposes of the individual for whom they are
  responsible, is the maximum amount that the individual is authorized
  to possess under subsection (2), (3) or (4) or 267(2), (3) or (4), as
  the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adult providing assistance

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that an adult referred
  to in paragraph (1)(f) is authorized to possess in a public place, for
  the purpose of providing assistance in administering cannabis to the
  individual referred to in that paragraph, is an amount equivalent to
  the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis indicated in
    the individual's registration document or registration certificate
    or on the hospital label that is applied to the container in which
    the cannabis was provided to or for the individual, as the case may
    be, or in the case where the individual has more than one
    registration document, or one or more registration documents and a
    registration certificate, the total of the daily quantities, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} This section does not authorize an adult who is
  referred to in more than one of paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) to possess,
  for their own medical purposes, more than the equivalent of 150 g of
  dried cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession --- young persons

- **[[267]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following young persons are authorized to possess cannabis --- other
  than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds --- that has been
  obtained under the Act, subject to the applicable maximum amounts and
  purposes set out in this section:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a young person who is registered with a holder of a
    licence for sale on the basis of a medical document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a young person who is a registered person; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a young person who, directly or indirectly,
    obtained the cannabis as an inpatient or outpatient of a hospital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Client registered on basis of medical
  document

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that a young person
  referred to in paragraph (1)(a) is authorized to possess, for their
  own medical purposes, is an amount that is equivalent to the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
    indicated in their registration document or, if they have more than
    one registration document, 30 times the total of the daily
    quantities indicated in the registration documents, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered person

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that a young person
  referred to in paragraph (1)(b) is authorized to possess, for their
  own medical purposes, is an amount that is equivalent to the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
    indicated in their registration certificate, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hospital patient

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount of cannabis that a young person
  referred to in paragraph (1)(c) is authorized to possess, for their
  own medical purposes, is an amount that is equivalent to the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
    indicated on the hospital label that is applied to the container in
    which the cannabis was provided to or for them, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} This section does not authorize a young person who is
  referred to in more than one of paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) to possess,
  for their own medical purposes, more than the equivalent of 150 g of
  dried cannabis.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cumulative quantities

**[[268]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-268 .sectionLabel}** Any quantity of
cannabis that an individual is authorized to possess under section 266
or 267 is in addition to any other quantity of cannabis that the
individual may possess under the Act.

### [Distribution]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849972 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution of cannabis

- **[[269]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-269 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to any other quantity of cannabis that they may distribute
  under the Act, an adult referred to in paragraph 266(1)(d) or (e) is
  authorized to distribute --- but not send or otherwise indirectly make
  available --- to the individual for whom they are responsible, or
  transport for that individual, a quantity of cannabis, other than
  cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, that does not exceed the
  maximum amount that the adult is authorized to possess under
  subsection 266(5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance by adult

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other quantity of cannabis that
  they may distribute under the Act, an adult referred to in paragraph
  266(1)(f) is authorized to administer, give, transfer, provide or
  otherwise directly make available to the individual to whom the
  assistance is being provided a quantity of cannabis, other than
  cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, that does not exceed the
  maximum amount that the adult is authorized to possess under
  subsection 266(6).

### [Non-application of Section 71 of Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849978 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 71 of Act

**[[270]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-270 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 71 of
the Act, an authorization to possess, sell, distribute or produce
cannabis that is conferred by this Part on an adult referred to in
paragraphs 266(1)(a) to (f), a young person referred to in paragraphs
267(1)(a) to (c) or a designated person does not authorize an employee
or agent or mandatary of the adult, young person or designated person
--- or a person who is acting under a contract with the adult, young
person or designated person --- to do anything that is prohibited by a
provision of Division 1 of Part 1 of the Act.

### [Health Care Practitioners]{.HTitleText2} {#h-849981 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

**[[271]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-271 .sectionLabel}** A health care
practitioner must not provide a medical document or issue a written
order except as authorized under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization --- health care
practitioner

- **[[272]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-272 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  health care practitioner is authorized, in respect of an individual
  who is under their professional treatment and if cannabis is required
  for the condition for which the individual is receiving treatment,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to provide a medical document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} while practising in a hospital, to issue a written
    order;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to administer to the individual a cannabis product,
    other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to transfer to the individual, or to an adult who
    is responsible for them, a cannabis product, other than cannabis
    plants or cannabis plant seeds,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that has been received from a holder of a licence
      for sale or a licence for processing, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which the quantity of cannabis
      does not exceed the quantity that the individual or adult is
      authorized to possess under section 266 or 267, as the case may
      be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession in public place

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A health care practitioner is authorized to possess a
  cannabis product, other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds,
  in a public place if they have obtained it under the Act and require
  it for the practice of their profession in the province where they
  possess it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Medical document

- **[[273]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-273 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  medical document that is provided under paragraph 272(1)(a) must
  indicate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner's given name, surname,
    profession, business address and telephone number and, if
    applicable, their facsimile number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the province in which the health care practitioner
    is authorized to practise their profession and the number assigned
    by the province to that authorization;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    individual who is under the professional treatment of the health
    care practitioner;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the individual
    consulted with the health care practitioner;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in
    grams, that the health care practitioner authorizes for the
    individual; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a period of use, specified as a number of days,
    weeks or months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The period of use specified in a medical document
  must not exceed one year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature and statement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A medical document must be signed and dated by the
  health care practitioner who is providing it and must include a
  statement confirming that the information in the document is correct
  and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of medical document

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A medical document is valid for the period of use
  specified in it, which period begins on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the individual referred to in
    paragraph (1)(c) is registered with a holder of a licence for sale
    on the basis of the document or, if there have been previous
    registrations on the basis of the document, the earliest
    registration date; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the individual referred to in paragraph (1)(c)
    uses the document to register with the Minister under Division 2 of
    this Part and has not previously been registered with a holder of a
    licence for sale on the basis of the document, the date of the
    registration with the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional period of validity

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (4), a medical document is valid
  for an additional period of six months beginning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the day on which this subsection comes into
    force, if the document ceases to be valid under subsection (4) on or
    after March 13, 2020 but before the day on which this subsection
    comes into force; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the day after the day on which the document
    ceases to be valid under subsection (4) if the day on which the
    document ceases to be valid is on or after the day on which this
    subsection comes into force but before October 1, 2020.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-149, s. 1](#1252264-1252676){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1252264-1252676.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-273-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 273"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written order

**[[274]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-274 .sectionLabel}** A written order that is
issued under paragraph 272(1)(b) must be signed and dated by the health
care practitioner and must indicate

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner's given name, surname
  and profession;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the given name and surname of the individual who is
  under the professional treatment of the health care practitioner; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in
  grams, that the health care practitioner authorizes for the
  individual.

### [General Prohibitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-850017 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alteration of documents

**[[275]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-275 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited to
alter or deface a medical document or other document that is issued or
provided under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obtaining from more than one source

- **[[276]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-276 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is prohibited to seek or obtain a cannabis product from more than one
  source at a time on the basis of the same medical document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a medical document forms
  the basis for a registration with the Minister under Division 2 of
  this Part, the registration certificate may be used to obtain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products, other than cannabis plants and
    cannabis plant seeds, from, or through, a single holder of a licence
    for sale; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds from one or
    more holders of a licence for sale.

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Holders of Licence for Sale]{.HTitleText2} {#h-850027 .Subheading}

#### [Notice to Licensing Authorities]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850028 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to licensing authorities

- **[[277]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-277 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, within 30 days after the issuance
  of their licence, provide a written notice to each provincial
  professional licensing authority and a copy to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder, the mailing address of the
    site specified in the licence and, if applicable, the holder's email
    address; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the licence.

#### [Registration of Clients]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850036 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility --- client

**[[278]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-278 .sectionLabel}** An individual is
eligible to be a client of a holder of a licence for sale only if the
individual ordinarily resides in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration application

- **[[279]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-279
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before registering an individual as
  a client, a holder of a licence for sale must receive a registration
  application, together with the original of the individual's medical
  document or a copy of their registration certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application on basis of medical
  document

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application that is submitted on the basis of a
  medical document must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's given name, surname and date of
    birth;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place in Canada where the
      applicant ordinarily resides, as well as, if applicable, their
      telephone number, facsimile number and email address, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant ordinarily resides in Canada
      but does not ordinarily reside at a specific place, the address as
      well as, if applicable, the telephone number, facsimile number and
      email address of a shelter, hostel or similar institution located
      in Canada that provides them with food, lodging or other social
      services;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the mailing address of the place referred to in
    paragraph (b) if different from the address provided under that
    paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the place referred to in subparagraph (b)(i) is
    an establishment that is not a private residence, the type and name
    of the establishment;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the shipping address is to
    be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the address referred to in subparagraph (b)(i),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the mailing address of the place referred to in
      subparagraph (b)(i), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the health care practitioner who provided
      the medical document has consented to receive cannabis products on
      behalf of the applicant, the address of the health care
      practitioner;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the given name, surname and date of
    birth of one or more adults who are responsible for the applicant;
    and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a statement signed and dated by the applicant, or
    an adult who is named under paragraph (f), confirming that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant ordinarily resides in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the information in the application is correct
      and complete,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the medical document that forms the basis for
      the application has not, to the knowledge of the individual
      signing the statement, been altered,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the medical document is not being used to seek
      or obtain cannabis products from another source,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the applicant is signing the
      statement, they intend to use any cannabis product that is
      supplied to them on the basis of the application only for their
      own medical purposes, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} in the case where an adult who is named under
      paragraph (f) is signing the statement, they are responsible for
      the applicant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application on basis of registration
  certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application that is submitted on the basis of a
  registration certificate must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraphs (2)(a) to
    (d), as applicable;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the application is being
    made to obtain

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products, other than cannabis plants or
      cannabis plant seeds,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, or
      both, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products referred to in both
      subparagraphs (i) and (ii);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the application is being made to obtain cannabis
    products, other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, an
    indication of which of the addresses referred to in paragraph (2)(e)
    is to be the shipping address;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the application is being made to obtain cannabis
