    plants or cannabis plant seeds, an indication of whether the
    shipping address is to be

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place where the applicant
      ordinarily resides,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place where the designated
      person, if any, ordinarily resides, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the address of the site for the production of
      cannabis that is specified in the registration certificate; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a statement signed and dated by the applicant, or
    an adult who is named in the registration certificate under
    paragraph 313(2)(c), confirming that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant ordinarily resides in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the information in the application is correct
      and complete,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the copy of the registration certificate is an
      accurate reproduction of the original,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if the application is being made to obtain
      cannabis products other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant
      seeds, the registration certificate is not being used to seek or
      obtain the cannabis products from another source,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the applicant is signing the
      statement, they intend to use any cannabis product that is
      supplied to them on the basis of the application only for their
      own medical purposes, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} in the case where an adult who is named in the
      registration certificate is signing the statement, they are
      responsible for the applicant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant without ordinary residence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an institution's address is included in the
  application in accordance with subparagraph (2)(b)(ii), the
  application must include a statement that is signed and dated by a
  manager of the institution confirming that it provides food, lodging
  or other social services to the applicant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health care practitioner's consent

- **[[280]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-280 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an application referred to in section 279 includes, as a shipping
  address, the address of the health care practitioner who provided the
  applicant with the medical document, the application must include a
  statement, signed and dated by the health care practitioner,
  consenting to receive cannabis products, other than cannabis plants
  and cannabis plant seeds, on the applicant's behalf.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of consent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant becomes a client of a holder of a
  licence for sale under this Division and the health care practitioner
  ceases to consent to receive cannabis products on the behalf of the
  client, the health care practitioner must send a written notice to
  that effect to the client and the holder.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of medical document

- **[[281]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-281 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale that intends to register an applicant
  whose application is based on a medical document must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} verify that the medical document meets the
    requirements set out in subsections 273(1) to (3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} verify that, at the time the medical document was
    provided to the applicant, the individual who provided the document
    was a health care practitioner who was entitled to practise their
    profession in the province in which the applicant consulted with
    them; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} verify with the office of the individual referred
    to in paragraph (b) that the applicant consulted the individual and
    that the information set out in the medical document is correct and
    complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder is not required to do the verification
  referred to in paragraph (1)(c) if the signature of the health care
  practitioner who provided the medical document is known to the holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uncertainty --- previous registration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder must, if it appears that the applicant was
  previously registered on the basis of the medical document but the
  information referred to in subsection 288(1) does not appear on the
  medical document,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to determine whether the
    applicant was previously registered on the basis of the medical
    document; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where they determine that the applicant
    was previously registered on the basis of the medical document,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to determine the date on
      which the applicant was first registered on that basis; and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they determine the date referred to in
      subparagraph (i), ensure that it appears on the medical document
      in accordance with subsection 288(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date illegible or incomplete

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder must, if a date of registration appears on
  the medical document but the date is illegible, incomplete or there is
  reason to doubt that it is accurate,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to determine the date on
    which the applicant was first registered on the basis of the medical
    document; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they determine the date referred to in paragraph
    (a), ensure that it appears on the medical document in accordance
    with subsection 288(1) unless there are reasonable grounds to
    believe that the date that already appears on the medical document
    has been falsified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unknown date

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the holder is, after taking the steps referred to
  in subparagraph (3)(b)(i) or paragraph (4)(a), unable to determine the
  date on which the applicant was first registered on the basis of the
  medical document, they must, if they proceed to register the
  applicant, indicate on the medical document the information referred
  to subsection 288(1) in accordance with the requirements set out in
  that subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of client

- **[[282]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-282 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale may, subject to section 284, register an
  applicant as a client.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration document and other
  information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder that registers an applicant as a client must
  provide them with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registration document that contains the following
    information:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the client's given name, surname and date of
      birth,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the given name and surname of the health care
      practitioner who provided the client's medical document,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of any
      adults named in the application under paragraph 279(2)(f) or in
      the registration certificate under paragraph 313(2)(c), as the
      case may be,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the registration is
      based on a medical document or a registration certificate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed
      in grams, indicated in the medical document or registration
      certificate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a registration that is based on
      a medical document, the shipping address indicated in the
      application under paragraph 279(2)(e),

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a registration that is based on
      a registration certificate, the shipping address or addresses
      indicated in the application under paragraph 279(3)(c) or (d), or
      both, as applicable, and

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information that will permit them to use a unique
    identifier for the purpose of ordering cannabis; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the current version of the document entitled
    Consumer Information --- Cannabis, published by the Government of
    Canada on its website.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry of registration

**[[283]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-283 .sectionLabel}** A client's registration
with a holder of a licence for sale expires

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it is based on a medical document, at the end of
  the period of validity of the document, as determined in accordance
  with subsections 273(4) and (5); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it is based on a registration certificate, when
  the registration with the Minister expires.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-149, s. 2](#1252266-1252679){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1252266-1252679.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-283-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 283"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to register

- **[[284]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-284 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must refuse to register an applicant as a
  client if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the application does not meet the requirements set
    out in section 279 or, if applicable, subsection 280(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder has reasonable grounds to believe that
    false or misleading information has, or false or falsified documents
    have, been provided in, or in support of, the application;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirements set out in subsection 281(1) have
    not been met;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the medical document that forms the basis for the
    application is no longer valid;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the registration with the Minister that forms the
    basis for the application has expired or been revoked;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname or date of birth of the
    applicant is different from the given name, surname or date of birth
    that appears on the medical document or the copy of the registration
    certificate that has been submitted; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who provided the
    medical document to the applicant notifies the holder in writing
    that the use of cannabis by the applicant is no longer supported for
    clinical reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification requirement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must not refuse to register an applicant
  on the basis that there are reasonable grounds to believe that the
  medical document is false or falsified unless the holder has contacted
  the office of the health care practitioner whose name appears in the
  medical document to ask about the matters referred to in paragraph
  281(1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of intention to refuse

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the holder proposes to refuse an applicant for a
  reason referred to in subsection (1) or a business reason, the holder
  must, without delay, send the applicant a written notice that sets out
  the reason for the proposed refusal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opportunity to be heard

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the holder sends a notice under subsection (3),
  they must not refuse to register the applicant unless the applicant
  has had 10 days after the day on which the applicant receives the
  notice to provide reasons why the refusal is unfounded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal to register

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the holder is provided with reasons under
  subsection (4), they must, if they refuse to register the applicant,
  send the applicant a written notice of the refusal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of medical document

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the holder refuses to register an applicant whose
  application is based on a medical document, the holder must, without
  delay, return the medical document unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is clear that the document is false or
    falsified; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registration is refused for a reason referred
    to in paragraph (1)(d) or (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Minister

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the holder refuses to register an applicant whose
  application is based on a registration certificate, the holder must,
  as soon as feasible, provide the Minister with a notice that includes
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    registered person named in the certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registration number indicated in the
    certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the refusal; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for the refusal.

#### [Amendment to Registration]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850157 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment to registration

- **[[285]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-285 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  there is a change in respect of any of the information provided under
  section 279, the client --- or, if applicable, an adult who is
  responsible for them --- must, without delay, submit an application to
  amend the registration to the holder of the licence for sale unless
  the client has ceased to ordinarily reside in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must include the following
  information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the new information;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there has been a change to the information
    referred to in paragraph 279(2)(a), proof of the change;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the statement of the health care
    practitioner consenting to receive cannabis products on behalf of
    the client, as required by subsection 280(1);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the application is being submitted by an adult
    who is responsible for the client but who is not a named responsible
    adult, the given name, surname and date of birth of the adult; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a statement, signed and dated by the individual who
    is submitting the application, confirming that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the client ordinarily resides in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the information included in the application is
      correct and complete,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the individual who is signing
      the statement is not the client, they are responsible for the
      client, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the individual who is signing
      the statement is neither the client nor a named responsible adult,
      the client and any named responsible adults have been notified of
      the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The new information that is included in the
  application under paragraph (2)(a) must meet the applicable
  requirements set out in section 279.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to amend

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence for sale must, if they
  receive an application that complies with subsections (2) and (3),
  amend the client's registration and provide them with an updated
  registration document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New responsible adult

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a registration is amended on the basis of an
  application that is signed by an adult who is neither the client nor a
  named responsible adult, the name of the adult who signed the
  application must be included in the updated registration document.

#### [Revocation of Registration]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850178 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of registration

- **[[286]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-286 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, without delay, revoke a client's
  registration if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the client --- or, if applicable, a named
    responsible adult --- requests it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the medical document that forms the basis for the
    registration is transferred to another holder of a licence for sale
    under section 287;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the holder has reasonable grounds to believe the
    client has ceased to ordinarily reside in Canada or ceased to have a
    shipping address in Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the holder has reasonable grounds to believe that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the registration was made on the basis of false
      or misleading information provided in, or false or falsified
      documents provided in support of, the registration application, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was, or false or
      falsified documents were, provided in, or in support of, an
      amendment application under section 285;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who provided the
    medical document notifies the holder in writing that the use of
    cannabis by the client is no longer supported for clinical reasons;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the registration is based on a registration
    certificate, the holder becomes aware that the registration with the
    Minister has been revoked; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the client dies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation for business reason

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale may revoke a client's
  registration for a business reason.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of all registrations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale whose licence is
  revoked must, without delay, revoke the registrations of all of their
  clients and send each client a written notice that sets out the reason
  for the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a registration under subsection (1)
  or (2), the holder must send the client a written notice that sets out
  the reason for the proposed revocation unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the client has requested that the registration be
    revoked or that the medical document that forms the basis for the
    registration be transferred;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the named responsible adult, if any, who signed the
    most recent statement that was included in an application that was
    submitted under section 279 or 285 to the holder in respect of the
    client has requested that the registration be revoked or that the
    medical document that forms the basis for the registration be
    transferred;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the client --- or, if applicable, the named
    responsible adult referred to in paragraph (b) --- has consented to
    the transfer of the medical document that forms the basis for the
    registration;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the client has died; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the holder has received a notice of revocation
    referred to in subsection 319(1) in respect of the client.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opportunity to be heard

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the holder sends a notice under subsection (4),
  the holder must not revoke the registration unless the client has had
  10 days after the day on which they receive the notice to provide
  reasons why the revocation is unfounded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Medical document

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the holder revokes a registration that is based on
  a medical document, they must, without delay, return the document to
  the client unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is clear that the document is false or
    falsified; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registration is revoked for a reason referred
    to in paragraph (1)(b), (e) or (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Minister

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the holder revokes a registration that is based on
  a registration certificate, they must, as soon as feasible, provide
  the Minister with a notice that includes the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    registered person named in the certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registration number indicated in the
    certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the revocation; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the revocation.

#### [Transfer of Medical Documents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850214 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of medical document

- **[[287]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-287 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, without delay, transfer the medical
  document that forms the basis for a client's registration to another
  holder of a licence for sale if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the client --- or, if applicable, a named
    responsible adult --- requests, or consents to, the transfer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder to which the document is to be
    transferred consents to the transfer; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} no revocation of the registration is pending for a
    reason referred to in any of paragraphs 286(1)(c) to (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to send information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder that transfers the medical document must
  send the information contained in the client's registration document
  to the holder to which the document is transferred.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to register

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder to which the medical document is
  transferred must, after receiving the information referred to in
  subsection (2), register the client and comply with the requirements
  set out in subsection 282(2).

#### [Indication of Date of Registration]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850225 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to indicate date of
registration

- **[[288]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-288 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, before returning a medical document
  under subsection 286(6) or transferring such a document under section
  287, indicate the following information on the document in indelible
  ink if no date of registration appears on the document:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the client was registered, in the
    order of day, month and year, preceded by the words "date of
    registration" or "date d'inscription"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Previously registered clients

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the obligation in
  subsection (1) applies in respect of the medical document of an
  individual who was not registered under this Part but who is a client
  of the holder by virtue of subsection 158(7) of the Act.

#### [Sale of Cannabis to Clients]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850233 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization to sell

- **[[289]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-289
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the other provisions of
  these Regulations, a holder of a licence for sale is authorized to
  sell cannabis products to a client --- or, if applicable, to a named
  responsible adult --- if the holder has received from the client or
  the named responsible adult

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a written purchase order that complies with
    subsection (2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a verbal purchase order that has been recorded in
    accordance with subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written purchase order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A written purchase order must contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it is placed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    client;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the given name and surname of the individual
    placing the order;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the shipping address specified in the client's
    registration document for the cannabis products that are being
    ordered;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the client's unique identifier; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the names assigned by the holder of the licence for
    sale to the cannabis products being ordered, the quantities desired
    and the brand names.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verbal purchase order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The record of a verbal purchase order must be in
  writing and contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraphs (2)(a) to
    (f);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the individual with whom the order was
    placed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New medical document or registration
  certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the authorization to sell cannabis products that is
    conferred by subsection (1) applies only in respect of the medical
    document or registration certificate that forms the basis for the
    client's registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the client wishes to obtain cannabis products
    from the holder on the basis of a different medical document or
    registration certificate, they must submit to the holder a new
    registration application that meets the requirements set out in
    section 279 and be registered on the basis of that document or
    certificate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sections 280 to 284 apply in respect of a
    registration application referred to in paragraph (b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal --- purchase order

- **[[290]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-290 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must refuse to fill a purchase order ---
  and must not ask a holder of a licence for processing or cultivation
  to fill it --- if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the order does not meet the requirements of section
    289;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any of the information referred to in paragraph
    289(2)(b), (c) or (d) does not correspond to the information set out
    in the client's registration document;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the client's unique identifier referred to in
    paragraph 289(2)(e) is not correct;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the client's registration has expired or been
    revoked;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the order specifies cannabis products, other than
    cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, in respect of which the
    quantities of cannabis exceed the equivalent of 150 g of dried
    cannabis;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a client who is registered on the
    basis of a medical document, the order specifies a quantity of
    cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the order specifies a total quantity of cannabis
    plants or cannabis plant seeds that, taking into account the
    seed-to-plant ratio specified in subsection (2), exceeds the maximum
    number of plants, determined in accordance with section 325, that
    are authorized to be under production by virtue of the client's
    registration with the Minister; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the order has been previously filled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seed-to-plant ratio

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (1)(g), three cannabis
  plant seeds are equivalent to one cannabis plant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the holder of the licence refuses to fill a
  purchase order, they must, except in the case of a refusal for a
  reason referred to in paragraph (1)(d), send the client a written
  notice that sets out the reason for the refusal.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending or delivery --- client

- **[[291]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-291 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale that sells a cannabis product under
  section 289 is authorized to send or deliver it to the relevant
  shipping address that is specified in the client's registration
  document unless, in the case where the shipping address is the address
  of a health care practitioner,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder has received a written notice from the
    health care practitioner indicating that they have ceased to consent
    to receive cannabis products on the client's behalf; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder has received a notice issued by the
    Minister under section 335 that names the health care practitioner
    and that has not been retracted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale that receives a notice
  referred to in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) must not ask a holder of a
  licence for processing to send or deliver cannabis products to a
  client whose shipping address is the address of the health care
  practitioner who sent the notice or is named in it, as the case may
  be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to holder of processing licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale that has asked a
  holder of a licence for processing to send or deliver cannabis
  products to the address of a health care practitioner must, without
  delay, provide the holder of the processing licence with a written
  notice if, within 48 hours after making the request, the holder of the
  sale licence receives

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice referred to in paragraph (1)(a) from the
    health care practitioner; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice referred to in paragraph (1)(b) that names
    the health care practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- licence for processing

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing that receives a
  notice under subsection (3) must not send or deliver the cannabis
  products that they had been asked to send or deliver.

#### [Return and Replacement of Cannabis]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850285 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of cannabis products

- **[[292]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-292 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual to whom cannabis products are sold under section 289 --- or
  a designated person to whom cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds
  are sent or delivered under section 291 --- may return the cannabis
  products if the holder of the licence for sale accepts the return.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of return

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The individual who is returning the cannabis products
  must, subject to subsection (3), send them or have them delivered to
  the holder of the licence for sale or the holder of a licence for
  processing or cultivation, as indicated by the holder of the licence
  for sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health care practitioner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of cannabis products that were
  transferred to the individual by a health care practitioner who had
  consented under subsection 280(1) to receive them, the individual who
  is returning the cannabis products may, with the consent of the health
  care practitioner, give them, send them or have them delivered to the
  health care practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parcel --- requirements

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The individual who is returning the cannabis products
  must, if they are sending or having delivered cannabis plants --- or
  other cannabis products in respect of which the total quantity of
  cannabis exceeds the equivalent of 30 g of dried cannabis,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prepare the parcel in a manner that ensures the
    security of its contents, such that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it will not open or permit the escape of its
      contents during handling and transportation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is sealed so that it cannot be opened without
      the seal being broken,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of cannabis plants, it
      prevents the escape of odours associated with cannabis plant
      material, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} it prevents its contents from being identified
      without it being opened; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use a method that ensures the tracking and
    safekeeping of the parcel during transportation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return by health care practitioner

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A health care practitioner must ensure that the
  requirements set out in paragraphs (4)(a) and (b) are met if they are
  sending or having delivered cannabis products referred to in
  subsection (4) that have been returned to them under subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-206, s. 68](#1143463-1204824){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143463-1204824.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-292-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 292"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Replacement of returned cannabis

- **[[293]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-293 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale to which cannabis products, other than
  cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, are returned in accordance
  with section 292 may replace them with cannabis products, other than
  cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, in respect of which the total
  quantity of cannabis does not exceed the equivalent of 150 g of dried
  cannabis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plants or seeds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale to which cannabis
  plants or cannabis plant seeds are returned in accordance with section
  292 may replace them with a quantity of cannabis plants or cannabis
  plant seeds, or both, that does not exceed, taking into account the
  seed-to-plant ratio specified in subsection 290(2), the maximum number
  of plants, determined in accordance with section 325, that are
  authorized to be under production under the client's registration with
  the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Processing licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing may replace
  cannabis products that have been returned in accordance with section
  292 with cannabis products in respect of which the total quantity of
  cannabis, according to information provided by the holder of the
  licence for sale, does not exceed the applicable quantity of cannabis
  referred to in subsection (1) or (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cultivation licence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for cultivation may replace
  cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds that have been returned in
  accordance with section 292 with a quantity of cannabis plants or
  cannabis plant seeds, or both, that, according to information provided
  by the holder of the licence for sale, does not exceed the quantity of
  cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds that could be replaced by the
  holder of the licence for sale under subsection (2).

#### [Sale, Display and Promotion to Young Persons]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850312 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of cannabis accessories to young
persons

**[[294]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-294 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for sale is authorized to sell a cannabis accessory --- other than a
cannabis accessory referred to in section 31 of the Act or a device
referred to in subsection 202(2) --- to a young person if the holder
takes reasonable steps to ensure that the young person is authorized to
possess cannabis under section 267.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display --- young persons

- **[[295]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-295 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale is authorized to display a cannabis
  product, or a package or label of a cannabis product, in a manner that
  may result in the cannabis product, package or label being seen by a
  young person if the holder takes reasonable steps to ensure that any
  such young person is authorized to possess cannabis under section 267.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis accessories --- young persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale that sells a cannabis
  accessory is authorized to display it, or its package or label, in a
  manner that may result in the cannabis accessory, package or label
  being seen by a young person if the holder takes reasonable steps to
  ensure that any such young person is authorized to possess cannabis
  under section 267.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Promotion of cannabis products --- young
persons

- **[[296]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-296 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale that promotes a cannabis product by means
  of informational promotion or brand-preference promotion that is
  communicated by means of a telecommunication is exempt from the
  condition set out in paragraph 17(2)(c) of the Act if they have taken
  reasonable steps to ensure that the promotion cannot be accessed by a
  young person other than a young person who is authorized to possess
  cannabis under section 267.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Promotion of cannabis accessories ---
  young persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale that promotes a
  cannabis accessory by means of informational promotion or
  brand-preference promotion that is communicated by means of a
  telecommunication is exempt from the condition set out in paragraph
  17(3)(c) of the Act if they have taken reasonable steps to ensure that
  the promotion cannot be accessed by a young person other than a young
  person who is authorized to possess cannabis under section 267.

#### [Reports to Minister]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850325 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Monthly reports

- **[[297]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-297 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, on or before the 15th day of each
  month, provide the Minister with a report that contains the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of clients who had a valid registration
    on the last day of the previous month;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of clients who, in the previous month,
    had their medical document transferred to another holder of a
    licence for sale or returned to them at their request or at the
    request of a named responsible adult;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the medical documents that formed the
    basis for registrations that were valid on the last day of the
    previous month,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the average daily quantity of dried cannabis,
      expressed in grams,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the median daily quantity of dried cannabis,
      expressed in grams, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the highest daily quantity of dried cannabis,
      expressed in grams;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number of applicants whom the holder refused to
    register during the previous month, including the number of them who
    were refused for each of the following reasons:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the application was incomplete,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the holder had reasonable grounds to believe
      that false or misleading information was, or false or falsified
      documents were, provided in, or in support of, the application,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the medical document or registration
      certificate that formed the basis for the application was not
      valid;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of purchase orders referred to in
    subsection 289(1) that the holder refused to fill during the
    previous month, including the number of them that were refused for
    each of the following reasons:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the purchase order was incomplete,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the client's registration had expired or been
      revoked,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the purchase order specified cannabis products,
      other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, in respect of
      which the quantities of cannabis exceeded the equivalent of 150 g
      of dried cannabis, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis product specified in the purchase
      order was unavailable;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname, profession and business
    address of each health care practitioner who provided a medical
    document referred to in paragraph (c), together with the province in
    which the health care practitioner was authorized to practise their
    profession at the time they signed the document and the number
    assigned by the province to that authorization; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the number of medical documents referred to in
    paragraph (c) that were signed by each health care practitioner
    referred to in paragraph (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[health care
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[health care
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who is, or was,
  a medical practitioner or nurse practitioner.

#### [Disclosure to Third Parties]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850348 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to police

- **[[298]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-298 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, if they are provided with an
  individual's given name, surname, and date of birth by a member of a
  Canadian police force who requests information in the course of an
  investigation under the Act, disclose the following information to the
  police force as soon as feasible and no later than 72 hours after
  receiving the request:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the individual is a client
    of the holder or a named responsible adult in respect of such a
    client;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the individual is an individual
    referred to in paragraph (a),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the client is a
      registered person and, if so, the classes of cannabis that the
      client is permitted to obtain by virtue of their registration with
      the holder, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis that is
      specified in the client's registration document in accordance with
      subparagraph 282(2)(a)(vi).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Information provided under this section must be used
  only for the purposes of the investigation or the administration or
  enforcement of the Act or these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to licensing authority

- **[[299]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-299 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must disclose, in writing and as soon as
  feasible, factual information about a health care practitioner --- in
  relation to cannabis --- that they have obtained under the Act or the
  [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for
    a province in which the health care practitioner is, or was,
    entitled to practise if the licensing authority submits to the
    holder a written request that sets out the health care
    practitioner's name and address, a description of the information
    being requested and a statement that the information is required for
    the purpose of assisting an investigation by the authority; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for
    a province in which the health care practitioner is not entitled to
    practise if the licensing authority submits to the holder

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a written request that sets out the health care
      practitioner's name and address and a description of the
      information being requested, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that shows that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner has applied to the
        licensing authority to practise in that province, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the licensing authority has reasonable grounds
        to believe that the health care practitioner is practising in
        that province without being authorized to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factual information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The factual information that may be requested
  includes information --- including patient information --- contained
  in, or in respect of, any medical document that was signed by the
  health care practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), the factual information that
  may be requested does not include information relating to an
  individual who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is or was registered as a client of the holder on
    the basis of a registration with the Minister under Division 2 of
    this Part; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the holder was a licensed
    producer under the former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230), was registered as a
    client of the licensed producer under section 133 of those
    Regulations on the basis of a registration with the Minister under
    Part 2 of those Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secure transmission

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder must ensure that the information that they
  disclose under this section is securely transmitted in an electronic
  format that is accessible to the provincial professional licensing
  authority.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[health care
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[health care
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who is, or was,
  a medical practitioner or nurse practitioner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quarterly reports

- **[[300]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-300 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  provincial professional licensing authority may submit a written
  request to a holder of a licence for sale to obtain information, on a
  quarterly basis, in respect of each client who is registered with the
  holder on the basis of a medical document that was signed by a health
  care practitioner who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was, at the time that they signed the medical
    document, entitled to practise the profession regulated by the
    licensing authority in the province in question; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} was consulted in that province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder that has received a request referred to in
  subsection (1) must, after the end of each quarter and in accordance
  with subsection (3), provide the provincial professional licensing
  authority with a report containing the following information in
  respect of each client referred to in subsection (1) who was validly
  registered at any time during the relevant quarter, together with
  details of any changes to the information that occurred during the
  quarter:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the client's given name, surname and date of birth;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the postal code for the address that was provided
    under paragraph 279(2)(b), together with the province specified in
    the address;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and business address of the
    health care practitioner who signed the medical document, together
    with the number assigned by the province to the health care
    practitioner's authorization to practise;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis specified in
    the medical document;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the period of use specified in the medical
    document;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the medical document was signed
    by the health care practitioner; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each shipment of cannabis products that was
    sent or delivered during the quarter,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date it was sent or delivered,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that was sent or
      delivered, expressed in grams, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to
      the Act to which the cannabis products belong.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quarterly deadlines

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The report must be provided to the provincial
  professional licensing authority no later than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} April 30 of a given year for the quarter beginning
    on January 1 and ending on March 31 of that year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} July 31 of a given year for the quarter beginning
    on April 1 and ending on June 30 of that year;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} October 31 of a given year for the quarter
    beginning on July 1 and ending on September 30 of that year; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} January 31 of a given year for the quarter
    beginning on October 1 and ending on December 31 of the previous
    year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First report

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the first quarter in respect
  of which a report must be provided is the quarter in which the request
  is received from the provincial professional licensing authority.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the holder has no information to include in a
  report for a quarter, they must send a notice to that effect to the
  provincial professional licensing authority by the relevant deadline
  referred to in subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of activities

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the holder ceases to conduct activities, they
  must, despite subsection (3), provide the provincial professional
  licensing authority with the report for the quarter in which the
  activities cease within 30 days after ceasing them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The provincial professional licensing authority may,
  at any time, send the holder a notice cancelling the request referred
  to in subsection (1), in which case the final quarter in respect of
  which a report must be provided is the one that precedes the quarter
  in which the notice is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission and format

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A holder, or former holder, that provides a report to
  a provincial professional licensing authority under this section must
  transmit it securely in an electronic format that is accessible to the
  licensing authority.

#### [Retention of Documents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850410 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verifications

- **[[301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-301 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must, each time that they do
  verifications under subsection 281(1), retain a document that contains
  details of the verifications and of any measures taken under
  subsections 281(3) to (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which it is prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents relating to registration

- **[[302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-302 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each registration application that they receive
    under subsection 279(1), together with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application that is based on a
      medical document, the original of the medical document or, in the
      case where the medical document has been returned under subsection
      284(6) or 286(6) or transferred under subsection 287(1), a copy of
      it that includes, if applicable, the information referred to in
      section 288, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application that is based on a
      registration certificate, the copy of the certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of each registration document that they
    provide under paragraph 282(2)(a) and of each updated registration
    document that they provide under subsection 285(4);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each amendment application referred to in section
    285 that they receive;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of each notice that they send or provide
    under subsection 284(3), (5) or (7), 286(3), (5) or (7), 290(3) or
    291(3);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} each notice referred to in paragraph 291(1)(a)
    or (b) that they receive; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} each notification referred to in paragraph
    284(1)(g) or 286(1)(e) that they receive.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention periods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The documents referred to in subsection (1) must be
  retained

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of documents referred to in paragraphs
    (1)(a), (c), (e) and (f), for at least two years after the day on
    which they are received; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the copies referred to in paragraphs
    (1)(b) and (d), for at least two years after the day on which the
    registration document or notice was provided or sent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Steps --- young persons

**[[303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303 .sectionLabel}** A holder of a licence
for sale must maintain documentation as to the steps that they take to
satisfy the conditions set out in sections 294 to 296 and retain each
version of the documentation for at least two years after the day on
which it is replaced by a new version or, if it has not been replaced,
for at least two years after the day on which the licence expires or is
revoked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purchase orders

- **[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must retain, in respect of each purchase
  order referred to in section 289 that they fill or cause to be filled,
  a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    client;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the given name and surname of the individual who
    placed the purchase order;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the purchase order was placed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the names they have assigned to the cannabis
    products that are sent or delivered and the brand names;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is sent or delivered;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are sent or
    delivered; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the address to which the cannabis products are sent
    or delivered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder must retain the document --- together with
  the relevant written purchase order or, in the case of a verbal
  purchase order, the record referred to in subsection 289(3) --- for at
  least two years after the day on which the document is prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communications with licensing authorities

- **[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence for sale must retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of each notice that they provide to a
    provincial professional licensing authority under section 277,
    together with the copy that they provide to the Minister under that
    section;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of each request that they receive from a
    provincial professional licensing authority under subsection 299(1),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the request, together with any
      supporting documentation received,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a written record of the date on which the
      request and any documentation are received,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the information that they disclose in
      response to the request,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a written record of the date on which the
      information is disclosed, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a written record of the steps that are taken to
      ensure that the information is securely transmitted to the
      authority;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of each request or notice referred to in
    section 300 that they receive from a provincial professional
    licensing authority, together with a written record of the date on
    which it is received;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of each report that they provide to a
    provincial professional licensing authority under section 300,
    together with a written record of the date on which it is provided
    and the steps that are taken to ensure that it is securely
    transmitted; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a copy of any notice that they send to a provincial
    professional licensing authority under subsection 300(5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention periods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The documents referred to in subsection (1) must be
  retained for at least two years after

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the documents referred to in
    paragraph (1)(a), the day on which the notice is provided to the
    licensing authority;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the documents referred to in
    paragraph (1)(b), the day on which the information is disclosed to
    the licensing authority;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of documents referred to in paragraph
    (1)(c), the day on which the request or notice is received from the
    licensing authority;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of documents referred to in paragraph
    (1)(d), the day by which the holder is required to provide the
    report to the licensing authority; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a notice referred to in paragraph
    (1)(e), the end of the quarter to which the notice relates.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Registration with Minister]{.HTitleText2} {#h-850465 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850466 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjacent land

**[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraphs 312(3)(g) and 326(1)(b), a parcel of land is considered to be
adjacent to another parcel of land if its boundary has at least one
point in common with the boundary of the other parcel of land.

#### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850469 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature and statement

- **[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application that is submitted under this Division must be signed and
  dated by the individual who is submitting it and must include a
  statement by the individual confirming that the information included
  in the application is correct and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsible adult

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the application is signed by an adult who is
  responsible for the applicant, it must include a statement by the
  adult confirming that they are responsible for the applicant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

**[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on
receiving an application under this Division, require the submission of
any additional information that pertains to the information contained in
the application and that is necessary for the Minister to consider the
application.

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Registration, Renewal, Amendment and Revocation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850477 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility -- registered person

- **[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual is eligible to be a registered person only if they
  ordinarily reside in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility -- production for own
  medical purposes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual is eligible to produce cannabis for
  their own medical purposes as a registered person only if they are an
  adult.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior offences

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An individual is not eligible to produce cannabis for
  their own medical purposes as a registered person if, within the
  preceding 10 years, they have been convicted, as an adult, of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a sale, distribution or export offence that was
    committed while they were authorized to produce cannabis under this
    Division;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a designated cannabis offence that was committed
    while they were authorized under the [Controlled Drugs and
    Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) --- other than under the former
    [Marihuana Medical Access
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-227) --- to produce a
    substance that was referred to in item 1 of Schedule II to that Act,
    as that item read immediately before the coming into force of
    subsection 204(1) of the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a designated marihuana offence that was committed
    while they were authorized to produce marihuana

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} under the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
      Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), other than under the former [Access to
      Cannabis for Medical Purposes
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by virtue of an injunction order issued by a
      court; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an offence committed outside Canada that, if
    committed in Canada, would have constituted an offence referred to
    in any of paragraphs (a) to (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[designated cannabis offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated cannabis offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an offence --- in relation to a substance that
        was referred to in item 1 of Schedule II to the [Controlled
        Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), as that item read
        immediately before the coming into force of subsection 204(1) of
        the Act --- under section 5 of the [Controlled Drugs and
        Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), or under section 6 of that
        Act except with respect to importation; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a conspiracy or an attempt to commit, being an
        accessory after the fact in relation to, or any counselling in
        relation to, an offence referred to in paragraph
        (a). ([infraction désignée relativement au chanvre
        indien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[designated marihuana offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated marihuana offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an offence, in relation to marihuana, under
        section 5 of the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
        Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), or under section 6 of that Act except
        with respect to importation; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a conspiracy or an attempt to commit, being an
        accessory after the fact in relation to, or any counselling in
        relation to, an offence referred to in paragraph
        (a). ([infraction désignée relativement à la
        marihuana]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[former Marihuana Medical Access Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Marihuana Medical Access
      Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the regulations made by
      Order in Council P.C. 2001-1146 of June 14, 2001 and registered as
      SOR/2001-227. ([ancien Règlement sur l'accès à la marihuana à des
      fins médicales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marihuana]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marihuana]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the substance referred to
      as "Cannabis (marihuana)" in subitem 1(2) of Schedule II to the
      [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), as that
      subitem read immediately before the coming into force of
      subsection 204(1) of the Act. ([marihuana]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[sale, distribution or export offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sale, distribution or export offence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an offence under subsection 9(1) or (2), 10(1)
        or (2) or 11(1) or (2) of the Act, with the exception of an
        offence under subsection 11(1) with respect to importation;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an offence under subsection 14(1) of the Act in
        respect of the commission of an offence referred to in paragraph
        (a); or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a conspiracy or an attempt to commit, being an
        accessory after the fact in relation to, or any counselling in
        relation to, an offence referred to in paragraph (a) or
        (b). ([infraction relative à la vente, à la distribution ou à
        l'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single registration

**[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** An individual is not
eligible to be registered under this Division more than once at any one
time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility --- designated person

- **[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual is eligible to be a designated person only if they are an
  adult who ordinarily resides in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior offences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual is not eligible to be a designated
  person if, within the preceding 10 years, they

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have been convicted, as an adult, of a designated
    offence or a controlled substance offence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have been convicted, as an adult, of an offence
    committed outside Canada that, if committed in Canada, would have
    constituted an offence referred to in paragraph (a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} were a [[young person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} who
    received an [[adult sentence]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms
    are defined in subsection 2(1) of the Youth Criminal Justice Act, in
    respect of an offence referred to in paragraph (a); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} received a sentence --- for an offence they
    committed outside Canada when they were at least 14 years old but
    less than 18 years old that, if committed in Canada, would have
    constituted an offence referred to in paragraph (a) --- that was
    longer than the maximum youth sentence that could have been imposed
    under the [Youth Criminal Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5) for such an
    offence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration application

- **[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before registering an individual
  under this Division, the Minister must receive an application for
  registration and the original of the individual's medical document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Basic information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The registration application must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's given name, surname and date of
    birth;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless the applicant intends to produce cannabis
    for their own medical purposes, either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place in Canada where the
      applicant ordinarily resides, as well as, if applicable, their
      telephone number, facsimile number and email address, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant ordinarily resides in Canada
      but does not ordinarily reside at a specific place, the address,
      as well as, if applicable, the telephone number, facsimile number
      and email address of a shelter, hostel or similar institution,
      located in Canada, that provides them with food, lodging or other
      social services;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the mailing address of the place referred to in
    paragraph (b) if different from the address provided under that
    paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the place referred to in subparagraph (b)(i) is
    an establishment that is not a private residence, the type and name
    of the establishment;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the given name, surname and date of
    birth of one or more adults who are responsible for the applicant,
    including any adult who is signing the application;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an indication that the individual who is signing
    the application will

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} comply with the possession limit referred to in
      section 266 that applies to them, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they are not the applicant, take reasonable
      steps to ensure that the applicant will comply with the possession
      limits that apply to them;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant intends to produce cannabis for
      their own medical purposes,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} cannabis is to be produced for the medical
      purposes of the applicant by a designated person, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} cannabis will not be produced under the
      registration; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant intends to produce cannabis for
    their own medical purposes, or to have it produced by a designated
    person, an indication that the individual who is signing the
    application will

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to ensure the security of
      the cannabis in their possession that they are authorized to
      produce, or that is produced for the medical purposes of the
      applicant, under this Division, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they are not the applicant, ensure that the
      applicant takes reasonable steps to ensure the security of the
      cannabis in the applicant's possession that has been produced
      under this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production for own medical purposes

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant intends to produce cannabis for
  their own medical purposes, the application must also include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place in Canada where they
    ordinarily reside, as well as, if applicable, their telephone
    number, facsimile number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the information referred to in
    paragraphs (2)(c) and (d) as it relates to the place referred to in
    paragraph (a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication that, within the 10 years preceding
    the application, they have not been convicted, as an adult, of an
    offence referred to in subsection 309(3);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an indication that they will comply with the limit
    on the number of cannabis plants under production, as specified in
    the registration certificate to be issued under subsection 313(1);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the full address of the site where the proposed
    production of cannabis plants is to be conducted;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the proposed production
    area is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} entirely indoors,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} entirely outdoors, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} partly indoors and partly outdoors; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if there is to be any outdoor production, an
    indication that the site referred to in paragraph (e) is not
    adjacent to a school, public playground, daycare facility or other
    public place frequented mainly by young persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production by designated person

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If cannabis is to be produced by a designated person,
  the application must include a declaration by the designated person
  that contains

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraph (2)(a) and
    subparagraph (2)(b)(i) --- and, if applicable, paragraphs (2)(c)
    and (d) --- in respect of that person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraphs (3)(e) to
    (g);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} within the 10 years preceding the day on which
      the declaration is made, they have not been convicted of an
      offence referred to in paragraph 311(2)(a) or (b) or received a
      sentence referred to in paragraph 311(2)(c) or (d),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} they will take reasonable steps to ensure the
      security of the cannabis in their possession that they produce
      under this Division, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} they will comply with the limit on the number
      of cannabis plants under production, as specified in the document
      to be provided under subsection 313(3); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a document, issued by a Canadian police force
    within the 90 days preceding the date on which the application is
    submitted, establishing that, within the 10 years preceding the date
    on which the document is issued, the designated person has not been
    convicted of an offence referred to in paragraph 311(2)(a) or
    received a sentence referred to in paragraph 311(2)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature and statement --- designated
  person

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The declaration must be signed and dated by the
  designated person and must include a statement by them confirming that
  the information contained in the declaration is correct and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent of owner

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the proposed site for the production of cannabis
  plants is not the ordinary place of residence of, and is not owned by,
  the applicant or, if applicable, the designated person, the
  application must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the full name, address and telephone number of the
    owner of the site; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a declaration, signed and dated by the site's owner
    --- or, if the owner is a corporation, by an authorized
    representative of the owner --- consenting to the production of
    cannabis at the site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant without ordinary residence

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If an institution's address is included in the
  application in accordance with subparagraph (2)(b)(ii), the
  application must include a statement that is signed and dated by a
  manager of the institution confirming that it provides food, lodging
  or other social services to the applicant.

- [SOR/2019-206, s. 69](#1143465-1204827){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1143465-1204827.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-312-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 312"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration with Minister

- **[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the requirements set out in section 312 are met, the Minister must,
  subject to section 317, register the applicant and issue them a
  registration certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The registration certificate must contain the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    registered person and, if applicable, the designated person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address specified in the application under
    paragraph 312(2)(b) and, if applicable, the address of the place
    where the designated person ordinarily resides;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of any
    adults who are named in the application under paragraph 312(2)(e);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a unique registration number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name of the health care practitioner who
    provided the medical document that forms the basis for the
    registration;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in
    grams, that is specified in the medical document that forms the
    basis for the registration;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the maximum quantity of dried cannabis, expressed
    in grams, that the registered person is authorized to possess by
    virtue of the registration, as determined in accordance with
    subsection 266(3) or 267(3), as the case may be;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the registration;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiry of the registration;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the type of production that is
    authorized, namely, production by the registered person or
    production by a designated person;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the full address of the site where
    the production of cannabis plants is authorized by virtue of the
    registration;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, an indication of whether the
    authorized production area is entirely indoors, entirely outdoors or
    partly indoors and partly outdoors; and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the maximum number of cannabis
    plants, determined in accordance with section 325, that may be under
    production at the production site by virtue of the registration and,
    if applicable, the maximum number of plants for each indoor and
    outdoor production period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Document for designated person

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a designated person is named in the registration
  certificate, the Minister must provide them with a document containing
  information relating to the production of cannabis that is authorized,
  including the information referred to in paragraphs (2)(g) to (m) and
  the given name and surname of any adults who are named in the
  registration certificate under paragraph (2)(c).
