- [SOR/2020-149, s. 3](#1252268-1252682){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1252268-1252682.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-313-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 313"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry of registration

**[[313.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313.1 .sectionLabel}** The registration of
a registered person expires at the end of the period of validity of the
medical document that forms the basis for the registration, as
determined in accordance with subsections 273(4) and (5).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-149, s. 4](#1252270-1252686){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1252270-1252686.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to renew registration

- **[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} To
  renew a registration, the registered person, or an adult who is
  responsible for them, must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} submit an application to the Minister that includes
    the registration number and the information and documents required
    under subsections 312(2) to (7); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a new medical document is sent to the
    Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of registration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister has received an application and
  medical document under subsection (1) but has not notified the
  applicant of the Minister's decision in respect of the application
  before the current registration expires, the registration remains
  valid until the Minister notifies the applicant --- and, in the case
  of a refusal to renew the registration, the designated person, if any
  --- of the decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a renewal application has been submitted in
  accordance with paragraph (1)(a) and the Minister has received a new
  medical document under paragraph (1)(b), the Minister must, subject to
  section 317,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} renew the registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the registered person with a new
    registration certificate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if there is a designated person, provide them with
    an updated version of the document referred to in subsection 313(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of renewal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a renewed registration
  replaces the previous one.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to amend registration

- **[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), to amend
  any of the information in a registration certificate, the registered
  person, or an adult who is responsible for them, must submit an
  application to the Minister that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registration number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the proposed amendment and the
    supporting reasons for it;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information and documents referred to in
    section 312 that are relevant to the proposed amendment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the event that has
    necessitated the application; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if there has been a change to the given name or
    surname of an individual who is named in the registration
    certificate under paragraph 313(2)(a) or (c), proof of the change.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New medical document

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amendment application cannot be submitted in
  respect of a new medical document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an application is submitted in accordance with
  subsection (1), the Minister must, subject to section 317,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} amend the registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the registered person with an amended
    registration certificate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if there is a designated person, provide them with
    an updated version of the document referred to in subsection 313(3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequences of renewal or amendment

- **[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  as a result of the renewal or amendment of a registration, an
  individual ceases to be a designated person, the Minister must notify
  them of the loss of their authorization to produce cannabis under the
  registration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of location

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, as a result of a renewal or amendment, the
  location of the authorized site for the production of cannabis plants
  --- or the place of residence of the registered person or designated
  person --- is changed, the Minister may specify the period during
  which the registered person or, if applicable, the designated person
  is authorized to transport cannabis from the former site or place of
  residence to the new site or place of residence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to register, renew or amend

- **[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must refuse to register an applicant or to renew or amend a
  registration if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant is not eligible under subsection
    309(1) or section 310;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the medical document that forms the basis for the
    application does not meet all of the requirements of section 273 or
    is no longer valid;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the time the medical document was provided to
    the applicant, the individual who provided it

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} was not a health care practitioner, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} was not entitled to practise their profession in
      the province in which the applicant consulted with them;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname or date of birth of the
    applicant is different from the given name, surname or date of birth
    that appears on the medical document;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who provided the
    medical document notifies the Minister in writing that the use of
    cannabis by the applicant is no longer supported for clinical
    reasons;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
    false or misleading information has, or false or falsified documents
    have, been provided in, or in support of, the application;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the registration, renewal or amendment would result
    in the applicant or, if applicable, the designated person being
    authorized to produce cannabis plants under more than two
    registrations;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the registration, renewal or amendment would result
    in the proposed site for the production of cannabis plants being
    authorized under more than four registrations;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the applicant intends to produce
    cannabis for their own medical purposes, the applicant is not
    eligible under subsection 309(2) or (3); and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case where cannabis is to be produced by a
    designated person, the individual who has been designated is not
    eligible under section 311.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to refuse

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refuse to register an applicant or
  renew or amend a registration if, in the case where cannabis is to be
  produced by the applicant or a designated person, the registration,
  renewal or amendment is likely to create a risk to public health or
  public safety, including the risk of cannabis being diverted to an
  illicit market or activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice and opportunity to be heard

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before refusing to register an applicant or refusing
  to amend or renew a registration, the Minister must send the applicant
  a written notice that sets out the reason for the proposed refusal and
  give them an opportunity to be heard.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of registration

- **[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a registration if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registered person is not eligible under section
    309;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the designated person is not eligible under section
    311;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the registration was issued, amended or renewed on
    the basis of false or misleading information or false or falsified
    documents;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who provided the
    medical document that forms the basis for the registration notifies
    the Minister in writing that the use of cannabis by the registered
    person is no longer supported for clinical reasons;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the registered person --- or an adult who is named
    in the registration certificate under paragraph 313(2)(c) ---
    requests, in writing, that the registration be revoked; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the registered person dies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of excess registrations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a site for the production of cannabis plants is
  authorized under more than four registrations, the Minister must
  revoke the excess registrations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to revoke

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke a registration if, in the
  case where the registered person or designated person is authorized to
  produce cannabis, the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  the revocation is necessary to protect public health or public safety,
  including to prevent cannabis from being diverted to an illicit market
  or activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of revocation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a registration, the Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where it is to be revoked for a reason
    set out in any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (d) or subsection (2) or (3),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} send the registered person a written notice that
      sets out the reason for the proposed revocation, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} give them an opportunity to be heard; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, send the designated person a written
    notice of the proposed revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if a registration is revoked,
  the activities that were previously authorized under Subdivision B by
  virtue of the registration cease to be authorized.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of revocation

- **[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  registration is revoked and the Minister is aware that the
  registration has formed the basis for a registration with a holder of
  a licence for sale under Division 1 of this Part, the Minister must
  provide the holder with a notice of revocation that includes the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    individual whose registration has been revoked;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registration number of the revoked
    registration; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to notify

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for sale that is notified under
  subsection (1) must, if within the previous 48 hours they have asked a
  holder of a licence for processing or cultivation to send or deliver
  cannabis products to, or for, the individual whose registration has
  been revoked, notify the holder of the licence for processing or
  cultivation, in writing, without delay.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence for processing or cultivation
  that is notified under subsection (2) must not send or deliver the
  cannabis products that they had been asked to send or deliver.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Production]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850671 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[production
site]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Subdivision, [[production site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a registered person, the site, if
    any, for the production of cannabis plants that is specified in
    their registration certificate under paragraph 313(2)(k); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a designated person, the site for the
    production of cannabis plants that is specified in the document that
    the person receives under subsection 313(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cumulative quantities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantities of cannabis that a registered person
    is authorized to obtain by cultivation, propagation and harvesting,
    or to send, deliver, transport or possess, by virtue of a
    registration under this Division are in addition to any other
    quantities that they are permitted to obtain by cultivation,
    propagation and harvesting, or to send, deliver, transport or
    possess, under the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the quantities of cannabis that a designated person
    is authorized to obtain by cultivation, propagation and harvesting,
    or to send, deliver, transport, sell or possess, by virtue of a
    registration under this Division are in addition to any other
    quantities that they are permitted to obtain by cultivation,
    propagation and harvesting, or to send, deliver, transport, sell or
    possess, under the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production by registered person

- **[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered person who is registered to produce cannabis for their own
  medical purposes is, in accordance with the registration and the
  provisions of this Division, authorized to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} obtain by cultivation, propagation and harvesting
    at the production site a quantity of cannabis plants that does not
    exceed the maximum number of plants that is specified in the
    registration certificate under paragraph 313(2)(m);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the production site is different from the place
    where the registered person ordinarily resides,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 326(2), transport directly
      from the place of residence to the production site a total
      quantity of cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds that, taking
      into account the seed-to-plant ratio specified in subsection
      290(2), does not exceed the equivalent of the maximum number of
      plants that is specified in the registration certificate under
      paragraph 313(2)(m), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} transport cannabis, other than cannabis plants
      or cannabis plant seeds, directly from the production site to the
      place of residence; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the registration has been renewed or amended
    and, as a result, the location of the production site or the place
    of residence of the registered person is changed, transport cannabis
    directly from the former production site or place of residence to
    the new production site or place of residence within any period that
    the Minister may specify under subsection 316(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession of cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A registered person referred to in subsection (1) is
  authorized to possess the cannabis that they are authorized to obtain
  by cultivation, propagation and harvesting or to transport under that
  subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production by designated person

- **[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  designated person is, in accordance with the registration and the
  provisions of this Division, authorized to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} obtain by cultivation, propagation and harvesting
    at the production site a quantity of cannabis plants that does not
    exceed the maximum number of plants that is specified in the
    document that the designated person receives under subsection
    313(3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the production site is different from the place
    where the designated person ordinarily resides,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 326(2), transport directly
      from the place of residence to the production site a total
      quantity of cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds that, taking
      into account the seed-to-plant ratio specified in subsection
      290(2), does not exceed the equivalent of the maximum number of
      plants that is specified in the document that the designated
      person receives under subsection 313(3), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} transport cannabis, other than cannabis plants
      or cannabis plant seeds, directly from the production site to the
      place of residence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} send, deliver, transport or sell to the registered
    person --- or an adult who is named in the document that the
    designated person receives under subsection 313(3) --- a quantity of
    cannabis, other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, that
    does not exceed the equivalent of the maximum quantity of dried
    cannabis that is specified in the document; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the registration has been renewed or amended
    and, as a result, the location of the production site or the place
    of residence of the designated person is changed, transport cannabis
    directly from the former production site or place of residence to
    the new production site or place of residence within any period that
    the Minister may specify under subsection 316(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parcel --- requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A designated person who sends or has cannabis
  delivered under paragraph (1)(c) must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prepare the parcel in a manner that ensures the
    security of its contents, such that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it will not open or permit the escape of its
      contents during handling or transportation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is sealed so that it cannot be opened without
      the seal being broken,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it prevents the escape of odours associated
      with cannabis plant material, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} it prevents the contents from being identified
      without it being opened; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use a method that ensures the tracking and
    safekeeping of the parcel during transportation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession of cannabis

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A designated person is authorized to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} possess the cannabis plants and cannabis plant
    seeds that they are authorized to obtain by cultivation, propagation
    and harvesting or to transport under paragraph (1)(a), (b) or (d);
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} possess in a public place the cannabis, other than
    cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, that they are authorized to
    send, deliver, transport or sell under paragraphs (1)(b) to (d).

  ajax-after="1143467-1204830.html"}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-322-20181017.html "Link to previous version of section 322"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation by registered person

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** If a designated person
is specified in a registration certificate, the registered person may,
if they are an adult, participate in the activities that the designated
person is authorized to conduct under subsection 322(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former designated person

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** An individual who
ceases to be a designated person may, within seven days after ceasing to
be designated, conduct the activities referred to in paragraph 322(1)(c)
in accordance with that paragraph unless the registration has expired or
has been revoked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum number of plants

- **[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the production area is entirely indoors, the maximum number of
  cannabis plants that may be under production at a production site by
  virtue of a registration is determined by the formula

  ::: {#850719 .Subsection}
  \[(A × 365) ÷ (B × 3C)\] × 1.2

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in grams,
      indicated in the medical document that forms the basis for the
      registration;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is 30 g, being the expected yield of dried cannabis per plant; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a constant equal to 1, representing the growth cycle of a
      cannabis plant from seeding to harvesting.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outdoor production only

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the production area is entirely outdoors, the
  maximum number of cannabis plants that may be under production at a
  production site by virtue of a registration is determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#850726 .Subsection}
  \[(A × 365) ÷ (B × C)\] × 1.3

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in grams,
      indicated in the medical document that forms the basis for the
      registration;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is 250 g, being the expected yield of dried cannabis per plant;
      and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a constant equal to 1, representing the growth cycle of a
      cannabis plant from seeding to harvesting.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indoor and outdoor production

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the production area is partly indoors and partly
  outdoors, the maximum number of cannabis plants that may be under
  production at a production site by virtue of a registration is
  determined

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the indoor production period, by the formula

    ::: {#850734 .Paragraph}
    \[(A × 182.5) ÷ (B × 2C)\] × 1.2

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in grams,
        indicated in the medical document that forms the basis for the
        registration,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is 30 g, being the expected yield of dried cannabis per plant,
        and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is a constant equal to 1, representing the growth cycle of a
        cannabis plant from seeding to harvesting; and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the outdoor production period, by the formula

    ::: {#850740 .Paragraph}
    \[(A × 182.5) ÷ (B × C)\] × 1.3

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the daily quantity of dried cannabis, expressed in grams,
        indicated in the medical document that forms the basis for the
        registration,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is 250 g, being the expected yield of dried cannabis per plant,
        and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is a constant equal to 1, representing the growth cycle of a
        cannabis plant from seeding to harvesting.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the maximum number of cannabis plants determined
  under this section is not a whole number, it is to be rounded to the
  next highest whole number.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- production of plants

- **[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual who is authorized to produce cannabis plants under this
  Division must not cultivate, propagate or harvest them

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} indoors and outdoors at the same time; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} outdoors if the production site is adjacent to a
    school, public playground, daycare facility or other public place
    frequented mainly by young persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit --- transportation of plants

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the production site of an individual who is
  authorized to produce cannabis plants under this Division is different
  from the place where the individual ordinarily resides, the individual
  must not transport to the production site cannabis plants that were
  ordered in accordance with section 289 if it has been more than seven
  days after the individual received the plants at their place of
  residence.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Security Obligations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850754 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security of cannabis and documents

- **[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered person who is authorized to produce cannabis, or for whom
  cannabis may be produced by a designated person, under this Division
  --- or, if applicable, any adult who is named in the registration
  certificate of the registered person --- must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to ensure the security of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis in their possession that was
      produced under this Division, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the registration certificate, if they possess
      it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} report the theft or loss of anything referred to in
    paragraph (a) to a police force within 24 hours after becoming aware
    of the theft or loss; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} report the theft or loss of anything referred to in
    paragraph (a) to the Minister, in writing, within 72 hours after
    becoming aware of the theft or loss and include confirmation that
    the requirement set out in paragraph (b) has been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requirements set out in subsection (1) also apply
  to a designated person in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis in their possession that they produced
    under this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the document referred to in subsection 313(3).

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850767 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to licensing authorities

- **[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  any time after receiving a medical document that forms the basis for
  an application under this Division, the Minister may disclose, in
  respect of the health care practitioner who is named in the medical
  document, the following information to the provincial professional
  licensing authority for the province identified in the medical
  document under paragraph 273(1)(b):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and business address of the
    health care practitioner, together with the number assigned by the
    province to the health care practitioner's authorization to
    practise;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis that is
    specified in the medical document; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the province that is specified in the
    application under paragraph 312(2)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure after registration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At any time after registering an individual under
  this Division, the Minister may disclose, in respect of the health
  care practitioner who is named in the medical document that forms the
  basis for the registration, the following information to the
  provincial professional licensing authority for the province
  identified in the document under paragraph 273(1)(b):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    individual who is or was registered with the Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the postal code for the address of the place
    indicated in the medical document as the place where the individual
    ordinarily resides;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the period of use that is specified in the medical
    document; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the medical document was signed
    by the health care practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[health care
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[health care
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who is, or was,
  a medical practitioner or nurse practitioner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to police

**[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
disclose any of the following information to a Canadian police force, or
a member of a Canadian police force, that requests the information in
the course of an investigation under the Act, on condition that the
information is used only for the purposes of the investigation or the
administration and enforcement of the Act or these Regulations:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a named individual, whether the
  individual is a registered person, a designated person or an adult who
  is named in a registration certificate under paragraph 313(2)(c);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a specified address, whether the
  address is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the place where a registered person or designated
    person ordinarily resides and, if so, the name of that person and
    the registration number, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the site where the production of cannabis plants
    is authorized under a registration and, if so, the registration
    number, the name of the individual who is authorized to produce and,
    if that individual is a designated person and the registered person
    is an adult, the name of the registered person; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a registration,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and date of birth of the
    registered person and, if applicable, the designated person and any
    adult who is named in the registration certificate under paragraph
    313(2)(c),

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the full address of the place where the registered
    person and, if applicable, the designated person ordinarily reside,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the registration number,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the maximum quantity of dried cannabis that the
    registered person is authorized to possess, as indicated in the
    registration certificate,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the effective date and date of expiry of the
    registration,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the registration remains valid by virtue of
    subsection 314(2), the status of the renewal application,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the full address of the site where the production
    of cannabis plants is authorized,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} whether the authorized production area is
    indoors, outdoors or partly indoors and partly outdoors, and

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the maximum number of cannabis plants that may be
    produced at the production site and, if applicable, the maximum
    number of cannabis plants that may be produced for each indoor and
    outdoor production period.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Health Professionals and Hospitals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-850798 .Subheading}

#### [Health Care Practitioners]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850799 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security of cannabis products

**[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** A health care
practitioner must, in respect of cannabis products that they possess for
the practice of their profession,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect them from theft or
  loss; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} report any theft or loss to the Minister within 10
  days after becoming aware of the theft or loss.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Returned cannabis products

- **[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  health care practitioner must, if they accept cannabis products that
  are returned under subsection 292(3), retain a document that contains
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the individual who is returning the
    cannabis products;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the location at which the cannabis
    products are received;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which they are received;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is received; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis products, including
    their brand names.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The health care practitioner is not required to
  include the information referred to in paragraphs (1)(d) and (e) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are unable to ascertain the information
    without unsealing a parcel that meets the requirements of paragraph
    292(4)(a) and the parcel is subsequently sent or delivered to the
    holder of the licence that originally sold or distributed the
    cannabis products to or for the client; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis accessory that contains
    cannabis, they are unable to ascertain the information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The document must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which it is prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former health care practitioner

**[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** If an individual ceases
to be a health care practitioner, they must, in respect of any document
that they were required to retain under this Part and for which the
retention period has not yet ended,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the document continues to be retained
  until the end of the retention period; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, of the address of
  the place of business at which the document is retained and of any
  subsequent change to the address.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to licensing authority

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
disclose, in writing, factual information about a health care
practitioner --- in relation to cannabis --- that has been obtained
under the Act or the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for a
  province in which the health care practitioner is, or was, entitled to
  practise if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the licensing authority submits to the Minister a
    written request that sets out the health care practitioner's name
    and address, a description of the information being requested and a
    statement that the information is required for the purpose of
    assisting an investigation by the authority,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
    that the health care practitioner has contravened, in relation to
    cannabis, a rule of conduct established by the licensing authority,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister becomes aware that the health care
    practitioner has been convicted of

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a designated offence,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a controlled substance offence in relation to
      cannabis,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the former [Access to Cannabis
      for Medical Purposes Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230)
      or the former [Marihuana for Medical Purposes
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2013-119), or

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
    that the health care practitioner has contravened this Part or the
    former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230), the former [Marihuana
    for Medical Purposes Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2013-119) or
    --- in relation to cannabis --- the [Narcotic Control
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for a
  province in which the health care practitioner is not entitled to
  practise if the licensing authority submits to the Minister

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a written request that sets out the health care
    practitioner's name and address and a description of the information
    being requested, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that shows that

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner has applied to the
      licensing authority to practise in that province, or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the licensing authority has reasonable grounds to
      believe that the health care practitioner is practising in that
      province without being authorized to do so.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[health care
practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** In sections 335 to 337,
[[health care practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is entitled under the laws of a
  province to practise medicine in that province; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual who meets the requirements of
  paragraph (a) of the definition [[nurse
  practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 264(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice from Minister

- **[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must, in the circumstances set out in subsection (2), issue a
  notice to the persons and pharmacies specified in subsection (3)
  advising them, as applicable, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holders of a licence for sale and the holders
    of a licence for processing that receive the notice must not send
    cannabis products to the health care practitioner who is named in
    the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any medical document that is signed by the health
    care practitioner who is named in the notice must not form the basis
    for registering a client if the medical document is signed after the
    day on which the notice is issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pharmacists practising in the notified pharmacies
    must not distribute or sell cannabis products on the basis of a
    medical document signed by, or a written order issued by, the health
    care practitioner who is named in the notice if the medical document
    is signed, or the written order is issued, after the day on which
    the notice is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circumstances --- mandatory issuance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be issued if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who is named in the
    notice has asked the Minister, in writing, to issue it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who is named in the
    notice has contravened, in relation to cannabis, a rule of conduct
    established by the provincial professional licensing authority for
    the province in which the health care practitioner is practising and
    the licensing authority has asked the Minister, in writing, to issue
    the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who is named in the
    notice has been convicted of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a designated offence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of these Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a controlled substance offence in relation to
      cannabis,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the former [Access to
      Cannabis for Medical Purposes
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) or the former
      [Marihuana for Medical Purposes
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2013-119), or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who is named in the
    notice has been named in a notice issued under subsection 189(2) or
    (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recipients of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A notice that is issued under this section must be
  issued to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who is named in the
    notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all holders of a licence for sale and holders of a
    licence for processing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all hospital pharmacies in the province in which
    the health care practitioner who is named in the notice is entitled
    to practise and is practising;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for
    the province in which the health care practitioner who is named in
    the notice is entitled to practise; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} on request, the provincial professional licensing
    authority for any province other than the province referred to in
    paragraph (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue a notice referred to in
  subsection (1) if he or she has reasonable grounds to believe that the
  health care practitioner who is named in the notice

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has conducted an activity referred to in section
    272 other than in accordance with that section;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has provided a medical document, or issued a
    written order, that contains false or misleading information;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, provided themselves
    with a medical document or issued a written order for themselves,
    contrary to accepted medical practice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, conducted an
    activity referred to in section 272 in respect of their spouse,
    common-law partner, parent or child, including a child adopted in
    fact, contrary to accepted medical practice; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is unable to account for a quantity of cannabis for
    which they were responsible under this Part, the [Narcotic Control
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) or the former [Access
    to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preconditions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing a notice under subsection (4), the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consult with the provincial professional licensing
    authority for the province in which the health care practitioner to
    whom the notice relates is entitled to practise;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} send to the health care practitioner a written
    notice that sets out the reasons why a notice is being considered
    and give him or her an opportunity to present reasons why the notice
    should not be issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consider

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any reasons that have been presented by the
      health care practitioner under paragraph (b),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the compliance history of the health care
      practitioner in respect of the Act, the [Controlled Drugs and
      Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) and the regulations made or
      continued under either Act, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} whether the actions of the health care
      practitioner pose a significant risk to public health or public
      safety, including the risk of cannabis being diverted to an
      illicit market or activity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retraction of notice

- **[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must retract a notice that was issued under section 335 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner who is named in the
    notice has requested in writing that it be retracted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the health care practitioner has provided a letter
    from the provincial professional licensing authority for the
    province in which the health care practitioner is entitled to
    practise that states that the authority consents to the retraction
    of the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the notice was issued at the
    request of the health care practitioner, one year has elapsed since
    the notice was issued; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the notice was issued in the
    circumstance referred to in paragraph 335(2)(d), the notice that was
    issued under subsection 189(2) or (4) has been retracted under
    subsection 190(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to notify

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister retracts a notice issued under
  section 335, he or she must notify, in writing, the persons and
  pharmacies to which it was issued.

#### [Pharmacists]{.HTitleText3} {#h-850890 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibitions --- notified pharmacies

- **[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pharmacist who is practising in a pharmacy that has received a notice
  issued under section 335 must not distribute or sell cannabis products
  on the basis of a medical document signed, or a written order issued,
  by the health care practitioner who is named in the notice unless the
  medical document is signed, or the written order is issued, before the
  day on which the notice is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- pharmacist named in
  notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist who is practising in a pharmacy that has
  received a notice issued under section 344 must not distribute or sell
  cannabis products to the pharmacist who is named in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of retraction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The prohibitions referred to in subsections (1)
  and (2) cease to apply if the notice is retracted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- dispensing

**[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** A pharmacist must not
use an order --- including a written order --- to dispense a cannabis
product if the quantity of cannabis that would be dispensed, when added
to the quantity of cannabis that has already been dispensed under the
order, would exceed the quantity of cannabis specified in the order.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hospital pharmacists

- **[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 337, a pharmacist
  who is practising in a hospital may, if authorized by the individual
  in charge of the hospital,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} distribute, sell or return, in accordance with
    paragraph 348(2)(b) or subsection 348(4) or (7), cannabis products,
    other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, that have been
    received from a holder of a licence for sale or a holder of a
    licence for processing; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} distribute --- but not send --- or sell cannabis
    products, other than cannabis plants or cannabis plant seeds, to an
    employee of the hospital or a health care practitioner practising in
    the hospital, on receipt of an order, in writing, that has been
    signed and dated by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist in charge of the hospital's
      pharmacy, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a health care practitioner who is authorized by
      the individual in charge of the hospital to sign the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of signature

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before distributing or selling cannabis products
  under paragraph (1)(b), the pharmacist receiving the order must verify
  the signature on the order, if it is unknown to them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist must not conduct the activities referred
  to in subsection (1) if the pharmacist has been named in a notice that
  has been issued under section 344 and that has not been retracted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  does not include administering.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of documents

- **[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pharmacist who receives cannabis products from a person must retain a
  document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is received;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis products, including
    their brand names;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
    received; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and mailing address of the person from
    which the cannabis products are received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale or distribution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist who distributes or sells cannabis
  products on the basis of a medical document or written order must
  retain a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist's name or initials;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, initials and address of the health care
    practitioner who provided the medical document or issued the written
    order;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and mailing address of the individual for
    whom the cannabis products are distributed or sold;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is distributed or
    sold;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis products, including
    their brand names;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
    distributed or sold; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the number that the pharmacist has assigned to the
    medical document or written order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergency transfer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist who distributes or sells cannabis
  products for emergency purposes on the basis of an order made in
  accordance with subsection 348(4) must retain a document that contains
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the pharmacist or health
    care practitioner who signed the order;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the individual to whom the
    cannabis products are distributed or sold;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is distributed or
    sold;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis products, including
    their brand names; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
    distributed or sold.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of cannabis products

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A pharmacist who returns cannabis products must
  retain a document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person to which the
    cannabis products are returned;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is returned;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the cannabis products, including
    their brand names; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
    returned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The documents must be retained for at least two years
  after the day on which they are prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former pharmacists

**[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** If an individual ceases
to be a pharmacist, they must, in respect of any document that they were
required to retain under this Part and for which the retention period
has not yet ended,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the document continues to be retained
  until the end of the retention period; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, of the address of
  the place of business at which the document is retained and of any
  subsequent change to the address.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security of cannabis products

**[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** The pharmacist in
charge of a hospital's pharmacy must, in respect of cannabis products
that are on the pharmacy premises or for which the pharmacist is
responsible,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect them from theft or
  loss; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} report any theft or loss to the Minister within 10
  days after becoming aware of the theft or loss.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to licensing authority

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
disclose, in writing, factual information about a pharmacist --- in
relation to cannabis --- that has been obtained under the Act or the
[Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for a
  province in which the pharmacist is, or was, entitled to practise if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the licensing authority submits to the Minister a
    written request that sets out the pharmacist's name and address, a
    description of the information being requested and a statement that
    the information is required for the purpose of assisting an
    investigation by the authority,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
    that the pharmacist has contravened, in relation to cannabis, a rule
    of conduct established by the licensing authority,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister becomes aware that the pharmacist
    has been convicted of

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a designated offence,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a controlled substance offence in relation to
      cannabis,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the former [Access to Cannabis
      for Medical Purposes Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230),
      or

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
      cannabis, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
    that the pharmacist has contravened these Regulations, the former
    [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) or --- in relation to
    cannabis --- the [Narcotic Control
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for a
  province in which the pharmacist is not entitled to practise if the
  licensing authority submits to the Minister

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a written request that sets out the pharmacist's
    name and address and a description of the information being
    requested, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that shows that

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist has applied to the licensing
      authority to practise in that province, or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the licensing authority has reasonable grounds to
      believe that the pharmacist is practising in that province without
      being authorized to do so.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice from Minister

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must, in the circumstances set out in subsection (2), issue a
  notice to the following persons and pharmacies advising them that
  holders of a licence for sale, holders of a licence for processing and
  pharmacists practising in the notified pharmacies must not distribute
  or sell cannabis products to the pharmacist who is named in the
  notice:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all holders of a licence for sale and all holders
    of a licence for processing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all hospital pharmacies in the province in which
    the pharmacist who is named in the notice is entitled to practise
    and is practising;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the provincial professional licensing authority for
    the province in which the pharmacist who is named in the notice is
    entitled to practise; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} on request, the provincial professional licensing
    authority for any province other than the province referred to in
    paragraph (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circumstances --- mandatory issuance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be issued if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice asks the
    Minister, in writing, to issue the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice has
    contravened, in relation to cannabis, a rule of conduct established
    by the provincial professional licensing authority for the province
    in which the pharmacist is practising and the licensing authority
    has asked the Minister, in writing, to issue the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice has been
    convicted of an offence referred to in subparagraph 343(a)(iii); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice has been
    named in a notice issued under subsection 181(2) or (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue a notice referred to in
  subsection (1) if he or she has reasonable grounds to believe that the
  pharmacist who is named in the notice

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has conducted an activity referred to in section
    339 other than in accordance with that section;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has, on more than one occasion, distributed or sold
    a cannabis product to their spouse, common-law partner, parent or
    child, including a child adopted in fact, contrary to accepted
    pharmaceutical practice; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is unable to account for a quantity of cannabis
    products for which they were responsible under this Part, the
    [Narcotic Control Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) or
    the former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preconditions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing a notice under subsection (3), the
  Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consult with the provincial professional licensing
    authority for the province in which the pharmacist to whom the
    notice relates is entitled to practise;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} send to the pharmacist a written notice that sets
    out the reasons why a notice is being considered and give him or her
    an opportunity to present reasons why the notice should not be
    issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consider

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any reasons that have been presented by the
      pharmacist under paragraph (b),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the compliance history of the pharmacist in
      respect of the Act, the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
      Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) and the regulations made or continued under
      either Act, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} whether the actions of the pharmacist pose a
      significant risk to public health or public safety, including the
      risk of cannabis being diverted to an illicit market or activity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retraction of notice

- **[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must retract a notice that was issued under section 344 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist who is named in the notice has
    requested in writing that it be retracted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the pharmacist has provided a letter from the
    provincial professional licensing authority for the province in
    which the pharmacist is entitled to practise that states that the
    authority consents to the retraction of the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the notice was issued at the
    request of the pharmacist, one year has elapsed since the notice was
    issued; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the notice was issued in the
    circumstance referred to in paragraph 344(2)(d), the notice that was
    issued under subsection 181(2) or (4) has been retracted under
    subsection 182(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to notify

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister retracts a notice issued under
  section 344, he or she must notify, in writing, the persons and
  pharmacies to which it was issued.

#### [Hospitals]{.HTitleText3} {#h-851004 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** In sections 347, 348,
350 and 351, [[distribute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include
administering.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security of cannabis products

**[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** An individual in charge
of a hospital must, in respect of cannabis products that they permit to
be administered, distributed or sold,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable steps to protect them from theft or
  loss; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} report any theft or loss to the Minister within 10
  days after becoming aware of the theft or loss.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration, distribution and sale

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual in charge of a hospital must not permit cannabis products
  to be administered, distributed or sold, except in accordance with
  this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hospital patients

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual in charge of a hospital may permit
  cannabis products, other than cannabis plants and cannabis plant
  seeds, received from a holder of a licence for sale or a holder of a
  licence for processing to be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} administered, on receipt of a medical document or
    written order, to an inpatient or outpatient of the hospital; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} distributed --- but not sent --- or sold, on
    receipt of a medical document or written order, to an individual
    referred to in paragraph (a) or an adult who is responsible for
    them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements --- distribution or sale

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An individual in charge of a hospital must, if they
  permit cannabis products to be distributed or sold under paragraph
  (2)(b), ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is distributed or
    sold does not exceed the equivalent of the lesser of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 30 times the daily quantity of dried cannabis
      that is specified in the medical document or written order, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 150 g of dried cannabis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis products are distributed or sold in
    the container in which they were received from the holder of the
    licence for sale or the holder of the licence for processing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a label is applied to the container in which the
    cannabis products were received indicating

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and profession of the
      health care practitioner who signed the medical document or
      written order,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the given name and surname of the patient,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis that is
      specified in the medical document or written order, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
      distributed or sold;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the patient or responsible adult is provided with
    the current version of the document entitled Consumer Information
    --- Cannabis, published by the Government of Canada on its website;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the patient or responsible adult is provided with a
    separate document containing the information referred to in
    paragraph (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergency purposes

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An individual in charge of a hospital may permit
  cannabis products, other than cannabis plants and cannabis plant
  seeds, received from a holder of a licence for sale or a holder of a
  licence for processing to be distributed or sold for emergency
  purposes to an employee of or a health care practitioner in another
  hospital on receipt of an order, in writing, that has been signed and
  dated by a pharmacist in the other hospital or a health care
  practitioner who is authorized by the individual in charge of the
  other hospital to order cannabis products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of signature

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An individual in charge of a hospital must not permit
  cannabis products to be distributed or sold under subsection (4)
  unless the signature on the order has been verified by the individual
  who distributes or sells the cannabis products, if it is unknown to
  them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution for research purposes

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An individual in charge of a hospital may permit
  cannabis products, other than cannabis plants and cannabis plant
  seeds, to be distributed for research purposes to an individual who is
  employed in a research laboratory in the hospital and who holds a
  licence for those purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return or destruction

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An individual in charge of a hospital may permit
  cannabis products to be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} returned to the holder of the licence for sale or
    the holder of the licence for processing from which the cannabis
    products were received, on receipt of a written request for the
    cannabis products that has been signed and dated by, or on behalf
    of, the holder; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} distributed or sold, for destruction, to a holder
    of a licence for sale, or a holder of a licence for processing, that
    is authorized to destroy cannabis that they did not produce, sell or
    distribute, on receipt of a written request for the cannabis
    products that has been signed and dated by, or on behalf of, the
    holder.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession --- hospital employees

**[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349 .sectionLabel}** A hospital employee is
authorized to possess cannabis products, other than cannabis plants or
cannabis plant seeds, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis products have been obtained in
  accordance with section 348 or 350; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employee requires the cannabis products for the
  purposes of, and in connection with, their employment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return and replacement

- **[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual to whom cannabis products are distributed or sold under
  paragraph 348(2)(b) may return them to a hospital employee who is
  authorized to distribute or sell cannabis products if the employee
  accepts the return.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of more than 30 g

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The individual returning the cannabis products must
  comply with the requirements set out in subsection 292(4) if they are
  returning more than the equivalent of 30 g of dried cannabis and are
  sending the cannabis products or having them delivered to the
  hospital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Replacement of cannabis products

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The individual in charge of the hospital may, subject
  to the limit referred to in paragraph 348(3)(a), permit cannabis
  products that have been returned to be replaced.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of documents

- **[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  individual who is in charge of a hospital must ensure that documents
  that contain the following information are retained:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of cannabis products that are received
    at the hospital,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to
      the Act to which the cannabis products belong and their brand
      names,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is received,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person from which
      the cannabis products are received, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
      received;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of cannabis products that are
    distributed or sold for a patient,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the given name and surname of the patient,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the given name, surname and profession of the
      health care practitioner who signed the relevant medical document
      or written order, together with the date on which it was signed,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the daily quantity of dried cannabis that is
      specified in the medical document or written order referred to in
      subparagraph (ii) and, if applicable, the period of use that is
      specified,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the class of cannabis set out in Schedule 4 to
      the Act to which the cannabis products belong,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is distributed or
      sold, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
      distributed or sold; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of cannabis products that are
    distributed or sold to a person that is authorized to sell or
    distribute such products --- other than an adult referred to in
    paragraph 266(1)(d) or (e) --- or that are returned under subsection
    348(7),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to which the cannabis
      products are distributed, sold or returned,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the cannabis products are
      distributed, sold or returned,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of cannabis that is distributed,
      sold or returned,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the brand names of the cannabis products, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cannabis products that are
      distributed, sold or returned under subsection 348(7), the address
      of the person to which they are distributed, sold or returned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The individual in charge of the hospital must ensure
  that the documents are retained for at least two years after the day
  on which they are prepared.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of operations --- hospital

**[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** If a hospital ceases
operations, the individual who was in charge of the hospital must, in
respect of any document that they were required to retain under this
Part and for which the retention period has not yet ended,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the document continues to be retained
  until the end of the retention period; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister, in writing, of the address of
  the place of business at which the document is retained and of any
  subsequent change to the address.

#### [Nurses]{.HTitleText3} {#h-851079 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to nursing statutory body

- **[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must disclose, in writing, factual information about a nurse
  --- in relation to cannabis --- that has been obtained under the Act
  or the [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nursing statutory body for a province in which
    the nurse is, or was, entitled to practise if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the nursing statutory body submits to the
      Minister a written request that sets out the nurse's name and
      address, a description of the information being requested and a
      statement that the information is required for the purpose of
      assisting an investigation by the body,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
      that the nurse has contravened, in relation to cannabis, a rule of
      conduct established by the nursing statutory body,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Minister becomes aware that the nurse has
      been convicted of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a designated offence,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a controlled substance offence in relation to
        cannabis,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the former [Access to
        Cannabis for Medical Purposes
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) or the former
        [Marihuana for Medical Purposes
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2013-119), or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of the [Narcotic Control
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) in relation to
        cannabis, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe
      that the nurse has contravened this Part, the former [Access to
      Cannabis for Medical Purposes
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230), the former [Marihuana
      for Medical Purposes Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2013-119)or
      --- in relation to cannabis --- the [Narcotic Control
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nursing statutory body for a province in which
    the nurse is not entitled to practise if the nursing statutory body
    submits to the Minister

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a written request that sets out the nurse's name
      and address and a description of the information being requested,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document that shows that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the nurse has applied to the nursing statutory
        body to practise in that province, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the nursing statutory body has reasonable
        grounds to believe that the nurse is practising in that province
        without being authorized to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[nurse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[nurse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual --- other than a
      nurse practitioner --- who is authorized by a nursing statutory
      body to practise nursing. ([infirmier]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[nursing statutory body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[nursing statutory body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the authority
      that is responsible for authorizing individuals to practise
      nursing in a province. ([organisme régissant la profession
      d'infirmier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

## [PART 15]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-851101 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licences --- former [Access to Cannabis
for Medical Purposes Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230)

**[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354 .sectionLabel}** A licence issued under
the former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230), as set out in column 1 of
the table to this section, is deemed to be a licence of the class set
out in column 2, and one of the licences of the subclass set out in
column 3 if the holder of the licence meets the requirements, if any, in
that column for that subclass.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licences --- [Narcotic Control
Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041)

- **[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence issued under the [Narcotic Control
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041) as they read
  immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into force,
  as set out in column 1 of the table to this section, is deemed to be a
  licence of the class set out in column 2 and, if applicable, one of
  the licences of the subclass set out in column 3 if the holder of the
  licence meets the requirements in that column and, despite subsections
  159(1) and (4) of the Act, continues in force until December 31, 2019,
  unless it is revoked before that date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licences not set out in table

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence issued under section 9.2 of the [Narcotic
  Control Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041), as they read
  immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into force,
  that is not set out in column 1 of the table to this section is deemed
  to be a licence for processing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence referred to in subsection (2)
  is not authorized to sell cannabis to any person unless they are
  authorized to do so as a condition of their licence.
