[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- sections 12, 15, 23
and 38

- **[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  a three-month period that begins on the day on which these Regulations
  come into force, sections 12, 15, 23 and 38 do not apply to a holder
  of a licence set out in column 3 of the table to section 354 or in
  items 1 to 4 and 6, column 2, of the table to section 355.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name of individuals to Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the licence must, within that period,
  provide the Minister with the names of the individuals who will be
  designated for the positions referred to in section 12, 15, 23 or 38,
  as the case may be.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quality assurance person

**[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357 .sectionLabel}** Every quality assurance
person designated under paragraph 75(1)(a) of the former [Access to
Cannabis for Medical Purposes
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) by the holder of a licence
set out in column 1 of the table to section 354 is deemed to be a
quality assurance person under subsection 19(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Senior person in charge

**[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** Every senior person in
charge designated under paragraph 32(1)(a) of the former [Access to
Cannabis for Medical Purposes
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) by the holder of a licence
set out in column 1 of the table to section 354 is deemed to be a
responsible person under subsection 37(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security clearance

- **[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a licence set out in column 3 of the table to section 354 or
  in column 2 of the table to section 355 must, within three months
  after the day on which these Regulations come into force,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide the Minister with the name and position of
    any individual who does not hold a security clearance, but is
    required to do so under section 50; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any individual referred to in
    paragraph (a) submits an application for a security clearance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- section 50

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual referred to in paragraph (1)(a) who is
  not ineligible to apply for a security clearance by virtue of section
  61 is not required to comply with section 50 until the occurrence of
  one of the following events:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the three-month period that begins on the day on
    which these Regulations come into force expires without the
    individual having submitted an application for a security clearance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister grants a security clearance to the
    individual;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individual is notified under subsection 55(2)
    that the Minister has refused to grant the security clearance; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual withdraws their application for the
    security clearance before the Minister makes a decision in respect
    of it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and labelling

**[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** For a six-month period
that begins on the day on which these Regulations come into force,
section 106 does not apply to a holder of a licence for sale for medical
purposes, as set out in item 3, column 3, of the table to section 354,
that sells, sends or delivers a cannabis product under section 289 or
291, if the holder complies with sections 80 to 86 and sections 90 and
91 of the former [Access to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) and the sale of that
cannabis product would have been permitted under those Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individual in charge of premises

- **[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every individual in charge of the
  premises to which a licence that is referred to in item 5, column 1,
  of the table to section 355 applies is deemed to be a senior person in
  charge under section 149.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualified person in charge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every qualified person in charge designated under
  paragraph 8.3(1)(a) of the [Narcotic Control
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041), as they read
  immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into force,
  by a holder of a licence referred to in item 5, column 1, of the table
  to section 355 is deemed to be the qualified person in charge under
  subsection 150(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate qualified person in charge

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every alternate qualified person in charge designated
  under to paragraph 8.3(1)(b) of the [Narcotic Control
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1041), as they read
  immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into force,
  by a holder of a licence referred to in item 5, column 1, of the table
  to section 355 is deemed to be an alternate qualified person in charge
  under subsection 150(2).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration document

**[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** For a six-month period
that begins on the day on which these Regulations come into force,
paragraph 282(2)(a) does not apply to a holder of a licence for sale for
medical purposes, as set out in item 3, column 3, of the table to
section 354, that registers an applicant as a client, if the holder
complies with section 87 and paragraph 133(2)(a) of the former [Access
to Cannabis for Medical Purposes
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions --- [Controlled Drugs and
Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8)

**[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** Despite subsection
156(1) of the Act, an exemption referred to in that subsection that
expires before December 31, 2018 continues in force until December 31,
2018, unless it is revoked before that date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention --- [Controlled Drugs and
Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8)

**[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** A person referred to in
section 157 of the Act must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any record, report, electronic data or
  other document referred to in that section is retained until the end
  of the applicable retention period imposed under the [Controlled Drugs
  and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is or was the holder of a licence that
  was issued under the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
  Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), notify the Minister, in writing, of any change
  to the address of the location at which the document is retained.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time for compliance

**[[365]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-365 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 241(1), the information that the holder of a licence that is
deemed by section 354 or 355 to be a licence for cultivation, processing
or sale would otherwise be required to include in the record referred to
in that subsection in the first 90 days after the day on which these
Regulations come into force is not required to be included until the
91st day after that day.

### [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-851178 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[]{.HistoricalNote}

S.C. 2018, c. 16

**[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_IndDAE6_hq_22544){#fn_IndDAE6_hq_22544-ID0EBFA}[[375]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375
.sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4),
these Regulations come into force on the day on which subsection 204(1)
of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) comes into force, but if they
are registered after that day, they come into force on the day on which
they are registered.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bill C-74

[(2)]{.lawlabel} If Bill C-74, introduced in the 1st session of the 42nd
Parliament and entitled [Budget Implementation Act, 2018, No.
1](/eng/acts/B-9.8592), has not received royal assent on the day on
which these Regulations are in force, then paragraphs 29(b), 30(c),
31(d), 155(c), 156(b) and 157(d) of these Regulations come into force on
the day on which Bill C-74 receives royal assent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Six months after registration

[(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 102 comes into force on the day that, in the
sixth month after the month in which these Regulations are registered,
has the same calendar number as the day on which they are registered or,
if that sixth month has no day with that number, the last day of that
sixth month.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[]{.HistoricalNote}

S.C. 2012, c. 24

[(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 374 comes into force on the day on which
section 90 of the [Safe Food for Canadians Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1) comes
into force.

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2019-206, s. 72

  - ###### Words and expressions {#words-and-expressions .MarginalNote}

    [72]{.lawlabel} Words and expressions used in sections 73 to 81 have
    the same meaning as in the [Cannabis
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144).

- --- SOR/2019-206, s. 74

  - ###### Exemption --- section 79 {#exemption-section-79 .MarginalNote}

    [74]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence that, before the day on which
    this section comes into force, initiated or completed any activity
    in relation to the production, packaging, labelling, storing,
    sampling or testing of dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis
    plants or cannabis plant seeds in accordance with Part 5 of the
    [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) as they read
    immediately before the day on which this section comes into force is
    exempt from the application of section 79 of the [Cannabis
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) if

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder was, on the day before the day
      on which this section comes into force, authorized to conduct the
      activities; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} from the day on which this section comes into
      force, the licence holder conducts all remaining activities in
      relation to the production, packaging, labelling, storing,
      sampling or testing of such cannabis in accordance with Part 5 of
      the [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144).

- --- SOR/2019-206, s. 76(3)

  - - ###### Exemption --- other person {#exemption-other-person .MarginalNote}

      [76]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person, other than a holder of
      a licence for cultivation or a licence for processing, that is
      authorized to sell dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis plants
      or cannabis plant seeds that are cannabis products --- or that are
      contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product ---
      is, in respect of such a sale, exempt from the application of
      section 25 of the Act if the cannabis product is packaged and
      labelled in accordance with sections 72 to 81, as applicable, and
      the [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144), other
      than paragraph 122.4(1)(c).

- --- SOR/2019-206, s. 79(3)

  - - ###### Exemption --- other person {#exemption-other-person-1 .MarginalNote}

      [79]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person, other than a holder of
      a licence for cultivation or a licence for processing, that is
      authorized to sell dried cannabis or fresh cannabis that are
      cannabis products --- or that are contained in a cannabis
      accessory that is a cannabis product --- is, in respect of such a
      sale, exempt from the application of section 25 of the Act if the
      cannabis product is packaged and labelled in accordance with

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sections 124 and 125 of the [Cannabis
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144), as applicable and
        as they read immediately before the day on which this section
        comes into force;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sections 72 to 81, as applicable; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Cannabis
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144), other than sections
        124, 124.1 and 125.

- --- SOR/2019-206, s. 80(3)

  - - ###### Exemption --- other person {#exemption-other-person-2 .MarginalNote}

      [80]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person, other than a holder of
      a licence for cultivation or a licence for processing, that is
      authorized to sell dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis plants
      or cannabis plant seeds that are cannabis products --- or that are
      contained in a cannabis accessory that is a cannabis product ---
      is, in respect of such a sale, exempt from the application of
      section 25 of the Act if the cannabis product is packaged and
      labelled in accordance with sections 72 to 81, as applicable, and
      the [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144), other
      than subparagraph 130(9)(d)(ii).

- --- SOR/2019-206, s. 81

  - ###### Activities prior to coming into force {#activities-prior-to-coming-into-force .MarginalNote}

    - [81]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a holder of
      a licence to sell, distribute or export a cannabis extract, a
      cannabis topical or edible cannabis --- or a cannabis accessory
      that contains any of these --- that was produced, packaged,
      labelled, stored, sampled or tested before the day on which this
      section comes into force, unless at the time it was produced,
      packaged, labelled, stored, sampled or tested

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicable requirements set out in Parts 5,
        6 and 11 of the [Cannabis
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) were met; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirements set out in subsections 19(1)
        and (1.1) of the [Cannabis
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) were met.

    - ###### Application {#application .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to the cannabis extract,
      cannabis topical or edible cannabis --- or the cannabis accessory
      that contains any of these --- whether it is a cannabis product or
      not.

- --- SOR/2022-251, s. 26

  - ###### Words and expressions {#words-and-expressions-1 .MarginalNote}

    [26]{.lawlabel} Words and expressions used in sections 27 and 28
    have the same meaning as in the [Cannabis
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144).

- --- SOR/2022-251, s. 27

  - ###### Exemption --- sale of cannabis beverage {#exemption-sale-of-cannabis-beverage .MarginalNote}

    - [27]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for
      processing that is authorized to sell cannabis is exempt from the
      application of section 25 of the Act in respect of the sale of a
      cannabis beverage that is a cannabis product if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis beverage has been packaged or
        labelled in accordance with the [Cannabis
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144), other than
        paragraph 123(1)(g) of those Regulations; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the label that is applied to any container in
        which the cannabis beverage is packaged contains the statement
        set out in paragraph 123(1)(g) of those Regulations but the
        equivalent quantity of dried cannabis, in grams, that is
        indicated on the label is, despite that paragraph, determined on
        the basis that 70 g of cannabis beverage is deemed to be
        equivalent to 1 g of dried cannabis.

    - ###### Exemption --- distribution of cannabis beverage {#exemption-distribution-of-cannabis-beverage .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for processing that is
      authorized to distribute cannabis is exempt from the application
      of subsection 106(1) of the [Cannabis
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) in respect of the
      distribution of a cannabis beverage that is a cannabis product if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicable requirements set out in sections
        108 to 136 of those Regulations, other than paragraph 123(1)(g),
        have been met in respect of the cannabis beverage; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the label that is applied to any container in
        which the cannabis beverage is packaged contains the statement
        set out in paragraph 123(1)(g) of those Regulations but the
        equivalent quantity of dried cannabis, in grams, that is
        indicated on the label is, despite that paragraph, determined on
        the basis that 70 g of cannabis beverage is deemed to be
        equivalent to 1 g of dried cannabis.

    - ###### Cessation of effect {#cessation-of-effect .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) cease to have effect on
      the day that, in the 12th month after the month in which these
      Regulations come into force, has the same calendar number as the
      day on which they come into force or, if that 12th month has no
      day with that number, the last day of that 12th month.

- --- SOR/2022-251, s. 28

  - ###### Prohibition --- research with human participants {#prohibition-research-with-human-participants .MarginalNote}

    - [28]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for
      research that is valid immediately before the day on which these
      Regulations come into force must not, despite subsection 28(4) of
      the [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) and any
      term or condition of their licence, conduct research with human
      participants after the second anniversary of the day on which
      these Regulations come into force unless, on or after the day on
      which these Regulations come into force, the licence holder is
      authorized to do so in one of the following circumstances:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence for research that
        contains a condition that all research conducted under the
        licence is reviewed and endorsed using the licence holder's
        Research Project Administration Framework, the Minister amends
        the holder's licence to authorize activities in relation to any
        non-therapeutic research on cannabis;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the Minister issues the
        holder a new licence for research authorizing activities in
        relation to non-therapeutic research on cannabis.

    - ###### Definition of [[research with human participants]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} {#definition-of-research-with-human-participants .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), [[research
      with human participants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means research that
      involves the distribution of cannabis to human participants and
      that is not a [[clinical trial]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined
      in section C.05.001 of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for which the sale
      of cannabis is authorized in accordance with section C.05.006 of
      those Regulations.

- --- SOR/2025-43, s. 76

  - ###### Words and expressions {#words-and-expressions-2 .MarginalNote}

    - [76]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Words and expressions used in
      this section have the same meaning as in the [Cannabis
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144).

    - ###### Exemption --- quantity or concentration of THC and CBD {#exemption-quantity-or-concentration-of-thc-and-cbd .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requirements set out in paragraph 130(8)(a)
      of the [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) do
      not apply in relation to the quantity or concentration of THC and
      CBD that is included on the label of a cannabis product in
      accordance with paragraphs 124(1)(d) and (f), 124.1(d) and (f),
      125(b) and (d), 132.1(1)(d) and (f), 132.11(d) and (f),
      132.12(1)(b) and (d), 132.15(d) and (f), 132.16(b) and (d),
      132.18(1)(c), (e), (g) and (i) and 132.19(1)(b) and (d), as the
      case may be, and paragraph 130(3)(e) of the [Cannabis
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) as they read before
      the coming into force of these Regulations.

    - ###### Exemption --- licence holder {#exemption-licence-holder .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a licence that is authorized to sell
      or distribute dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis extracts,
      cannabis topicals or edible cannabis that are cannabis products
      --- or that are contained in an accessory that is a cannabis
      product --- is, in respect of such a sale or distribution, exempt
      from the application of subsection 106(1) of the [Cannabis
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) and from section 25 of
      the Act if the cannabis products are packaged and labelled in
      accordance with subsection (2) and the [Cannabis
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) as they read before
      the coming into force of these Regulations.

    - ###### Exemption --- other person {#exemption-other-person-3 .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person, other than a holder of a licence, that
      is authorized to sell dried cannabis, fresh cannabis, cannabis
      extracts, cannabis topicals or edible cannabis that are cannabis
      products --- or that are contained in an accessory that is a
      cannabis product --- is, in respect of such a sale, exempt from
      the application of section 25 of the Act if the cannabis products
      are packaged and labelled in accordance with subsection (2) and
      the [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144) as they
      read before the coming into force of these Regulations.

    - ###### Cessation of effect {#cessation-of-effect-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) ceases to have effect on the day
      that, in the 12th month after the month in which the present
      section comes into force, has the same calendar number as the day
      on which it comes into force or, if that 12th month has no day
      with that number, the last day of that 12th month.
:::
::::
